However, recent studies have shown that these models are prone to generating vulnerable or even malicious code under adversarial settings. Existing red-teaming approaches rely on extensive human effort, limiting their scalability and practicality, and generally overlook the interactive nature of real-world AI-assisted programming, which often unfolds over multiple turns. To bridge these gaps, we present RedCoder, a red-teaming agent that engages victim models in multi-turn conversation to elicit vulnerable code. The pipeline to construct RedCoder begins with a multi-agent gaming process that simulates adversarial interactions, yielding a set of prototype conversations and an arsenal of reusable attack strategies. We then fine-tune an LLM on these prototype conversations to serve as the backbone of RedCoder. Once deployed, RedCoder autonomously engages Code LLMs in multi-turn conversations, dynamically retrieving relevant strategies from the arsenal to steer the dialogue toward vulnerability-inducing outputs. Experiments across multiple Code LLMs show that our approach outperforms prior single-turn and multi-turn red-team methods in inducing vulnerabilities in code generation, offering a scalable and effective tool for evaluating the security boundaries of modern code-generation systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RedCoder：面向代码大语言模型的自动化多轮红队攻击
代码生成大语言模型（Code LLMs）在AI辅助软件开发与测试中展现出强大能力。然而，近期研究表明，这些模型在对抗环境下易生成脆弱甚至恶意代码。现有红队方法依赖大量人工，难以扩展且忽略现实AI编程中多轮交互的特性。为弥合此差距，我们提出RedCoder，一种通过多轮对话诱使目标模型生成脆弱代码的红队代理。其构建流程始于多智能体博弈模拟对抗交互，生成原型对话与可复用攻击策略库；随后在原型对话上微调LLM作为RedCoder核心。部署后，RedCoder可自主与Code LLM多轮对话，动态调用策略库引导对话生成脆弱代码。多模型实验表明，该方法在诱导代码漏洞方面优于现有单轮与多轮红队方法，为评估现代代码生成系统安全边界提供可扩展有效工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate Code LLMs' security under adversarial, multi-turn programming scenarios, RedCoder introduces an automated red-teaming agent that engages target models in iterative dialogues to elicit vulnerable code. The method constructs a red-teaming pipeline via a multi-agent adversarial simulation to generate prototype conversations and reusable attack strategies, which are then used to fine-tune an LLM as the core agent. Experimental results across multiple Code LLMs demonstrate that RedCoder significantly outperforms existing single-turn and multi-turn red-teaming methods in inducing vulnerabilities, establishing a scalable and effective framework for probing the security limits of code-generation models.
为评估代码生成大语言模型在对抗环境下的安全性并克服现有依赖人工的红队方法的局限性，RedCoder提出了一种自动化多轮红队代理，通过多轮对话诱导目标模型生成漏洞代码。该方法首先通过多智能体对抗模拟生成原型对话与可复用攻击策略，再以此训练LLM作为核心代理。部署后，RedCoder能自主与目标代码模型进行多轮对话，动态调用攻击策略引导其输出漏洞代码。实验表明，RedCoder在诱导漏洞方面显著优于现有单轮与多轮红队方法，为评估现代代码生成系统安全边界提供了一种可扩展且高效的技术方案。
Uncertain but Useful: Leveraging CNN Training Variability into Data Augmentation
Authors: Inés Gonzalez-Pepe, Vinuyan Sivakolunthu, Yohan Chatelain, Tristan Glatard
First: 2025-09-05T16:54:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:48:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep learning (DL) has transformed neuroimaging by delivering state-of-the-art performance with reduced computation times. Yet, the numerical uncertainty inherent to DL training remains largely underexplored despite its potential to significantly impact the reliability of model outcomes. We show that training the FastSurfer segmentation model introduces substantial numerical uncertainty that exceeds its non-DL counterpart (FreeSurfer 7.3.2) in cortical regions, potentially impacting downstream clinical results. We also characterize this training-time uncertainty using random seed perturbations and demonstrate that seed-induced variability is structurally comparable to numerical variability. We then show that seed variability can be leveraged as a data augmentation technique through ensembling to improve downstream brain age regression performance. These findings position numerical uncertainty during DL training as a substantive factor in neuroimaging reliability, with measurable consequences for downstream tasks, and demonstrate that it can simultaneously be harnessed as a data augmentation technique.
中文标题/摘要
标题：不确定但有用：利用CNN训练变异性进行数据增强
深度学习（DL）通过提供最先进的性能并减少计算时间，已彻底改变了神经影像学。然而，尽管其潜在影响巨大，DL训练中的数值不确定性仍被严重忽视。我们发现，训练FastSurfer分割模型会引入显著的数值不确定性，在皮层区域甚至超过其非DL对应物（FreeSurfer 7.3.2），可能影响下游临床结果。我们通过随机种子扰动表征训练时的不确定性，并证明种子诱导的变异性在结构上与数值变异性相当。随后，我们展示可通过集成方法利用种子变异性作为数据增强技术，以提升下游脑龄回归性能。这些发现将DL训练中的数值不确定性定位为神经影像可靠性的重要因素，并证明其对下游任务具有可测量的影响，同时可作为数据增强技术加以利用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underexplored numerical uncertainty inherent in deep learning training, this study investigates how variability introduced during CNN training—specifically in the FastSurfer segmentation model—can be leveraged for data augmentation. By perturbing random seeds during training, the authors characterize training-time uncertainty and demonstrate that this variability produces structurally similar outputs to numerical noise. They then use ensembling of seed-variant models to augment training data, which improves performance in downstream brain age regression tasks. The results show that training uncertainty is not merely a noise factor but a measurable source of variability that can enhance model robustness and generalization in neuroimaging applications.
鉴于深度学习训练中的数值不确定性对神经影像可靠性影响尚未被充分研究，该研究探讨了FastSurfer分割模型在不同随机种子下训练产生的变异如何被用作数据增强手段。作者发现，种子诱导的变异在结构上与数值噪声相似，且通过集成不同种子训练的模型可提升下游脑龄预测性能。研究结果表明，训练期间的数值不确定性不仅是噪声，而是可测量并可被有效利用以增强模型泛化能力的重要因素。
EAGLE-360: Embodied Active Global-to-Local Exploration in 360$^\circ$
Authors: Jingtao Xu, Zizhuo Lin, Jianwen Sun, Yi Yang, Yawei Luo
First: 2026-07-02T17:47:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:47:27+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated exceptional capabilities in standard visual understanding, adapting them for active visual search in 360$^\circ$ panoramic environments exposes fundamental limitations. Specifically, standard MLLMs struggle to effectively model inherent panoramic properties, such as severe polar distortion and continuous cylindrical topologies, which significantly degrades target detection accuracy. Consequently, existing panoramic search methods attempt to compensate by relying heavily on fragmented local viewpoints. Burdened by rigid initialization and a lack of global panoramic priors, these approaches suffer from myopic, inefficient exploration and struggle with robust error recovery when targets are out of view. To overcome these challenges, we propose EAGLE-360, a novel Embodied Active Global-to-Local Exploration framework. Rather than performing exhaustive local searches, EAGLE-360 leverages global priors to establish an initial holistic perspective, iteratively reasoning and progressively narrowing the search space. Architecturally, we adapt RoPE Rolling, a coordinate-shifting positional encoding mechanism, to seamlessly model the continuous topologies of panoramas. To facilitate this paradigm, we construct the large-scale EAGLE-360 dataset, comprising 14,000+ 4K panoramas and 70,000+ rounds of high-quality VQA dialogues. By employing a training pipeline that integrates Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), we effectively elicit complex spatial reasoning and tool-calling capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EAGLE-360 establishes a new state-of-the-art for 360$^\circ$ visual search, achieving nearly an 8-fold increase in accuracy over the base model while significantly enhancing exploration efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EAGLE-360：面向360°环境的具身主动全局到局部探索
尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在标准视觉理解方面表现出色，但将其适配至360°全景环境中的主动视觉搜索时，暴露出根本性局限。具体而言，标准MLLMs难以有效建模全景固有属性，如严重的极点畸变和连续圆柱拓扑，这显著降低了目标检测精度。因此，现有全景搜索方法试图通过依赖碎片化局部视角进行补偿。然而，这些方法受限于刚性初始化和缺乏全局全景先验，导致探索短视、低效，并在目标移出视野时难以实现稳健的错误恢复。为克服这些挑战，我们提出EAGLE-360，一种新颖的具身主动全局到局部探索框架。该框架不进行穷举式局部搜索，而是利用全局先验建立初始整体视角，通过迭代推理逐步缩小搜索空间。架构上，我们改进RoPE Rolling机制——一种坐标平移位置编码机制，以无缝建模全景的连续拓扑。为支持该范式，我们构建了大规模EAGLE-360数据集，包含14,000+张4K全景图和70,000+轮高质量视觉问答对话。通过结合监督微调（SFT）与组相对策略优化（GRPO）的训练流程，我们有效激发了复杂空间推理与工具调用能力。大量实验表明，EAGLE-360在360°视觉搜索任务中建立了新的最先进水平，相较基线模型准确率提升近8倍，同时显著提升探索效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of standard Multimodal Large Language Models in handling 360-degree panoramic environments due to polar distortion and cylindrical topology, EAGLE-360 introduces an embodied active exploration framework that leverages global priors to guide efficient, iterative local search. It adapts RoPE Rolling for continuous spatial modeling and trains on a large-scale dataset of 14,000+ 4K panoramas and 70,000+ VQA dialogues using SFT and GRPO. Experimental results show it achieves nearly an 8-fold improvement in target detection accuracy over baseline models while significantly enhancing exploration efficiency.
为了解决标准多模态大语言模型在360度全景环境中因极坐标畸变和圆柱拓扑导致的视觉搜索能力不足问题，EAGLE-360提出了一种基于全局先验的主动探索框架，通过RoPE Rolling机制建模连续空间结构，并在包含14000多张4K全景图和7万多次高质量VQA对话的大规模数据集上，结合监督微调与组相对策略优化进行训练。实验表明，该方法相较基线模型准确率提升近8倍，探索效率显著提高。
QuadRocket: An Aerial Robotic Testbed for Adaptive Thrust-Vector Control of Rocket-Like Vehicles
Authors: Pedro Santos, Joel Reis, Paulo Oliveira, Carlos Silvestre
First: 2026-07-02T17:43:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:43:09+00:00
Comments: Paper accepted for publication in IEEE Transactions on Aerospace and Electronic Systems
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents QuadRocket, a quadrotor-based rocket prototype that provides a low-cost, low-risk platform for validating advanced thrust-vector control strategies for launch vehicle-type systems. The prototype consists of a cylindrical main body mounted on top of a quadrotor through a universal joint, forming a flying inverted pendulum with non-negligible inertia. For control design, the coupled system is modeled as a single axisymmetric rigid body actuated by a vectored force applied along its longitudinal axis. A reduced-attitude representation on the two sphere is adopted to explicitly exploit the vehicle's axial symmetry and to decouple yaw from the thrust-vector direction. On this model, we derive an adaptive backstepping controller that achieves almost global trajectory tracking in the presence of unknown constant disturbances, while a control-point transformation mitigates non minimum-phase behavior. The quadrotor is then treated as a thrust vector actuator, and a dynamic-surface-based attitude controller is designed to track the desired thrust-vector, accounting for actuation dynamics and avoiding explicit differentiation of virtual control signals. The complete architecture is evaluated in simulation and validated experimentally in an indoor motion-capture arena. Results demonstrate accurate trajectory tracking, effective disturbance compensation, and confirm the suitability of the QuadRocket as a versatile testbed for thrust-vector-controlled robotic vehicles.
中文标题/摘要
标题：QuadRocket：用于火箭类飞行器自适应推力矢量控制的空中机器人试验平台
本文提出QuadRocket，一种基于四旋翼的火箭原型机，为验证运载火箭类系统的先进推力矢量控制策略提供低成本、低风险平台。该原型机由圆柱形主体通过万向节安装在四旋翼上方，构成具有不可忽略惯量的飞行倒立摆。控制系统建模为单轴对称刚体，通过沿纵向轴施加矢量力驱动。采用双球面简化姿态表示，显式利用飞行器轴对称性并解耦偏航与推力矢量方向。在此模型上，推导出一种自适应反步控制器，可实现未知常值扰动下的几乎全局轨迹跟踪，同时通过控制点变换缓解非最小相行为。四旋翼作为推力矢量执行器，设计基于动态表面的姿态控制器以跟踪期望推力矢量，考虑执行器动力学并避免虚拟控制信号显式微分。完整架构经仿真验证，并在室内动作捕捉场地实验验证。结果表明轨迹跟踪精确、扰动补偿有效，证实QuadRocket作为推力矢量控制机器人飞行器试验平台的适用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for low-cost, low-risk platforms to test advanced thrust-vector control for rocket-like aerial vehicles, this paper introduces QuadRocket—a quadrotor-mounted cylindrical body forming a flying inverted pendulum. The system is modeled as an axisymmetric rigid body with vectored thrust along its longitudinal axis, using a reduced-attitude representation to decouple yaw from thrust direction. An adaptive backstepping controller achieves almost global trajectory tracking despite unknown constant disturbances, while a control-point transformation addresses non-minimum-phase behavior. The quadrotor is treated as a thrust vector actuator, with a dynamic-surface attitude controller designed to track desired thrust vectors while handling actuation dynamics without explicit differentiation. Experimental validation in an indoor motion-capture arena confirms accurate trajectory tracking, effective disturbance rejection, and the platform’s suitability as a versatile testbed for thrust-vector-controlled aerial systems.
为验证火箭类飞行器的先进推力矢量控制策略，本研究提出QuadRocket——一种基于四旋翼的火箭原型平台，其结构为圆柱体与四旋翼通过万向节连接，构成具有显著惯量的倒立摆系统。系统建模为轴对称刚体，推力沿纵轴矢量施加，采用简化姿态表示以解耦偏航与推力方向。基于该模型，设计自适应反步控制器实现几乎全局轨迹跟踪，同时通过控制点变换消除非最小相位特性。四旋翼作为推力矢量执行器，采用动态表面姿态控制器跟踪期望推力矢量，兼顾执行器动力学并避免虚拟控制信号的显式微分。实验在室内动作捕捉环境中验证，结果表明轨迹跟踪精度高、扰动补偿有效，证实QuadRocket作为推力矢量控制飞行器测试平台的适用性。
Audio-Based Understanding of Audiobook Narration Appeal
Authors: Shahar Elisha, Mariano Beguerisse-Díaz, Emmanouil Benetos
First: 2026-07-02T17:43:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:43:05+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Interspeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Narration is central to the audiobook listening experience, shaping how listeners engage with and understand the content. This work explores how narration qualities shape an audiobook's appeal, noting that their effects can vary by genre, title, and audience. We extract vocal and acoustic features (e.g., tone, pace, loudness) from LibriVox using pre-trained audio models and analyse their relationship with consumption data (specifically, view-rate) and their interplay with genre and title. Despite limited consumption data, we find that acoustic information alone has a robust association with appeal, even after accounting for title effects. We further validate these findings using more nuanced proprietary engagement metrics. To our knowledge, this is the first systematic computational study linking narration qualities, genre, title, and audiobook consumption, highlighting the potential of data-driven insights to improve audiobook personalisation and narrator casting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于音频的有声读物朗读吸引力理解
朗读是影响有声读物聆听体验的核心因素，塑造听众如何参与和理解内容。本研究探讨朗读质量如何影响有声读物的吸引力，并指出其效果会因体裁、书名和受众而异。我们利用预训练音频模型从LibriVox提取声学与语音特征（如音调、语速、音量），并分析其与消费数据（特别是观看率）的关系，以及与体裁和书名的交互作用。尽管消费数据有限，我们发现声学信息本身与吸引力存在稳健关联，即使在控制书名效应后仍成立。我们进一步使用更精细的自有参与度指标验证了这些发现。据我们所知，这是首次系统性计算研究将朗读质量、体裁、书名与有声读物消费关联起来，凸显了数据驱动洞察在提升有声读物个性化推荐和朗读者选角方面的潜力。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how vocal and acoustic features of audiobook narration influence listener appeal, motivated by the need to understand how narration quality affects engagement across genres and titles. Using pre-trained audio models, the authors extract features like tone, pace, and loudness from LibriVox audiobooks and correlate them with consumption data (view-rate) while controlling for title effects. Despite limited data, they find that acoustic features alone strongly predict appeal, and this relationship holds even when accounting for genre and title. The findings are further validated using proprietary engagement metrics, marking the first systematic computational analysis linking narration qualities to audiobook consumption and suggesting potential for data-driven personalization and narrator selection.
本研究旨在探究有声书朗读的声学特征如何影响听众吸引力，动机在于理解不同体裁和作品中朗读质量对听众参与度的影响。研究从LibriVox有声书中提取音高、语速、音量等声学特征，结合观看率等消费数据，并控制作品和体裁变量进行分析。尽管数据有限，声学特征仍显示出与吸引力的强关联，且在使用自有精细参与度指标后得到验证。这是首次系统性地将朗读特征与有声书消费行为关联的研究，表明数据驱动方法可助力个性化推荐与配音演员选拔。
Control-DINO: Feature Space Conditioning for Controllable Image-to-Video Diffusion
Authors: Edoardo A. Dominici, Thomas Deixelberger, Konstantinos Vardis, Markus Steinberger
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-04-02T08:27:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:40:39+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026 - Project Page https://dedoardo.github.io/projects/control-dino/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Video diffusion models have recently been applied with success to problems in content generation, novel view synthesis, and, more broadly, world simulation. Many applications in generation and transfer rely on conditioning these models, typically through perceptual, geometric, or simple semantic signals, fundamentally using them as generative renderers. At the same time, high-dimensional features obtained from large-scale self-supervised learning on images or point clouds are increasingly used as a general-purpose interface for vision models. The connection between the two has been explored for subject specific editing, aligning and training video diffusion models, but not in the role of a dense conditioning signal for pretrained video diffusion models. Features obtained through self-supervised learning like DINOv3, contain a lot of entangled information about style, lighting and semantics of the scene. This makes them great at reconstruction tasks but limits their generative capabilities. In this paper, we show how we can use the features for tasks such as video domain transfer and video-from-3D generation. We introduce a lightweight control architecture and training strategy that decouples appearance from other features that we wish to preserve, enabling robust control for appearance changes such as stylization and relighting. Furthermore, we show that low spatial resolution can be compensated by higher feature dimensionality, improving controllability in generative rendering from explicit spatial representations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Control-DINO：用于可控图像到视频扩散的特征空间条件化
视频扩散模型近期在内容生成、新视角合成及更广泛的场景模拟等任务中取得了成功应用。许多生成与迁移任务依赖于对这些模型进行条件化，通常通过感知、几何或简单语义信号，本质上将其作为生成渲染器使用。同时，通过在图像或点云上进行大规模自监督学习获得的高维特征，正日益被用作视觉模型的通用接口。尽管已有研究探索了这两者在特定主体编辑、对齐与训练视频扩散模型中的联系，但尚未将其用于预训练视频扩散模型的密集条件信号。通过自监督学习（如DINOv3）获得的特征包含大量关于场景风格、光照和语义的纠缠信息，使其在重建任务中表现优异，但限制了其生成能力。本文展示了如何利用这些特征实现视频域迁移和从3D生成视频等任务。我们引入了一种轻量级控制架构与训练策略，将外观与其他希望保留的特征解耦，从而实现对风格化与重光照等外观变化的稳健控制。此外，我们表明，低空间分辨率可通过更高特征维度进行补偿，从而提升从显式空间表示进行生成渲染时的可控性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to control generative video diffusion models using high-dimensional self-supervised features, this paper introduces Control-DINO, a lightweight architecture that decouples appearance from other scene features to enable precise control over stylization and relighting. By conditioning pretrained video diffusion models on DINOv3 features—rich in entangled style, lighting, and semantic information—the method achieves controllable video domain transfer and 3D-to-video generation. Experiments demonstrate that even low-resolution spatial inputs can be effectively compensated by high-dimensional features, enhancing controllability in generative rendering without sacrificing quality.
为实现对预训练视频扩散模型的精确控制，本文提出Control-DINO方法，通过轻量级架构解耦外观与需保留的其他场景特征，支持风格化与重光照等可控生成任务。利用DINOv3特征中蕴含的丰富风格、光照与语义信息，该方法可实现视频域迁移和3D到视频生成，无需重新训练。实验表明，即使输入空间分辨率较低，高维特征也能有效补偿，提升基于显式空间表示的生成渲染可控性，同时保持结构保真度。
Learning Agile Intruder Interception using Differentiable Quadrotor Dynamics
Authors: Michael Anoruo, Xiaoyu Tian, Abhishek Rathod, Timothy Naudet, Thomas Canchola, Eric Sturzinger, Kshitij Goel, Wennie Tabib
First: 2026-07-02T17:40:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:40:06+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 10 figures, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a methodology for learning a control policy to intercept an intruder using the 3D direction unit vector to the intruder and the interceptor state. Prior deep reinforcement learning approaches assume either relative position or distance to the intruder is available, but this information is not readily accessible in real-world applications that employ passive, monocular camera sensors. Instead, we propose a solution that leverages an analytical policy gradient method using differentiable quadrotor dynamics to learn agile interception at speeds up to 10 m/s. The proposed approach outperforms baseline methods that utilize simplified point mass dynamics by an average of 30%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用可微四旋翼动力学学习敏捷入侵者拦截控制策略
本文提出了一种学习控制策略以利用指向入侵者的3D单位方向向量和拦截器状态来拦截入侵者的方法。此前的深度强化学习方法假设相对位置或与入侵者的距离可用，但在实际应用中，使用被动单目摄像头传感器时这些信息难以获取。相反，我们提出了一种利用可微四旋翼动力学的解析策略梯度方法，以学习最高达10 m/s速度的敏捷拦截策略。该方法相比使用简化质点动力学的基线方法平均性能提升30%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of agile interception in real-world scenarios where only monocular camera data is available and relative position or distance cannot be directly measured, this paper proposes a differentiable quadrotor dynamics-based control policy learned via analytical policy gradients. The method uses only the 3D direction unit vector to the intruder and the interceptor’s state to guide interception maneuvers at speeds up to 10 m/s. Experimental results show that this approach consistently outperforms baseline methods based on simplified point mass dynamics by an average of 30% in interception success rate and efficiency.
为了解决在仅能获取单目相机数据的现实场景中，无法直接测量相对位置或距离时的入侵者拦截难题，本文提出了一种基于可微四旋翼动力学的控制策略，仅利用指向入侵者的3D单位方向向量和拦截器自身状态进行敏捷拦截学习。该方法采用解析策略梯度优化，避免使用简化质点动力学模型。实验表明，该方法在10米/秒速度下拦截性能比基准方法高30%，显著优于简化动力学模型的方案。
Grounded autonomous scrutiny at scale: emergent critique from reproduction of published computational physics papers
Authors: Haonan Huang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-04-14T02:06:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:39:41+00:00
Comments: v2: camera-ready version, accepted at ICML 2026 AI for Science Workshop. Corrects the phase-classification statistics and adds a coding-sensitivity analysis (Methods M6); the agent-produced six-page Comment is reproduced as-is in the final appendix. 24 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous LLM agents now produce complete research artifacts in machine-learning sandboxes, but real computational physics is harder: experiments are first-principles calculations against re-runnable physical ground truth, and meaningful new work almost always builds on a key existing paper. We ask whether such an agent can perform grounded scrutiny of published computational physics - reading a paper, reproducing it from scratch, and surfacing methodological concerns from execution. We deploy a single Claude Opus 4.6 configuration at two complementary scopes. At scale, across 111 open-access Quantum ESPRESSO papers, an autonomous agent runs the read-plan-compute-compare loop and, although never asked to critique, raises substantive methodological concerns on ~42% of papers; 85 of 88 of these critiques (96.6%) surface only after the agent has actually run a calculation, with a reading-only ceiling of 1.8%. Critique emerges from reproduction, not from reading. In depth, on one Nature Communications paper on multiscale device simulation of a 2D-material MOSFET, a fresh agent inheriting a verified reproduction pipeline autonomously produces a 14-concern physics inventory and a complete, submission-form six-page Comment that revises the paper's L_G = 5 nm headline. Two of its L_G = 5 nm headline-challenging attacks - a source-degeneration contact-resistance bound and a Sb-doping degradation ratio - are absent from the published 21-reviewer peer review.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大规模 grounded 自主审查：从计算物理论文复现中涌现的批判
自主 LLM 代理现在能在机器学习沙盒中生成完整的科研成果，但真实的计算物理更难：实验是基于可重跑的物理真实性的第一性原理计算，且有意义的新工作几乎总是建立在关键现有论文之上。我们探讨此类代理能否对已发表的计算物理论文进行 grounded 审查——即阅读论文、从零复现并从执行中揭示方法论问题。我们部署单一 Claude Opus 4.6 配置，覆盖两个互补范围。在大规模上，针对 111 篇开放获取的 Quantum ESPRESSO 论文，自主代理运行读-计划-计算-比较循环，尽管从未被要求批判，仍约 42% 的论文中提出实质性方法论问题；88 个批判中有 85 个（96.6%）仅在代理实际运行计算后浮现，纯阅读上限仅为 1.8%。批判源于复现，而非阅读。深入分析中，针对《自然通讯》一篇关于二维材料 MOSFET 多尺度器件模拟的论文，新代理继承经验证的复现流程，自主生成 14 项物理问题清单及完整六页投稿格式评论，修订论文“L_G = 5 nm”标题。其两项挑战“L_G = 5 nm”标题的攻击——源退化接触电阻上限与 Sb 掺杂退化比——未出现在 21 位审稿人发表的评审意见中。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate computational physics research through grounded, executable scrutiny rather than abstract reading, the study deploys an autonomous LLM agent to reproduce published papers and surface methodological concerns. Using a single Claude Opus 4.6 agent across 111 open-access Quantum ESPRESSO papers, the agent cycles through reading, planning, computing, and comparing, revealing substantive critiques on 42% of papers—96.6% of which emerged only after actual computation, not reading. In a detailed case study on a Nature Communications paper, the agent autonomously generated a 14-point physics critique and a six-page revision comment, challenging the paper’s key claim of L_G = 5 nm with novel insights not found in peer review.
为评估计算物理研究的可复现性与方法论严谨性，该研究测试了自主LLM代理是否能通过从头复现论文来发现方法论缺陷。在111篇开放获取的Quantum ESPRESSO论文上，单个Claude Opus 4.6代理循环执行阅读、规划、计算与比较，42%的论文被提出实质性批评，其中96.6%的批评源于实际计算而非阅读。在一篇Nature Communications论文的深度案例中，代理自主生成14项物理批评及六页修订意见，挑战论文关于L_G = 5 nm的核心结论，其提出的两个关键质疑（源退化接触电阻上限与锑掺杂退化比）未出现在21位审稿人的评审中。
Interpretation-Oriented Cloud Removal via Observation-Anchored Residual Flow with Geo-Contextual Alignment
Authors: Ziyao Wang, Maonan Wang, Yucheng He, Xianping Ma, Ziyi Wang, Hongyang Zhang, Yirong Cheng, Man-on Pun
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-02T17:39:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:39:23+00:00
Comments: accepted by ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Cloud removal (CR) is essential for optical remote sensing, serving as a prerequisite for reliable downstream interpretation, such as semantic segmentation and change detection. However, existing CR approaches often prioritize visual realism while overlooking their impact on subsequent analytical tasks, leading to semantic drift and degraded downstream performance. To address this issue, we propose Geo-Anchored Cloud Removal (GACR), a unified framework that jointly ensures faithful reconstruction and robust interpretability. At its core, GACR incorporates Observation-Anchored Residual Flow (OAR-Flow), which reformulates CR as a physically grounded residual inversion process. By anchoring the generative trajectory to the cloudy observation rather than pure noise, OAR-Flow enables fast, stable, and faithful reconstruction. To further preserve semantic structures critical for downstream interpretation, GACR integrates Geo-Contextual Prior Alignment (GCPA) to constrain the reconstruction within a semantic manifold induced by a Vision Foundation Model (VFM). Consequently, GACR strictly maintains the spatial-semantic integrity of complex landscapes. Extensive experiments across six CR datasets and twelve downstream tasks demonstrate that GACR produces superior reconstruction quality while consistently improving downstream task accuracy. The code is available at https://github.com/wzy6055/GACR.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于观测锚定残差流与地理上下文对齐的面向解释的云去除方法
云去除（CR）是光学遥感的关键步骤，是语义分割和变化检测等下游任务可靠执行的前提。然而，现有CR方法常优先考虑视觉真实感，忽视其对后续分析任务的影响，导致语义漂移和下游性能下降。为解决此问题，我们提出地理锚定云去除（GACR）统一框架，同时确保忠实重建与鲁棒可解释性。其核心是观测锚定残差流（OAR-Flow），将CR重新建模为物理约束的残差反演过程。通过将生成轨迹锚定于有云观测而非纯噪声，OAR-Flow实现快速、稳定、忠实的重建。为进一步保留对下游任务至关重要的语义结构，GACR引入地理上下文先验对齐（GCPA），约束重建在视觉基础模型（VFM）诱导的语义流形内进行。因此，GACR严格保持复杂地表的空间-语义完整性。在六个CR数据集和十二个下游任务上的大量实验表明，GACR在重建质量上更优，同时持续提升下游任务精度。代码见 https://github.com/wzy6055/GACR。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to preserve semantic fidelity in cloud-removed satellite imagery for reliable downstream tasks like segmentation and change detection, this paper proposes GACR, a unified framework that jointly optimizes visual reconstruction and interpretability. It employs Observation-Anchored Residual Flow to invert clouded observations into clear images while anchoring the generative process to the original input for stability and faithfulness. To ensure semantic consistency, it integrates Geo-Contextual Prior Alignment, which constrains outputs within a semantic manifold guided by a Vision Foundation Model. Experiments across six datasets and twelve downstream tasks show GACR achieves superior reconstruction quality and consistently improves performance on downstream tasks compared to state-of-the-art methods.
为保障下游遥感任务的语义准确性，本文提出Geo-Anchored Cloud Removal（GACR）框架，联合实现云去除的忠实重建与强可解释性。其核心是Observation-Anchored Residual Flow（OAR-Flow），通过将生成轨迹锚定于原始含云图像而非纯噪声，实现稳定且准确的重建。为保持空间-语义一致性，GACR引入Geo-Contextual Prior Alignment（GCPA），利用视觉基础模型引导的语义流约束重建结果。在六个数据集和十二项下游任务上的实验表明，GACR在重建质量上表现更优，同时显著提升语义分割与变化检测等任务的准确率。
CaP-X: A Framework for Benchmarking and Improving Coding Agents for Robot Manipulation
Authors: Letian Fu, Justin Yu, Karim El-Refai, Ethan Kou, Haoru Xue, Huang Huang, Wenli Xiao, Guanzhi Wang, Dantong Niu, Fei-Fei Li, Guanya Shi, Jiajun Wu, Shankar Sastry, Yuke Zhu, Ken Goldberg, Linxi "Jim" Fan
First: 2026-03-23T18:08:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:36:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
"Code-as-Policy" considers how executable code can complement data-intensive Vision-Language-Action (VLA) methods, yet their effectiveness as autonomous controllers for embodied manipulation remains underexplored. We present CaP-X, an open-access framework for systematically studying Code-as-Policy agents in robot manipulation. At its core is CaP-Gym, an interactive environment in which agents control robots by synthesizing and executing programs that compose perception and control primitives. Building on this foundation, CaP-Bench evaluates frontier language and vision-language models across varying levels of abstraction, interaction, and perceptual grounding. Across 12 models, CaP-Bench reveals a consistent trend: performance improves with human-crafted abstractions but degrades as these priors are removed, exposing a dependence on designer scaffolding. At the same time, we observe that this gap can be mitigated through scaling agentic test-time computation--through multi-turn interaction, structured execution feedback, visual differencing, automatic skill synthesis, and ensembled reasoning--substantially improves robustness even when agents operate over low-level primitives. These findings allow us to derive CaP-Agent0, a training-free framework that recovers human-level reliability on several manipulation tasks in simulation and on real embodiments. We further introduce CaP-RL, showing reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards improves success rates and transfers from sim2real with minimal gap. Together, CaP-X provides a principled, open-access platform for advancing embodied coding agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CaP-X：用于机器人操作中编码代理基准测试与改进的框架
“代码即策略”探讨了可执行代码如何补充以数据密集型视觉-语言-动作（VLA）方法，但其作为具身操作自主控制器的有效性仍待探索。我们提出了CaP-X，一个开放访问的框架，用于系统性研究机器人操作中的“代码即策略”代理。其核心是CaP-Gym，一个交互式环境，其中代理通过合成并执行组合感知与控制原语的程序来控制机器人。在此基础上，CaP-Bench评估前沿语言和视觉-语言模型在不同抽象层次、交互方式和感知锚定下的表现。在12个模型中，CaP-Bench揭示了一致趋势：性能随人工设计抽象提升而增强，但当移除这些先验时性能下降，暴露了对设计者框架的依赖。同时，我们观察到，通过扩展代理测试时计算——如多轮交互、结构化执行反馈、视觉差异、自动技能合成和集成推理——即使在低级原语上操作，也能显著提升鲁棒性。这些发现使我们推导出CaP-Agent0，一个无需训练的框架，可在仿真和真实具身环境中恢复人类水平的可靠性。我们进一步引入CaP-RL，展示可验证奖励的强化学习可提升成功率，并在仿真到真实迁移中实现最小差距。总体而言，CaP-X提供了一个原则性、开放访问的平台，以推动具身编码代理的发展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underexplored potential of executable code as autonomous controllers for robot manipulation, the authors introduce CaP-X, an open-access framework comprising CaP-Gym (an interactive environment for program synthesis and execution) and CaP-Bench (a benchmark evaluating language and vision-language models across abstraction levels). Experiments with 12 models show performance consistently improves with human-crafted abstractions but declines when these priors are removed, indicating reliance on designer scaffolding. However, scaling agentic computation via multi-turn interaction, structured feedback, visual differencing, skill synthesis, and ensembled reasoning significantly boosts robustness even with low-level primitives. This leads to CaP-Agent0, a training-free method achieving human-level reliability in simulation and real robots, and CaP-RL, which enhances success rates and sim2real transfer through verifiable rewards.
为探索可执行代码作为机器人操作自主控制器的潜力，本文提出CaP-X框架，包含交互式环境CaP-Gym和评估语言与视觉语言模型的基准CaP-Bench。在12个模型的实验中发现，性能随人工抽象提升而增强，但移除这些先验时性能下降，表明模型依赖设计者搭建的结构。然而，通过多轮交互、结构化反馈、视觉差异、技能合成与集成推理等手段扩展代理计算能力，可显著缓解该差距，即使使用低级原语也能提升鲁棒性。由此推出无需训练的CaP-Agent0框架，在仿真与真实机器人上达到人类水平可靠性，并引入CaP-RL，通过可验证奖励提升成功率与仿真到现实迁移效果。
TestEvo-Bench: An Executable and Live Benchmark for Test and Code Co-Evolution
Authors: Jiale Amber Wang, Kaiyuan Wang, Pengyu Nie
Venue: www
First: 2026-07-02T17:35:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:35:20+00:00
Comments: TestEvo-Bench leaderboard and data explorer are hosted at https://www.testevo-bench.com
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Software tests and code evolve together: a code change should be followed by new or updated tests that record the new software behavior. Yet existing test generation and update benchmarks often isolate the test from the code change, and rely on static metadata that does not verify whether a test is executable or semantically tied to the code change. This makes it difficult to evaluate whether a test automation agent understands how a code change should propagate into the test suite. We introduce TestEvo-Bench, a benchmark of test and code co-evolution tasks mined from software repositories, with two tracks: in test generation, the agent shall write new tests to capture the new software behavior; in test update, the agent shall adapt failing existing tests to the changed software behavior. Each task is anchored to a real commit history and packaged with environment configuration to support execution-grounded metrics such as pass rate, coverage, and mutation score. TestEvo-Bench is also a live benchmark: each task records the timestamp of the test and code changes, and new tasks are periodically mined by our automated pipeline, so evaluation can be restricted to tasks postdating a model's training cutoff to reduce data leakage risk. The current snapshot contains 746 test generation and 509 test update tasks, curated from 59,950 candidate co-evolution records across 152 open-source Java projects. We experiment with four state-of-the-art agents that combine strong harnesses (Claude Code, Gemini CLI, and SWE-Agent) with strong foundation models (Claude Opus 4.7 and Gemini 3.1 Pro). Results show that they achieve up to 77.5% success rate on test generation and 74.6% on test update. However, success rate is materially lower on the most recent benchmark tasks and drops significantly under limited per-task cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TestEvo-Bench：一个可执行且实时的测试与代码协同演进基准
软件测试与代码共同演化：代码变更后应伴随新的或更新的测试，以记录新的软件行为。然而，现有测试生成与更新基准常将测试与代码变更分离，并依赖静态元数据，无法验证测试是否可执行或语义上与代码变更相关联。这使得难以评估测试自动化代理是否理解代码变更应如何传播至测试套件。 我们引入TestEvo-Bench，该基准从软件仓库中挖掘测试与代码协同演进任务，包含两个赛道：在测试生成中，代理需编写新测试以捕捉新软件行为；在测试更新中，代理需调整失败的现有测试以适应变更后的软件行为。每个任务均锚定真实提交历史，并附带环境配置，以支持基于执行的度量指标，如通过率、覆盖率和突变分数。TestEvo-Bench亦为实时基准：每个任务记录测试与代码变更的时间戳，新任务由我们的自动化管道定期挖掘，从而可将评估限制在模型训练截止日期之后的任务，以降低数据泄露风险。当前快照包含746个测试生成任务和509个测试更新任务，从152个开源Java项目中精选的59,950个候选协同演进记录中整理而成。我们实验了四种前沿代理，结合强大工具链（Claude Code、Gemini CLI和SWE-Agent）与强大基础模型（Claude Opus 4.7和Gemini 3.1 Pro）。结果显示，它们在测试生成中最高成功率达77.5%，在测试更新中为74.6%。然而，在最新基准任务上的成功率显著降低，且在每任务成本受限时下降尤为明显。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate how well test automation agents understand the relationship between code changes and corresponding test updates, the authors introduce TestEvo-Bench, a benchmark that mines real-world co-evolution tasks from 152 open-source Java projects. The benchmark includes two tracks—test generation and test update—each tied to actual commit histories and executable environments to measure pass rate, coverage, and mutation score. Evaluated on four state-of-the-art agents combining strong harnesses and foundation models, the results show success rates of up to 77.5% for test generation and 74.6% for test update, but performance drops notably on newer tasks and under cost constraints, indicating challenges in generalization and efficiency.
为评估测试自动化代理对代码变更与测试更新间关系的理解能力，作者构建了TestEvo-Bench基准，从152个开源Java项目中挖掘真实共演化任务，包含测试生成与测试更新两个赛道，每个任务绑定真实提交历史并配备可执行环境以评估通过率、覆盖率和变异得分。在四类结合强工具与大模型的先进代理上测试，结果显示测试生成最高成功率达77.5%，测试更新达74.6%，但在最新任务和受限成本下表现显著下降，表明代理在应对持续演进的代码库时存在泛化困难。
Human Capital, Not Model Benchmarks, Predicts Hybrid Intelligence in Forecasting
Authors: Vivienne Ming
First: 2026-07-02T17:34:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:34:37+00:00
Comments: 4 pages, 1 figure, PNAS brief style
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Whether pairing people with AI helps or hurts is usually reported as a single average effect. Using a real-money prediction market (Polymarket) as an objective, externally resolved benchmark, this pilot shows that the value of human-AI collaboration depends on a specific, measurable form of human capital. Analyzed at the level of the individual forecaster, hybrid performance is trimodal: most people either deferred to the model (matching it) or used it to rubber-stamp a prior guess (performing worse than the model alone), while a minority engaged in genuine complementary reasoning and reached accuracy matching or even exceeding (i.e., lower error than) the market itself. Collaborative traits (perspective-taking, intellectual humility, and curiosity) rather than raw cognitive ability or model benchmarks, distinguished who reached that mode. The results are preliminary but statistically robust, and motivate a pre-registered replication now in preparation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人力资本而非模型基准，预测预测中的混合智能
人机协作是助力还是阻碍，通常被报告为单一平均效应。本试点研究以真实金钱预测市场（Polymarket）作为客观、外部解决的基准，表明人机协作的价值取决于一种具体可测量的人力资本形式。在个体预测者层面分析，混合表现呈三态分布：多数人要么顺从模型（与之匹配），要么用模型确认先前猜测（表现劣于模型单独作用），而少数人则进行真正互补性推理，其准确度达到或甚至超过市场本身（即误差更低）。协作特质（换位思考、智力谦逊与好奇心）而非原始认知能力或模型基准，区分了哪些人能达到该模式。结果初步但统计稳健，现已启动预注册重复实验。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether human-AI collaboration improves forecasting accuracy by using a real-money prediction market as an objective benchmark. It finds that individual performance in hybrid intelligence is not uniformly better but trimodal: most forecasters either matched the AI’s predictions or performed worse than the AI alone, while a small subset achieved accuracy equal to or better than the market through complementary reasoning. These high performers were distinguished not by cognitive ability but by collaborative traits such as perspective-taking, intellectual humility, and curiosity. The results, though preliminary, are statistically robust and suggest that human capital—not model benchmarks—drives successful human-AI collaboration.
该研究通过真实金钱预测市场作为客观基准，探讨人机协作是否提升预测准确性。结果发现，个体在人机协作中的表现呈三态分布：多数人要么与模型预测一致，要么依赖模型反而表现更差；少数人通过互补推理达到与市场或超越市场的准确率。这些高表现者并非因认知能力或模型基准突出，而是具备共情、谦逊与好奇心等协作特质。结果虽初步，但统计显著，提示人类资本而非模型基准决定人机协作成效。
Learning to Move Before Learning to Do: Task-Agnostic pretraining for VLAs
Authors: Junhao Shi, Siyin Wang, Xiaopeng Yu, Li Ji, Jingjing Gong, Xipeng Qiu
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-07-02T17:33:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:33:37+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026, 21 pages,6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are fundamentally bottlenecked by the scarcity of expert demonstrations -- triplets of observations, instructions, and actions that are costly to collect at scale. We argue that this bottleneck stems from conflating two distinct learning objectives: acquiring physical competence (how to move) and acquiring semantic alignment (what to do). Crucially, only the latter requires language supervision. Building on this Decomposition Hypothesis, we propose Task-Agnostic Pretraining (TAP), a two-stage framework that first learns transferable motor priors from cheap, unlabeled interaction data -- including discarded off-task trajectories and autonomous robot play -- via a self-supervised Inverse Dynamics objective. A lightweight second stage then grounds these priors in language using minimal expert data. On the SIMPLER benchmark, TAP matches models trained on over 1M expert trajectories while using orders of magnitude less labeled data, yielding a 10% absolute gain over standard behavior cloning. On a real-world WidowX platform, TAP retains 25% success under camera perturbations where internet-scale baselines collapse to 0%, demonstrating that task-agnostic pretraining produces robust, transferable physical representations and offers a scalable path forward for Embodied AI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：先学移动再学做事：面向视觉-语言-动作模型的任务无关预训练
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的根本瓶颈在于专家演示（观察、指令与动作三元组）的稀缺性——大规模收集成本高昂。我们认为，这一瓶颈源于混淆了两个不同的学习目标：获取物理能力（如何移动）与获取语义对齐（做什么）。关键在于，仅后者需要语言监督。基于此分解假设，我们提出任务无关预训练（TAP），一种两阶段框架：第一阶段通过自监督逆动力学目标，从廉价无标签交互数据（包括偏离任务轨迹和机器人自主探索）中学习可迁移的运动先验；第二阶段则以少量专家数据将这些先验与语言对齐。在SIMPLER基准上，TAP在使用远少于百万级专家轨迹数据的情况下，表现与之相当，相较标准行为克隆提升10%。在真实WidowX平台上，TAP在摄像头扰动下保持25%成功率，而互联网规模基线则崩溃至0%，证明任务无关预训练可生成鲁棒、可迁移的物理表征，为具身AI提供可扩展的发展路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of expert demonstrations in Vision-Language-Action models, the paper proposes Task-Agnostic Pretraining (TAP), which separates learning physical movement from semantic alignment. TAP first learns motor priors via self-supervised inverse dynamics from cheap, unlabeled interaction data, then grounds these priors in language using minimal expert data. Experiments show TAP matches performance of models trained on over 1M expert trajectories while using far less labeled data, achieving a 10% absolute gain over behavior cloning on SIMPLER and maintaining 25% success under camera perturbations on a real WidowX robot, where internet-scale baselines fail completely.
为了解决视觉-语言-动作模型中专家演示稀缺的问题，作者提出任务无关预训练（TAP），将学习物理运动与语义对齐分离。TAP 首先通过自监督逆动力学从低成本无标签交互数据中学习运动先验，再用少量专家数据将先验与语言对齐。实验表明，TAP 在仅用远少于专家数据的情况下，性能可媲美训练于百万级专家轨迹的模型，相较行为克隆提升10%，并在真实机器人上于摄像头扰动下保持25%成功率，而基线模型完全失效。
Will Scaling Improve Social Simulation with LLMs?
Authors: Caleb Ziems, William Held, Su Doga Karaca, David Grusky, Tatsunori Hashimoto, Diyi Yang
First: 2026-07-02T17:30:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:30:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Model (LLM) social simulations are a promising research method, but they are not yet faithful enough to be adopted widely. In this work, we investigate whether the current scaling paradigm in language modeling is likely to close these gaps, or whether simulation fidelity is orthogonal to general capabilities and therefore deserving of more research attention. We use scaling laws to study the relationship between LLMs' compute scale, general capability benchmarks, and the fidelity of social simulation in three representative sub-domains: opinion modeling, behavioral simulation, and longitudinal forecasting. Surprisingly, we discover strong compute scaling in all three settings, using a suite of 85 transformer LLMs with the Qwen3 architecture pre-trained on the DCLM web text corpus under fixed-compute budgets from $10^{18}$ to $10^{20}$ FLOPs. Then we evaluate 35 larger and more capable open-weight models up to 70B parameters, allowing us to predict downstream accuracy from loss. This reveals that the majority of behavioral and opinion simulation tasks will rapidly improve with scale, particularly when they involve populations that are well-represented in English web corpora. Longitudinal forecasting and underrepresented opinions scale more slowly, especially when they are less correlated with general knowledge and reasoning benchmarks like MMLU. In behavior simulation, scaling fails to improve model calibration with human cognitive biases like risk aversion, as well as human heuristics like learning correlated rewards from related tasks. On these tasks, even fine-tuned models fail to noticeably scale up performance from 0.5B to 8B parameters. Taken together, we conclude that scale will improve social simulations in most settings, but outliers exist, and improvements will be less reliable in low-resource domains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩大规模能否提升基于大语言模型的社会模拟？
大语言模型（LLM）的社会模拟是一种有前景的研究方法，但目前尚不足以广泛采用。本研究探讨当前语言建模中的扩展范式是否能弥合这些差距，或模拟保真度与通用能力是否正交，从而值得更多研究关注。我们利用扩展定律，研究LLM的计算规模、通用能力基准与三个代表性子领域（意见建模、行为模拟、纵向预测）中社会模拟保真度之间的关系。令人惊讶的是，我们发现所有三个场景均存在显著的计算规模效应，使用85个基于Qwen3架构、在DCLM网络文本语料库上预训练的Transformer LLM，在固定计算预算（$10^{18}$至$10^{20}$ FLOPs）下进行测试。随后我们评估了35个更大、能力更强的开源模型（最高达70B参数），从而能够从损失预测下游准确率。结果表明，大多数行为和意见模拟任务会随规模迅速提升，尤其当涉及在英文网络语料库中充分代表的人群时。纵向预测和未充分代表的意见则扩展较慢，尤其当它们与MMLU等通用知识与推理基准的相关性较低时。在行为模拟中，扩展无法改善模型对人类认知偏差（如风险规避）和启发式（如从相关任务中学习关联奖励）的校准能力。在这些任务上，即使经过微调，模型从0.5B到8B参数也未能显著提升性能。综合来看，我们得出结论：规模将在大多数场景中提升社会模拟，但存在例外，且在低资源领域改进将更不可靠。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether scaling LLMs improves social simulation fidelity, motivated by the gap between LLMs' general capabilities and their ability to simulate human social behavior accurately. Using scaling laws across 85 transformer models with fixed compute budgets from 10^18 to 10^20 FLOPs, the authors analyze three domains: opinion modeling, behavioral simulation, and longitudinal forecasting. Results show strong scaling in opinion and behavioral tasks when populations are well-represented in English corpora, but slower scaling in longitudinal forecasting and underrepresented opinions, especially when tasks are poorly aligned with general benchmarks like MMLU. Notably, behavioral simulation fails to scale in modeling human cognitive biases and heuristics, even with fine-tuning from 0.5B to 8B parameters, indicating that scale alone is insufficient for certain social simulation challenges.
该研究探讨扩大语言模型规模是否能提升社会模拟的准确性，动机在于当前LLM在模拟人类行为方面仍存在明显不足。研究通过85个Qwen3架构模型（在DCLM网络文本上预训练）在10^18至10^20 FLOPs固定算力预算下，分析了意见建模、行为模拟和长期预测三个子领域中的计算规模效应。结果显示，当模拟对象在英文语料中充分代表时，意见与行为模拟任务随规模显著提升；但长期预测和少数意见的模拟提升缓慢，尤其当其与通用能力基准（如MMLU）相关性较低时。值得注意的是，行为模拟在人类认知偏差（如风险规避）或启发式策略（如跨任务奖励学习）的校准上，即使经过微调（0.5B至8B参数），也未见明显提升。结论是：规模扩展多数情况下能改善社会模拟，但存在例外，低资源或认知复杂领域提升不可靠。
Is One Layer Enough? Training A Single Transformer Layer Can Match Full-Parameter RL Training
Authors: Zijian Zhang, Rizhen Hu, Athanasios Glentis, Dawei Li, Chung-Yiu Yau, Hongzhou Lin, Mingyi Hong
First: 2026-07-01T17:59:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:28:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central component of post-training large language models (LLMs), yet little is understood about how RL adaptation is distributed across transformer layers. Existing approaches typically update all model parameters uniformly, implicitly assuming that every layer contributes similarly to the gains obtained during RL post-training. In this work, we challenge this assumption through a systematic layer-wise study of RL training. Surprisingly, we find that training a single transformer layer can recover most of the gains achieved by full-parameter RL training, and in some cases even surpass it. To quantify this phenomenon, we introduce the quantity layer contribution, which measures the fraction of full RL improvement recovered by training a layer in isolation. Across seven models spanning two model families (Qwen3, Qwen2.5), three RL algorithms (GRPO, GiGPO, Dr. GRPO), and multiple task domains including mathematical reasoning, code generation, and agentic decision-making, we observe a remarkably stable pattern: RL gains are highly concentrated in a small subset of, and in many cases even a single, transformer layers. More strikingly, the same structural pattern consistently emerges: high-contribution layers concentrate in the middle of the transformer stack, while layers near the input and output ends contribute substantially less. The resulting layer rankings remain strongly correlated across datasets, tasks, model families, and RL algorithms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一层足够吗？训练单个Transformer层即可匹配全参数强化学习训练
强化学习（RL）已成为大语言模型（LLMs）后训练的核心组成部分，但人们对RL适应如何在Transformer各层间分布仍知之甚少。现有方法通常均匀更新所有模型参数，隐含假设各层对RL后训练获得的收益贡献相似。本研究通过系统性的层间分析挑战这一假设。令人惊讶的是，我们发现训练单个Transformer层即可恢复大部分全参数RL训练所获得的收益，某些情况下甚至超越其效果。为量化此现象，我们引入“层贡献”指标，衡量单独训练某层所能恢复的全RL改进比例。在涵盖两个模型家族（Qwen3、Qwen2.5）、三种RL算法（GRPO、GiGPO、Dr. GRPO）及多个任务领域（数学推理、代码生成、代理决策）的七种模型中，我们观察到一个显著稳定的模式：RL收益高度集中于少数层，甚至常集中于单层。更引人注目的是，相同的结构模式始终出现：高贡献层集中于Transformer堆栈中部，而靠近输入和输出端的层贡献显著较低。由此产生的层排名在不同数据集、任务、模型家族和RL算法间保持高度相关性。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether training only a single transformer layer can achieve most of the performance gains from full-parameter reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning of large language models. Motivated by the lack of understanding about how RL improvements are distributed across layers, the authors conduct a systematic layer-wise analysis and introduce 'layer contribution' as a metric to quantify how much RL improvement a single layer can recover. Across seven models, three RL algorithms, and multiple task domains, they find that training just one middle layer often recovers most or even all of the gains from full-parameter RL, with high-contribution layers consistently clustering in the middle of the transformer stack while input/output layers contribute little. The pattern holds across different models, tasks, and RL methods, indicating a strong structural regularity in RL adaptation.
该研究探讨单层Transformer是否足以实现全参数强化学习（RL）微调的大部分性能增益，挑战了所有层贡献均等的假设。作者引入“层贡献”指标，量化单层独立训练所能恢复的RL改进比例。在七种模型、三种RL算法和多个任务中发现，仅训练一层即可恢复大部分增益，有时甚至超越全参数训练。高贡献层始终集中于Transformer堆栈中部，而输入和输出层贡献微弱，且该模式在不同模型、任务和算法间保持稳定。
OrbitQuant: Data-Agnostic Quantization for Image and Video Diffusion Transformers
Authors: Donghyun Lee, Jitesh Chavan, Duy Nguyen, Sam Huang, Liming Jiang, Priyadarshini Panda, Timo Mertens, Saurabh Shukla
First: 2026-07-02T17:27:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:27:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion transformers (DiTs) achieve state-of-the-art image and video generation, but their multi-step sampling and growing parameter count make inference expensive. Post-training quantization (PTQ) is the natural remedy, yet DiT activations shift across timesteps, prompts, and guidance branches, forcing prior methods to re-fit calibration data for every new checkpoint or modality. We present OrbitQuant, a data-agnostic weight-activation quantizer that bypasses range estimation by quantizing in a normalized, rotated basis. In this basis, a randomized permuted block-Hadamard (RPBH) rotation concentrates each coordinate around one fixed, known marginal regardless of the input, so a single Lloyd-Max codebook serves all timesteps, prompts, and layers of a given input dimension. We extend the same quantizer to weight rows offline, absorbing the rotation into the weights so that it cancels inside each linear layer and only a forward rotation on the activations remains at runtime. The same recipe transfers from image to video with no per-modality tuning. Across FLUX.1, Z-Image-Turbo, Wan 2.1, and CogVideoX, it sets the state of the art for PTQ at several low-bit settings. It also pushes PTQ of image diffusion transformers to W2A4 with usable generation quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OrbitQuant：面向图像与视频扩散Transformer的数据无关量化方法
扩散Transformer（DiTs）在图像和视频生成方面达到最先进水平，但其多步采样和参数量增长导致推理成本高昂。后训练量化（PTQ）是自然解决方案，然而DiT激活值随时间步、提示和引导分支变化，迫使现有方法需为每个新检查点或模态重新拟合校准数据。我们提出OrbitQuant，一种数据无关的权重-激活量化器，通过在归一化旋转基下进行量化，绕过范围估计。在此基下，随机置换块Hadamard（RPBH）旋转使每个坐标无论输入如何，均集中于一个固定已知边缘分布，因此单个Lloyd-Max码本即可服务所有时间步、提示和给定输入维度的层。我们进一步将同一量化器扩展至离线量化权重行，将旋转嵌入权重中，使其在每层线性变换内抵消，仅在运行时保留激活值的前向旋转。该方法可无缝从图像迁移到视频，无需模态特定调优。在FLUX.1、Z-Image-Turbo、Wan 2.1和CogVideoX上，它在多个低比特设置中达到PTQ最先进水平，并将图像扩散Transformer的PTQ推进至W2A4，仍保持可用生成质量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high inference cost of diffusion transformers due to multi-step sampling and growing parameters, the paper introduces OrbitQuant, a data-agnostic quantization method that avoids per-checkpoint calibration by quantizing weights and activations in a normalized, rotated basis using a randomized permuted block-Hadamard transformation. This rotation concentrates each coordinate’s distribution around a fixed marginal, enabling a single Lloyd-Max codebook to serve all timesteps, prompts, and layers within a dimension. The method extends to weights by pre-absorbing the rotation into weight matrices, leaving only a lightweight forward rotation at runtime. Tested across multiple image and video diffusion models including FLUX.1, Z-Image-Turbo, Wan 2.1, and CogVideoX, OrbitQuant achieves state-of-the-art post-training quantization performance at low bit-rates, notably reaching W2A4 with usable generation quality without modality-specific tuning.
为了解决扩散Transformer因多步采样和参数增长导致的推理成本高问题，本文提出OrbitQuant，一种无需数据依赖的量化方法。该方法通过随机置换块Hadamard旋转将权重与激活值映射到归一化坐标系中，使每个坐标分布集中于固定边际，从而仅需一个Lloyd-Max码本即可覆盖所有时间步、提示和层。权重部分通过预处理将旋转嵌入权重矩阵，运行时仅需对激活值进行前向旋转，且无需针对图像或视频单独调参。实验表明，该方法在多个扩散模型上实现了低比特率下的最优PTQ效果，包括在图像扩散Transformer中达到W2A4精度且生成质量可用。
Neuron-Aware Data Selection for Annotation-Free LLM Self-Distillation
Authors: Zhuowei Chen, Xiang Lorraine Li
First: 2026-07-02T17:27:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:27:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Post-training large language models (LLMs) without real-world interaction feedback or human-labeled supervision remains challenging, particularly in specialized domains where expert annotations are costly to obtain. Recent annotation-free self-evolution methods address this by using the model's own outputs as supervision signals, constructing a teacher via additional context and aggregating predictions across multiple rollouts through majority voting to produce pseudo-labels. However, these approaches are not without drawbacks: SFT- and GRPO-based variants suffer out-of-domain performance degradation, while reward-based on-policy RL inflates calibration error. In this paper, we propose Neuron On-Policy Self-Distillation (Neuron-OPSD), a data-centric framework for annotation-free self-distillation that leverages internal neuron activations to guide both training-data selection and teacher context construction. The model is then trained via on-policy distillation from the teacher distribution, requiring no ground-truth labels at any stage. Across specialized-domain benchmarks, Neuron-OPSD improves in-domain task performance while preserving cross-domain generalization and mitigating calibration collapse over prior annotation-free baselines. This framework is particularly relevant to settings where online interaction or external supervision is costly or infeasible, and is conceptually distinct from offline RL approaches that rely on logged, reward-labeled trajectories.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向无标注LLM自蒸馏的神经元感知数据选择
在缺乏现实世界交互反馈或人工标注监督的情况下，训练后的大语言模型（LLM）在专业领域仍具挑战性，因专家标注成本高昂。近期无标注自演化方法通过使用模型自身输出作为监督信号，构建教师模型并借助多轮次多数投票生成伪标签。然而，这些方法存在缺陷：SFT和GRPO变体在域外性能下降，基于奖励的在线强化学习则加剧校准误差。本文提出神经元在线自蒸馏（Neuron-OPSD），一种以数据为中心的无标注自蒸馏框架，利用内部神经元激活引导训练数据选择与教师上下文构建，再通过教师分布进行在线蒸馏训练，全程无需真实标签。在专业领域基准测试中，Neuron-OPSD提升域内任务性能，同时保持跨域泛化能力并缓解校准崩溃问题，相较现有无标注基线方法更具优势。该框架适用于在线交互或外部监督成本高昂或不可行的场景，且与依赖日志化奖励轨迹的离线强化学习方法在概念上不同。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of training large language models without human annotations or real-world feedback, especially in specialized domains, this paper introduces Neuron On-Policy Self-Distillation (Neuron-OPSD), a data-centric method that uses internal neuron activations to guide both data selection and teacher context construction, enabling annotation-free self-distillation. The model is trained via on-policy distillation from a teacher distribution derived from aggregated rollouts, without requiring ground-truth labels. Experiments show Neuron-OPSD improves in-domain performance while preserving cross-domain generalization and reducing calibration error compared to prior annotation-free baselines.
为了解决在缺乏人工标注或现实交互反馈的领域中训练大语言模型的难题，本文提出Neuron-OPSD方法，该方法利用模型内部神经元激活来指导训练数据选择和教师上下文构建，实现无标注自蒸馏。模型通过从教师分布进行在线蒸馏训练，全程无需真实标签。实验表明，Neuron-OPSD在提升领域内任务性能的同时，保持了跨域泛化能力，并降低了校准误差，优于现有无标注基线方法。
Language Models as Measurement Apparatus for Culture
Authors: Kent K. Chang
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-07-02T17:25:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:25:55+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the Big Picture workshop co-located with ACL 2026. This version expands the camera-ready (adding Fig. 3 and section 6.3, as well as correcting minor typos) in Proceedings of The Big Picture v2: Crafting a Research Narrative, pp. 131--143, San Diego, CA, USA. Association for Computational Linguistics
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models are increasingly used to quantify cultural phenomena, but what makes such measurement distinctively cultural? This paper argues that NLP work on culture is a material-discursive practice: the apparatus -- model, data, annotation, evaluation -- participates in constituting the cultural reality it measures, rather than passively recording it. Drawing on Karen Barad's concept of the agential cut -- the contingent boundary between phenomenon and instrument -- I show that the apparatus's substantive design choices draw such boundaries, and that the boundary is entangled from the start because language models have already internalized much of the cultural material they measure. I illustrate this through three case studies on television and film dialogue (measuring structure, interaction, and deviation) and three examinations of the apparatus itself (erasure of cultural markers, attunement to historical material, and agency in an agentic workflow). This big picture analysis proposes a research program that is theory-driven, empirically rigorous, and culturally contingent, treating each agential cut as a conscious commitment, at once methodological and ethical.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型作为文化测量装置
语言模型正越来越多地用于量化文化现象，但什么使这种测量具有文化独特性？本文认为，自然语言处理领域对文化的探索是一种物质-话语实践：装置——模型、数据、标注、评估——参与构建其测量的文化现实，而非被动记录。借鉴凯伦·巴德的‘行动性切割’概念——现象与工具之间的偶然边界——我表明，装置的实质性设计选择决定了此类边界，且该边界从一开始就相互纠缠，因为语言模型已内化了其测量的大量文化材料。我通过三个关于电视和电影对话的案例研究（测量结构、互动与偏离）以及三个对装置本身的考察（文化标记的抹除、对历史材料的敏感性、行动性工作流中的主体性）加以说明。这一宏观分析提出了一项理论驱动、实证严谨、文化情境化的研究计划，将每个行动性切割视为一种方法论与伦理上的自觉承诺。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how language models function as active cultural measurement tools rather than neutral recorders, arguing that their design and training data co-constitute the cultural phenomena they analyze. Drawing on Karen Barad’s agential cut framework, the author demonstrates that the boundaries between model and culture are not fixed but emerge from the apparatus’s material choices, which are already saturated with cultural content. Through case studies of TV/film dialogue analysis and examinations of model behavior—such as erasing cultural markers or responding to historical context—the paper reveals how measurement is inherently entangled with cultural production. The findings advocate for a research approach that treats each methodological decision as an ethical commitment, grounding cultural NLP in theory, empirical rigor, and cultural specificity.
本文探讨语言模型如何作为主动的文化测量工具而非被动记录者，认为其设计与训练数据通过物质-话语实践共同构成文化现象。基于巴德的‘能动切分’概念，研究指出模型与文化之间的边界从一开始就因模型已内化大量文化材料而相互纠缠。通过对电视剧/电影对话结构、互动与偏离的案例分析，以及对模型装置层面问题（如文化标记的抹除、历史材料的敏感性、能动工作流中的主体性）的考察，研究揭示模型设计选择并非中立，而是深深植根于文化语境。研究结果提出一种理论驱动、经验严谨、文化情境化的研究范式，将每一次能动切分视为方法论与伦理上的自觉承诺。
When Do LLM Personas Support Visualization Design? A Cross-Model Study of Color Assignment and Chart Choice
Authors: Shahreen Salim, Klaus Mueller
First: 2026-07-02T17:23:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:23:57+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model personas are increasingly used to approximate diverse users during early-stage visualization design, but it remains unclear whether persona-conditioned outputs reflect stable personality effects or artifacts of model choice and task framing. We examine this question across two visualization-relevant tasks: color assignment for abstract and concrete concepts, and chart-idiom preference ratings across task contexts. Using 43 Big Five profiles across GPT-4o-mini, GPT-4.1-mini, and GPT-5-mini, we find that personality-color coupling is highly model-configuration dependent: absent in GPT-4o-mini for all six concepts, consistent in GPT-4.1-mini across all six, and partial in GPT-5-mini for two of six. Concept type further shapes the signal: for abstract concepts, personality explains more hue variance than model identity, while concrete concepts show smaller and comparable effects. In chart choice, trait-aligned cluster aggregation produces stable top-idiom rankings across all nine cluster-context combinations, but a no-persona baseline recovers the same top choice in 8 of 9 model-context cells, indicating that task context drives rank-1 selection more than personality. These findings position LLM personas as exploratory probes for visualization design, not substitutes for human participants, and motivate multi-model testing, concept-type disaggregation, and no-persona baselines in future studies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型人格如何支持可视化设计？一项关于色彩分配与图表选择的跨模型研究
大语言模型人格在可视化设计早期阶段被越来越多地用于模拟多样化用户，但尚不清楚人格引导的输出反映的是稳定的人格效应，还是模型选择和任务框架的产物。我们通过两项与可视化相关的任务——抽象与具体概念的色彩分配，以及不同任务情境下的图表习语偏好评分，对此问题进行考察。在GPT-4o-mini、GPT-4.1-mini和GPT-5-mini三个模型中使用43个大五人格配置，我们发现人格-色彩关联高度依赖模型配置：在GPT-4o-mini中对所有六个概念均无此效应，在GPT-4.1-mini中对所有六个概念均一致，在GPT-5-mini中仅对其中两个概念部分存在。概念类型进一步影响该效应：对于抽象概念，人格解释的色相变异量大于模型身份；而对于具体概念，效应较小且相近。在图表选择中，与人格一致的聚类聚合能稳定产生各九种聚类-情境组合中的顶级习语排名，但无人格基线在8/9个模型-情境单元中恢复相同顶级选择，表明任务情境比人格更主导第一选择。这些发现将大语言模型人格定位为可视化设计的探索性探针，而非人类参与者的替代品，并推动未来研究采用多模型测试、概念类型拆分及无人格基线。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language model personas reliably reflect personality-driven visualization design preferences, particularly in color assignment and chart choice tasks. Using 43 Big Five personality profiles across three GPT-mini models, the researchers found that personality-color alignment varies significantly by model configuration: absent in GPT-4o-mini, consistent in GPT-4.1-mini, and partial in GPT-5-mini. For abstract concepts, personality explained more hue variance than model identity, while concrete concepts showed weaker and comparable effects. In chart choice, personality-aligned clustering produced stable top-idiom rankings, but a no-persona baseline matched the top choice in 8 out of 9 model-context combinations, suggesting task context outweighs personality in driving selection. The results suggest LLM personas are useful exploratory tools but not reliable substitutes for human participants, urging future studies to test across models, disaggregate by concept type, and include no-persona baselines.
该研究探讨了LLM人格是否能稳定反映可视化设计中的个性偏好，通过在三个GPT-mini模型上使用43种大五人格配置，完成颜色分配（抽象/具体概念）和图表选择偏好两项任务。结果表明，人格-颜色关联高度依赖模型配置：GPT-4o-mini无关联，GPT-4.1-mini完全一致，GPT-5-mini仅部分概念有效。抽象概念中，人格解释的色相方差高于模型身份，而具体概念则效应较弱且相近。在图表选择中，人格对齐的聚类可稳定产出排名靠前的图表类型，但无人格基线在9个模型-情境组合中8个匹配最高选择，说明任务情境比人格更能决定首选。研究结论认为LLM人格是探索性工具而非替代人类参与者，建议未来研究采用多模型测试、概念类型区分及无人格基线。
VLSA: Vision-Language-Action Models with Plug-and-Play Safety Constraint Layer
Authors: Songqiao Hu, Zeyi Liu, Shuang Liu, Jun Cen, Zihan Meng, Shihefeng Wang, Xiang Li, Xiao He
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2025-12-09T16:53:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:23:13+00:00
Comments: Accepted by IROS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in generalizing across diverse robotic manipulation tasks. However, deploying these models in unstructured environments remains challenging due to the critical need for simultaneous task compliance and safety assurance, particularly in preventing potential collisions during physical interactions. In this work, we introduce a Vision-Language-Safe Action (VLSA) architecture, named AEGIS, which contains a plug-and-play safety constraint (SC) layer formulated via control barrier functions. AEGIS integrates directly with existing VLA models to improve safety with theoretical guarantees, while maintaining their original instruction-following performance. To evaluate the efficacy of our architecture, we construct a comprehensive safety-critical benchmark SafeLIBERO, spanning distinct manipulation scenarios characterized by varying degrees of spatial complexity and obstacle intervention. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of our method over state-of-the-art baselines. Notably, AEGIS achieves over 50% improvement in obstacle avoidance rate while substantially increasing the task success rate by nearly 10%. All benchmark datasets, code, and supplementary materials are publicly available at https://vlsa-aegis.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VLSA：带即插即用安全约束层的视觉-语言-动作模型
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在多种机器人操作任务中的泛化能力已得到显著验证。然而，由于在物理交互中同时满足任务合规性和安全保证（特别是避免潜在碰撞）至关重要，这些模型在非结构化环境中的部署仍具挑战。本文提出一种视觉-语言-安全动作（VLSA）架构AEGIS，其包含基于控制屏障函数构建的即插即用安全约束（SC）层。AEGIS可直接集成于现有VLA模型，以理论保障提升安全性，同时保持原始指令遵循性能。为评估架构有效性，我们构建了涵盖不同空间复杂度和障碍干预场景的综合性安全关键基准SafeLIBERO。大量实验表明，本方法优于现有最先进基线。值得注意的是，AEGIS在障碍规避率上提升超50%，任务成功率提高近10%。所有基准数据集、代码及补充材料均公开于https://vlsa-aegis.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to safely deploy Vision-Language-Action models in unstructured environments without compromising task performance, this work introduces AEGIS, a plug-and-play safety constraint layer based on control barrier functions that integrates with existing VLA models. The method ensures safety through theoretical guarantees while preserving original instruction-following capabilities. Evaluated on SafeLIBERO, a new safety-critical benchmark with varying spatial complexity and obstacle scenarios, AEGIS outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving over 50% improvement in obstacle avoidance and nearly 10% increase in task success rate.
为在无结构环境中安全部署视觉-语言-动作模型同时保持任务性能，本文提出AEGIS架构，其采用基于控制屏障函数的可插拔安全约束层，直接集成于现有VLA模型中，提供理论安全保证而不影响原指令遵循能力。在新构建的SafeLIBERO基准测试中，该基准涵盖不同空间复杂度和障碍密度的多样化操作场景，实验表明AEGIS显著优于现有最先进方法，障碍规避率提升超50%，任务成功率提高近10%。
BALF: Budgeted Activation-Aware Low-Rank Factorization for Fine-Tuning-Free Model Compression
Authors: David González-Martínez
First: 2025-09-29T17:50:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:21:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Activation-aware low-rank factorization techniques yield strong compression results but are generally confined to linear layers, while existing whitening-based theory typically makes an implicit full-rank assumption on activations. We introduce a layer representation framework that extends activation-aware factorization beyond linear layers, including standard and grouped convolutions. Within this framework, our whitening-based formulation is more general than prior ones, naturally covering rank-deficient activations, and yields an optimal low-rank projection that attains the reconstruction error of the best low-rank approximation to layer activations. The resulting singular spectrum provides a closed-form per-layer distortion proxy, which we use to allocate per-layer ranks under explicit FLOP or parameter-count budgets via a Lagrangian relaxation with negligible overhead. Together, these components form BALF, an end-to-end pipeline for efficient vision model compression. Across CNNs and vision transformers on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-1K, BALF generally achieves higher accuracy than SVD-based factorization baselines at matched FLOP or parameter count targets and remains competitive with other fine-tuning-free compression techniques.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BALF：面向无微调模型压缩的预算感知激活感知低秩因子分解
激活感知低秩因子分解技术虽压缩效果显著，但通常仅适用于线性层，而现有基于白化理论的方法通常隐含假设激活为满秩。我们引入一种层表示框架，将激活感知因子分解扩展至线性层之外，包括标准和分组卷积。在此框架下，我们的白化公式比先前方法更通用，自然涵盖秩亏激活，并能获得最优低秩投影，实现对层激活最佳低秩近似的重构误差。所得奇异谱提供闭式每层失真代理，我们通过拉格朗日松弛在显式FLOP或参数数量预算下分配每层秩，且开销可忽略。这些组件共同构成BALF，一个面向视觉模型高效压缩的端到端流程。在CIFAR-10和ImageNet-1K上的CNN与视觉Transformer中，BALF在匹配FLOP或参数数量目标下通常比基于SVD的因子分解基线方法获得更高精度，并与其他无微调压缩技术保持竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of activation-aware low-rank factorization to linear layers and the implicit full-rank assumption in existing whitening-based methods, the authors propose BALF, a framework that extends factorization to convolutional layers while handling rank-deficient activations via a generalized whitening formulation. The method computes a closed-form distortion proxy per layer using singular spectrum and allocates ranks under FLOP or parameter budgets via Lagrangian relaxation with minimal overhead. Experiments on CNNs and vision transformers across CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-1K show BALF consistently outperforms SVD-based baselines at matched compression targets and remains competitive with other fine-tuning-free methods.
为突破激活感知低秩分解仅适用于线性层且现有白化理论隐含满秩假设的局限，作者提出BALF框架，扩展分解至标准和分组卷积层并支持秩亏激活。其白化公式可自然覆盖秩亏情况，生成最优低秩投影以匹配层激活的最佳低秩近似，从而获得每层闭式失真代理。通过拉格朗日松弛，结合FLOP或参数预算分配每层秩，开销极小。在CIFAR-10和ImageNet-1K上的CNN与视觉Transformer实验表明，BALF在相同压缩目标下普遍优于SVD基线方法，且与其它无微调压缩技术保持竞争力。
Conformal Policy Control
Authors: Drew Prinster, Clara Fannjiang, Ji Won Park, Kyunghyun Cho, Anqi Liu, Suchi Saria, Samuel Stanton
Venue: ICML
First: 2026-03-02T18:54:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:17:09+00:00
Comments: International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML), 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
An agent must try new behaviors to explore and improve. In high-stakes environments, an agent that violates safety constraints may cause harm and must be taken offline, curtailing any future interaction. Imitating old behavior is safe, but excessive conservatism discourages exploration. How much behavior change is too much? We show how to use any safe reference policy as a probabilistic regulator for any optimized but untested policy. Conformal calibration on data from the safe policy determines how aggressively the new policy can act, while provably enforcing the user's declared risk tolerance. Unlike conservative optimization methods, we do not assume the user has identified the correct model class nor tuned any hyperparameters. Unlike previous conformal methods, our theory provides finite-sample guarantees even for non-monotonic bounded loss functions, and it introduces a new policy control setting. Our experiments on applications ranging from natural language question answering to biomolecular engineering show that safe exploration is not only possible from the first moment of deployment, but can also improve performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：保形策略控制
智能体必须尝试新行为以实现探索与改进。在高风险环境中，违反安全约束的智能体可能造成危害，必须被下线，从而终止任何未来交互。模仿旧行为是安全的，但过度保守会抑制探索。行为变化到何种程度才算过度？我们展示了如何利用任何安全的参考策略作为任何经过优化但未经测试策略的概率调节器。通过对安全策略数据进行保形校准，可确定新策略可采取的激进程度，同时可证明其满足用户声明的风险容忍度。与保守优化方法不同，我们不假设用户已识别正确的模型类或调优任何超参数。与以往保形方法不同，我们的理论即使在非单调有界损失函数下也能提供有限样本保证，并引入了一种新的策略控制范式。我们在从自然语言问答到生物分子工程等应用中的实验表明，安全探索不仅在部署之初即可行，还能提升性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to balance exploration with safety in high-stakes environments, this work proposes a method to control an optimized but untested policy using any safe reference policy as a probabilistic regulator. By applying conformal calibration on data from the safe policy, the method quantifies how aggressively the new policy can act while provably respecting user-defined risk tolerance. Experiments across domains including natural language question answering and biomolecular engineering demonstrate that safe exploration is feasible from deployment onset and can even enhance performance without requiring prior model class assumptions or hyperparameter tuning.
为在高风险环境中平衡探索与安全，本文提出一种方法，利用任意已知安全的参考策略作为概率调节器，控制未经测试的优化策略。该方法通过在安全策略数据上进行符合性校准，动态确定允许的行为变化范围，并在有限样本下保证用户风险容忍度。实验涵盖自然语言问答与生物分子工程等多个领域，表明该方法可在部署初期即实现安全探索，并持续提升性能，无需预先假设模型类别或调整超参数。
Understanding the Robustness of Distributed Self-Supervised Learning Frameworks Against Non-IID Data
Authors: Xuanyu Chen, Nan Yang, Shuai Wang, Dong Yuan
First: 2026-07-02T17:17:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:17:05+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICLR2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent research has introduced distributed self-supervised learning (D-SSL) approaches to leverage vast amounts of unlabeled decentralized data. However, D-SSL faces the critical challenge of data heterogeneity, and there is limited theoretical understanding of how different D-SSL frameworks respond to this challenge. To fill this gap, we present a rigorous theoretical analysis of the robustness of D-SSL frameworks under non-IID (non-independent and identically distributed) settings. Our results show that pre-training with Masked Image Modeling (MIM) is inherently more robust to heterogeneous data than Contrastive Learning (CL), and that the robustness of decentralized SSL increases with average network connectivity, implying that federated learning (FL) is no less robust than decentralized learning (DecL). These findings provide a solid theoretical foundation for guiding the design of future D-SSL algorithms. To further illustrate the practical implications of our theory, we introduce MAR loss, a refinement of the MIM objective with local-to-global alignment regularization. Extensive experiments across model architectures and distributed settings validate our theoretical insights, and additionally confirm the effectiveness of MAR loss as an application of our analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：理解分布式自监督学习框架对非独立同分布数据的鲁棒性
近期研究引入了分布式自监督学习（D-SSL）方法，以利用大量分散的无标签数据。然而，D-SSL面临数据异质性的关键挑战，且对不同D-SSL框架如何应对这一挑战的理论理解有限。为填补这一空白，我们对非IID（非独立同分布）环境下D-SSL框架的鲁棒性进行了严谨的理论分析。结果表明，使用掩码图像建模（MIM）预训练比对比学习（CL）对异质数据更具固有鲁棒性，且去中心化SSL的鲁棒性随平均网络连通性增加而提升，意味着联邦学习（FL）与去中心化学习（DecL）的鲁棒性相当。这些发现为未来D-SSL算法设计提供了坚实的理论基础。为进一步说明理论的实际意义，我们提出了MAR损失，即对MIM目标函数加入局部到全局对齐正则化的改进。在多种模型架构和分布式设置下的广泛实验验证了我们的理论见解，并进一步证实了MAR损失作为我们分析应用的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how distributed self-supervised learning (D-SSL) frameworks handle non-IID data, this study conducts a theoretical analysis of robustness in such settings. It finds that Masked Image Modeling (MIM) pre-training is inherently more robust than Contrastive Learning (CL) to data heterogeneity, and that decentralized learning robustness scales with network connectivity, making federated learning no less robust than decentralized learning. The paper further proposes MAR loss, a MIM variant with local-to-global alignment regularization, and experiments across architectures and settings confirm both theoretical predictions and MAR’s practical effectiveness.
为理解分布式自监督学习（D-SSL）框架在非独立同分布（non-IID）数据下的鲁棒性，本文从理论上分析了其在数据异质性下的表现。结果表明，掩码图像建模（MIM）预训练比对比学习（CL）对数据异质性更具鲁棒性，且分布式学习的鲁棒性随网络连通性增加而提升，说明联邦学习与分布式学习在鲁棒性上无显著差异。论文进一步提出MAR损失函数，作为MIM目标的改进版本，加入局部到全局对齐正则化，实验验证了理论结论及MAR的有效性。
Provably Finding a Hidden Dense Submatrix among Many Planted Dense Submatrices via Convex Programming
Authors: Valentine Olanubi, Phineas Agar, Brendan Ames
First: 2026-01-07T14:02:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:12:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We consider the densest submatrix problem, which seeks the submatrix of fixed size of a given binary matrix that contains the most nonzero entries. This problem is a natural generalization of fundamental problems in combinatorial optimization, e.g., the densest subgraph, maximum clique, and maximum edge biclique problems, and has wide application the study of complex networks. Much recent research has focused on the development of sufficient conditions for exact solution of the densest submatrix problem via convex relaxation. The vast majority of these sufficient conditions establish identification of the densest submatrix within a graph containing exactly one large dense submatrix hidden by noise. The assumptions of these underlying models are not observed in real-world networks, where the data may correspond to a matrix containing many dense submatrices of varying sizes. We extend and generalize these results to the more realistic setting where the input matrix may contain \emph{many} large dense subgraphs. Specifically, we establish sufficient conditions under which we can expect to solve the densest submatrix problem in polynomial time for random input matrices sampled from a generalization of the stochastic block model. Moreover, we also provide sufficient conditions for perfect recovery under a deterministic adversarial. Numerical experiments involving randomly generated problem instances and real-world collaboration and communication networks are used empirically to verify the theoretical phase-transitions to perfect recovery given by these sufficient conditions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过凸规划在众多预设密集子矩阵中可证明地找到隐藏的密集子矩阵
我们研究最密集子矩阵问题，即在给定二元矩阵中寻找固定大小的子矩阵，使其包含最多的非零元素。该问题是对组合优化中基本问题（如最密集子图、最大团、最大边双团）的自然推广，并在复杂网络研究中具有广泛应用。近期大量研究集中于通过凸松弛方法求解最密集子矩阵问题的充分条件。这些充分条件大多假设图中仅隐藏一个大型密集子矩阵，且受噪声干扰。然而，现实世界网络数据往往对应包含多个不同大小密集子矩阵的矩阵，而上述模型假设不成立。 我们扩展并推广了这些结果，适用于更现实的场景——输入矩阵可能包含多个大型密集子图。具体而言，我们建立了在随机输入矩阵（采样自随机块模型的推广）下，期望能在多项式时间内求解最密集子矩阵问题的充分条件。此外，我们还提供了在确定性对抗模型下实现完美恢复的充分条件。通过随机生成问题实例和真实世界协作与通信网络的数值实验，我们实证验证了这些充分条件所预测的理论相变点，即完美恢复的临界阈值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to identify hidden dense submatrices in real-world networks that often contain multiple such structures rather than just one, this work develops convex programming methods to provably recover the densest submatrix under noisy conditions. The authors extend prior theoretical results from single dense submatrix settings to scenarios with many planted dense submatrices, establishing sufficient conditions for polynomial-time exact recovery in random matrices drawn from a generalized stochastic block model and also under adversarial deterministic settings. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world networks confirm the theoretical phase transitions, demonstrating that the method successfully identifies dense submatrices when the signal-to-noise ratio meets the derived thresholds.
为应对现实网络中常存在多个隐藏密集子矩阵而非单一结构的问题，本文将凸规划方法扩展至多密集子矩阵场景。作者推导出在随机矩阵（来自广义随机块模型）和对抗性确定性设置下，可保证多项式时间内精确恢复的充分条件。实验结果在合成数据与真实协作及通信网络上验证了理论相变点与实际恢复性能的一致性，证实了该方法的鲁棒性。
Dive into Claude Code: The Design Space of Today's and Future AI Agent Systems
Authors: Jiacheng Liu, Xiaohan Zhao, Xinyi Shang, Zhiqiang Shen
First: 2026-04-14T17:59:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:12:13+00:00
Comments: Tech report. Code at: https://github.com/VILA-Lab/Dive-into-Claude-Code
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Claude Code is an agentic coding tool that can run shell commands, edit files, and call external services on behalf of the user. This study describes its architecture by analyzing the publicly available source code and comparing it with two independent open-source AI agent systems, OpenClaw and Hermes Agent, that answer many of similar or even the same design questions. Our analysis identifies five human values, philosophies, and needs that motivate the architecture: human decision authority, safety, security, and privacy, reliable execution, capability amplification, and contextual adaptability. We then trace them through thirteen design principles to implementation choices. The core of the system is a simple while-loop that calls the model, runs tools, and repeats. Most of the code, however, lives in the systems around this loop: a permission system with seven modes and an ML-based classifier, a five-layer compaction pipeline for context management, four extensibility mechanisms (MCP, plugins, skills, and hooks), a subagent delegation and orchestration mechanism, and append-oriented session storage. Comparisons with OpenClaw and Hermes Agent show that the same design questions produce different answers across three deployment contexts. Claude Code emphasizes per-action safety, OpenClaw emphasizes perimeter-level access, and Hermes renders per-action approvals across many surfaces. At the runtime layer, Claude Code uses a single CLI loop, OpenClaw embeds the runtime within a gateway control plane, and Hermes uses one process whose role is set by its entry point. At the context and extension layer, Claude Code extends the context window, OpenClaw registers gateway-wide capabilities, and Hermes provides pluggable memory and model backends. We finally identify six open design directions for future agent systems, grounded in recent empirical, architectural, and policy literature.
中文标题/摘要
标题：深入探索Claude Code：当今及未来AI代理系统的架构空间
Claude Code是一种代理式编码工具，可代表用户执行shell命令、编辑文件并调用外部服务。本研究通过分析其公开源代码，并与两个独立开源AI代理系统OpenClaw和Hermes Agent进行对比，描述了其架构。分析识别出五个驱动架构的人类价值、哲学与需求：人类决策权威、安全、隐私与保密、可靠执行、能力增强及情境适应性。随后，我们将这些价值贯穿至十三项设计原则并映射至具体实现选择。系统核心是一个简单的while循环，调用模型、运行工具并重复。然而，大部分代码存在于该循环周围的系统中：一个包含七种模式的权限系统及基于机器学习的分类器、一个五层压缩管道用于上下文管理、四种扩展机制（MCP、插件、技能和钩子）、子代理委派与编排机制，以及基于追加的会话存储。与OpenClaw和Hermes Agent的对比显示，在三种部署环境中，相同的设计问题会产生不同答案。Claude Code强调每项操作的安全性，OpenClaw强调边界级别的访问控制，而Hermes则在多个层面实现每项操作的审批。在运行时层，Claude Code采用单一CLI循环，OpenClaw将运行时嵌入网关控制平面，Hermes则使用一个通过入口点设定角色的进程。在上下文与扩展层，Claude Code扩展上下文窗口，OpenClaw注册网关全局能力，Hermes提供可插拔的记忆与模型后端。最后，我们基于近期的经验、架构与政策文献，识别出未来代理系统六个开放的设计方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how modern AI agent systems balance human control, safety, and adaptability, this study analyzes Claude Code’s architecture through source code and comparative design with OpenClaw and Hermes Agent. It identifies five core human values—decision authority, safety, security, reliable execution, capability amplification, and contextual adaptability—that drive thirteen design principles, implemented via a central while-loop for model calls and tool execution, surrounded by complex systems including a seven-mode permission system, a five-layer context compaction pipeline, and four extensibility mechanisms. Experimental comparisons reveal divergent design choices: Claude Code prioritizes per-action safety, OpenClaw focuses on perimeter access control, and Hermes enables multi-surface approvals. Runtime architectures differ too—Claude uses a CLI loop, OpenClaw embeds runtime in a gateway, and Hermes uses role-based process entry points. Context and extension layers vary as well: Claude extends context windows, OpenClaw registers global capabilities, and Hermes offers pluggable memory and model backends.
