The paper concludes with six open design directions for future agent systems, informed by empirical, architectural, and policy research.
为理解当前AI代理系统如何在人类控制、安全与适应性间取得平衡，本研究通过分析Claude Code源代码并对比OpenClaw与Hermes Agent，揭示其架构设计。研究识别出五大核心设计动机——人类决策权、安全、隐私、可靠执行、能力增强与上下文适应性，并将其通过十三项设计原则映射到具体实现。系统核心是一个简单while循环，反复调用模型并执行工具，而大部分复杂性体现在其周边系统：七模式权限系统、五层上下文压缩管道、四种扩展机制（MCP、插件、技能、钩子）、子代理调度机制及追加式会话存储。对比实验显示不同系统对相同设计问题采取不同策略：Claude Code强调每步操作安全，OpenClaw侧重边界访问控制，Hermes则支持多界面审批。运行时架构亦不同：Claude采用CLI循环，OpenClaw将运行时嵌入网关控制平面，Hermes则依赖入口点角色设定。在上下文与扩展层，Claude扩展上下文窗口，OpenClaw注册全局能力，Hermes提供可插拔的记忆与模型后端。最后，研究提出六个面向未来代理系统的开放设计方向，基于近期实证、架构与政策文献。
Optimal Stabilizer Testing and Learning with Limited Quantum Memory
Authors: Srinivasan Arunachalam, Louis Schatzki
First: 2026-07-02T17:11:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:11:38+00:00
Comments: 66 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study stabilizer state testing and learning with limited coherent quantum memory. Here an algorithm sequentially receives copies of an unknown $n$-qubit state, but may keep only $k$ qubits of coherent quantum memory between measurements. With unrestricted memory, seminal work of Gross, Nezami and Walter showed how to test $n$-qubit stabilizer states using $6$ copies, which is dimension independent, unlike the learning complexity of $Θ(n)$. We show that this testing-vs-learning separation is lost under memory constraints. More concretely we show that (1) The sample complexity of testing stabilizer states in the $k$-qubit memory framework is $Θ(n-k)$. Our upper bound goes via a novel connection to the hidden shift problem and the lower bound is proven using a novel approach to average case bounds on likelihood ratios via combinatorics of the stochastic orthogonal group. (2) The sample complexity of learning stabilizer states with $k$ qubits of memory, in the non-adaptive framework, is $Θ(n^2/k)$. As a further application of our techniques, we prove an exponential lower bound for purity testing even when the memory may be left coherent throughout the protocol. Our main results identify coherent quantum memory as the resource enabling the usual separation between stabilizer testing and learning. In particular, even with $k=0.99n$ qubits of memory, there is no constant-copy stabilizer tester; furthermore for $k=cn$ qubits of memory (for $0< c < 1$), stabilizer testing is as hard as learning, with both requiring $Θ(n)$ copies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：有限量子内存下的最优稳定子态测试与学习
我们研究在有限相干量子内存约束下稳定子态的测试与学习问题。在此框架中，算法依次接收未知n量子比特态的副本，但每次测量间最多仅能保持k个量子比特的相干量子内存。在无内存限制情况下，Gross、Nezami和Walter的经典工作表明，使用6个副本即可测试n量子比特稳定子态，该复杂度与维度无关，而学习复杂度为Θ(n)。我们证明，在内存受限条件下，这种测试与学习的分离性消失。具体而言，我们证明：(1) 在k量子比特内存框架下，测试稳定子态的样本复杂度为Θ(n-k)。上界通过与隐藏移位问题的新联系获得，下界则通过随机正交群组合学方法证明了似然比的平均情况界。(2) 在非自适应框架下，使用k量子比特内存学习稳定子态的样本复杂度为Θ(n²/k)。作为技术应用，我们证明即使协议全程保持内存相干，纯度测试仍需指数级下界。我们的主要结果表明，相干量子内存是实现稳定子测试与学习通常分离性的关键资源。特别地，即使拥有k=0.99n量子比特内存，也不存在常数副本的稳定子测试器；对于k=cn量子比特内存（0<c<1），稳定子测试与学习难度相当，均需Θ(n)个副本。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the question of how quantum memory constraints affect the complexity of testing versus learning stabilizer states, this work investigates the sample complexity under limited coherent quantum memory. The authors show that with only k qubits of memory, testing stabilizer states requires Θ(n−k) samples, while learning them non-adaptively requires Θ(n²/k) samples. These results demonstrate that the well-known separation between testing and learning—where testing can be done with constant samples and learning requires Θ(n)—disappears under memory constraints. The testing upper bound leverages a novel connection to the hidden shift problem, while the lower bound uses combinatorial methods on the stochastic orthogonal group. Additionally, the techniques yield an exponential lower bound for purity testing even with coherent memory preserved. Crucially, even with 0.99n qubits of memory, no constant-copy stabilizer tester exists, and for any c<1, testing and learning require the same Θ(n) samples.
该研究旨在探讨有限相干量子内存对稳定子态测试与学习复杂度的影响。作者证明，在仅能保留k个量子比特内存的情况下，稳定子态测试需要Θ(n−k)个样本，而非适应性学习则需要Θ(n²/k)个样本。这些结果表明，内存限制消除了测试与学习之间的传统分离——当内存充足但未完全满载（如k=cn，0<c<1）时，测试与学习的复杂度均上升至Θ(n)。测试下界通过随机正交群的组合方法证明，上界则利用了与隐藏移位问题的新关联。此外，研究还证明即使内存全程保持相干，纯度测试仍存在指数级下界，进一步确认了相干量子内存是实现测试与学习分离的关键资源。
EvoPolicyGym: Evaluating Autonomous Policy Evolution in Interactive Environments
Authors: Zhilin Wang, Han Song, Runzhe Zhan, Jusen Du, Jiacheng Chen, Tianle Li, Qingyu Yin, Yulun Wu, Zhennan Shen, Tong Zhu, Yanshu Li, Guanjie Chen, Derek F. Wong, Yafu Li, Yu Cheng, Yang Yang
First: 2026-07-02T17:10:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:10:13+00:00
Comments: 24 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous agents are increasingly expected to improve executable policies through feedback, yet existing evaluations often collapse this process into a final score or confound it with open-ended software-engineering progress. We introduce Autonomous Policy Evolution, a controlled evaluation setting in which a harness-model agent repeatedly edits an executable policy system under a fixed interaction budget. We instantiate this setting in EvoPolicyGym, a benchmark built from compact interactive RL environments that evaluates how agents iteratively improve explored policies. On the EvoPolicyGym suite, GPT-5.5 achieves the strongest aggregate rank score and top-two performance on all 16 environments. Beyond leaderboard results, EvoPolicyGym also provides trajectory-level diagnostics that distinguish how agents allocate budget, convert feedback into parametric tuning. These analyses show that strong autonomous policy evolution depends not only on isolated task wins, but on discovering task-appropriate mechanisms and refining policies under bounded feedback.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EvoPolicyGym：在交互环境中评估自主策略演化
自主代理日益被期望通过反馈改进可执行策略，但现有评估常将其过程简化为最终得分，或与开放式的软件工程进展混淆。我们引入自主策略演化，这是一种受控评估设置，其中承载模型代理在固定交互预算下反复编辑可执行策略系统。我们通过EvoPolicyGym基准实现该设置，该基准由紧凑的交互式强化学习环境构建，用于评估代理如何迭代改进探索到的策略。在EvoPolicyGym套件中，GPT-5.5获得最强综合排名得分，并在全部16个环境中均取得前两名表现。除排行榜结果外，EvoPolicyGym还提供轨迹级诊断，可区分代理如何分配预算、将反馈转化为参数调优。这些分析表明，强大的自主策略演化不仅依赖于孤立任务的胜利，更依赖于发现任务适配机制并在有限反馈下优化策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate how autonomous agents iteratively improve policies through feedback without conflating progress with final scores, the paper introduces EvoPolicyGym, a benchmark that enforces a fixed interaction budget and requires agents to repeatedly edit executable policies. Evaluated across 16 compact interactive RL environments, GPT-5.5 achieved the highest aggregate rank and top-two performance in all environments, demonstrating strong autonomous policy evolution. Trajectory-level diagnostics further revealed that success hinges not just on task completion but on discovering appropriate mechanisms and refining policies under constrained feedback.
为解决现有评估方法将政策改进过程简化为最终得分或与软件工程进展混淆的问题，本文提出EvoPolicyGym基准，通过固定交互预算强制代理反复编辑可执行策略系统，以评估其迭代优化能力。方法通过轨迹级诊断分析代理如何分配预算及将反馈转化为参数调整。实验结果显示，GPT-5.5在16个环境中综合排名最高，且在所有环境中均位列前二，表明卓越的自主策略演化不仅依赖任务成功，更取决于发现任务适配机制并在有限反馈下持续优化策略。
BLAgent: Agentic RAG for File-Level Bug Localization
Authors: Md Afif Al Mamun, Gias Uddin
First: 2026-05-18T07:20:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:09:54+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication in ACM Transactions on Software Engineering and Methodology (TOSEM)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bug localization remains a key bottleneck for large language model (LLM)-based software maintenance, where accurately identifying faulty code is essential for debugging, root cause analysis, triage, and automated program repair (APR). File-level bug localization is especially critical in hierarchical localization and repair pipelines, where incorrect file selection can propagate to downstream stages such as function-level localization and patch generation. While Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) offers a promising way to ground LLMs in repository context, existing RAG pipelines often rely on static retrieval and lack the reasoning needed to accurately identify faulty code. In this work, we present BLAgent, a novel agentic RAG framework for file-level bug localization that integrates three key ideas: (i) code structure-aware repository encoding with path-augmented AST-based chunking, (ii) dual-perspective query transformation that captures both structural and behavioral signals from bug reports, and (iii) two-phase agentic reranking that combines symbolic inspection with evidence-grounded reasoning. Unlike prior graph-based or multi-hop agentic approaches, BLAgent adopts a bounded reasoning strategy that limits LLM-based inspection and reranking to a compact, retrieval-filtered set of candidate files, avoiding open-ended repository traversal. This design balances localization accuracy with computational cost. On SWE-bench-Lite, BLAgent attains over 78% Top-1 accuracy with open-source models and over 86% with a closed-source model, while being over 18x cheaper than the strongest baseline using the same model. When integrated into an APR framework, BLAgent improves end-to-end repair success by up to 25%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BLAgent：面向文件级缺陷定位的代理式RAG框架
缺陷定位仍是基于大语言模型（LLM）的软件维护中的关键瓶颈，准确识别故障代码对调试、根因分析、问题分类和自动化程序修复（APR）至关重要。在分层定位与修复流程中，文件级缺陷定位尤为关键，错误的文件选择会传播至下游阶段，如函数级定位和补丁生成。尽管检索增强生成（RAG）为将LLM锚定在仓库上下文提供了有前景的方案，但现有RAG流程常依赖静态检索，缺乏准确识别故障代码所需的推理能力。本文提出BLAgent，一种面向文件级缺陷定位的新型代理式RAG框架，融合三大核心思想：（i）路径增强的AST基块划分的代码结构感知仓库编码；（ii）双视角查询变换，捕捉缺陷报告中的结构与行为信号；（iii）两阶段代理重排序，结合符号检查与证据锚定推理。与先前基于图或多跳代理方法不同，BLAgent采用有界推理策略，将LLM的检查与重排序限制在紧凑的、检索过滤后的候选文件集合内，避免无限制的仓库遍历。该设计在定位精度与计算成本间取得平衡。在SWE-bench-Lite上，BLAgent使用开源模型时Top-1准确率超78%，使用闭源模型时超86%，且成本比最强基线低18倍以上。当集成至APR框架时，BLAgent可提升端到端修复成功率最高达25%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to accurately localize bugs at the file level for efficient software maintenance, BLAgent introduces an agentic RAG framework that enhances LLM-based bug localization by integrating code structure-aware chunking, dual-perspective query transformation, and two-phase agentic reranking. The method encodes repositories using path-augmented AST chunks, transforms queries to capture structural and behavioral signals, and applies bounded reasoning over retrieval-filtered candidate files to avoid costly open-ended traversal. Experiments on SWE-bench-Lite show BLAgent achieves over 78% Top-1 accuracy with open-source models and over 86% with closed-source models, while being 18x cheaper than the best baseline. When integrated into an APR pipeline, it boosts end-to-end repair success by up to 25%.
为提升大型语言模型在软件维护中对文件级缺陷的定位精度，BLAgent提出一种代理式RAG框架。该框架结合路径增强的AST分块编码、双视角查询转换（捕捉结构与行为信号）及两阶段代理重排序（符号检查与证据推理结合），通过限制LLM仅在检索过滤后的候选文件集上进行推理，避免全库遍历。在SWE-bench-Lite数据集上，其使用开源模型时Top-1准确率超78%，闭源模型超86%，成本仅为最优基线的1/18。集成至自动修复框架后，修复成功率提升最高达25%。
Extreme Adaptive Transformer for Time Series Forecasting
Authors: Sanjeev Shrestha, Hui Liu, Yifan Zhang
First: 2026-07-02T17:09:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:09:14+00:00
Comments: Submitted to Scientific Reports
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Time series forecasting remains challenging when the underlying data contain rare but critical extreme events. This issue is particularly important in hydrologic forecasting, where streamflow distributions are often highly skewed and extreme peaks can have substantial impacts on flood monitoring, water resource management, and early warning systems. Although Transformer-based forecasting models have achieved strong performance by modeling long-range temporal dependencies, they typically treat all time points uniformly and may therefore underrepresent rare extreme patterns. In this paper, we propose the Extreme-Adaptive Transformer (Exformer), a forecasting framework designed to explicitly model temporal dependencies involving both normal and extreme events. Exformer introduces an extreme-adaptive attention mechanism composed of three sparse components: Local, Stride, and Extreme. The Local and Stride components capture short-term and periodic temporal dependencies, respectively, while the Extreme component selectively models event-aware dependencies between normal and extreme streamflow patterns. Experiments on four real-world hydrologic streamflow datasets show that Exformer achieves superior 3-day forecasting performance compared with state-of-the-art baselines. Our findings demonstrate that explicitly incorporating extreme-aware attention improves the forecasting capacity of Transformer models on imbalanced time series with rare but consequential events.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于时间序列预测的极值自适应变换器
当底层数据包含罕见但关键的极端事件时，时间序列预测仍具挑战性。这一问题在水文预测中尤为重要，因为径流分布常高度偏斜，极端峰值对洪水监测、水资源管理和预警系统有重大影响。尽管基于Transformer的预测模型通过建模长程时序依赖关系取得了优异性能，但它们通常对所有时间点一视同仁，可能低估罕见极端模式。本文提出极值自适应变换器（Exformer），一种旨在显式建模正常与极端事件间时序依赖关系的预测框架。Exformer引入由局部、步进和极值三个稀疏组件构成的极值自适应注意力机制：局部组件捕捉短期时序依赖，步进组件捕捉周期性依赖，极值组件则选择性建模正常与极端径流模式间的事件感知依赖。在四个真实水文径流数据集上的实验表明，Exformer在3日预测任务上优于当前最先进基线模型。我们的研究结果表明，显式引入极值感知注意力可提升Transformer模型在包含罕见但重要事件的不平衡时间序列上的预测能力。

Motivated by the challenge of accurately forecasting rare but impactful extreme events in skewed hydrologic time series, this paper introduces the Extreme-Adaptive Transformer (Exformer), a framework that enhances standard Transformers by incorporating an extreme-adaptive attention mechanism with three sparse components: Local, Stride, and Extreme. The Local and Stride components capture short-term and periodic dependencies, while the Extreme component selectively models interactions between normal and extreme streamflow patterns. Experiments on four real-world hydrologic datasets demonstrate that Exformer outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in 3-day-ahead forecasting, confirming that explicit modeling of extreme events improves forecasting accuracy in imbalanced time series.
为解决在偏斜水文时序数据中罕见但关键的极端事件难以准确预测的问题，本文提出极端自适应变换器（Exformer），通过引入包含局部、步进和极端三个稀疏组件的极端自适应注意力机制，增强标准Transformer对正常与极端事件间依赖关系的建模能力。实验在四个真实水文数据集上表明，Exformer在3日预测任务中显著优于现有最先进模型，证明显式建模极端事件可提升Transformer在存在罕见但重要事件的不平衡时序数据上的预测能力。
Optimizing RAG Rerankers with LLM Feedback via Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Yuhang Wu, Xiangqing Shen, Fanfan Wang, Cangqi Zhou, Zhen Wu, Xinyu Dai, Rui Xia
First: 2026-04-02T14:19:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:08:51+00:00
Comments: 17 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Rerankers play a pivotal role in refining retrieval results for Retrieval-Augmented Generation. However, current reranking models are typically optimized on static human annotated relevance labels in isolation, decoupled from the downstream generation process. This isolation leads to a fundamental misalignment: documents identified as topically relevant by information retrieval metrics often fail to provide the actual utility required by the LLM for precise answer generation. To bridge this gap, we introduce ReRanking Preference Optimization (RRPO), a reinforcement learning framework that directly aligns reranking with the LLM's generation quality. By formulating reranking as a sequential decision-making process, RRPO optimizes for context utility using LLM feedback, thereby eliminating the need for expensive human annotations. To ensure training stability, we further introduce a reference-anchored deterministic baseline. Extensive experiments on knowledge-intensive benchmarks demonstrate that RRPO significantly outperforms strong baselines, including the powerful list-wise reranker RankZephyr. Further analysis highlights the versatility of our framework: it generalizes seamlessly to diverse readers (e.g., GPT-4o), integrates orthogonally with query expansion modules like Query2Doc, and remains robust even when trained with noisy supervisors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过强化学习利用大语言模型反馈优化RAG重排序器
重排序器在检索增强生成的检索结果精炼中起着关键作用。然而，当前的重排序模型通常在静态人工标注的相关性标签上独立优化，与下游生成过程脱节。这种脱节导致根本性错位：信息检索指标识别为主题相关的文档，往往无法满足大语言模型生成精确答案所需的实际效用。为弥合这一差距，我们提出ReRanking Preference Optimization（RRPO），一种强化学习框架，直接将重排序与大语言模型的生成质量对齐。通过将重排序建模为序列决策过程，RRPO利用大语言模型反馈优化上下文效用，从而无需昂贵的人工标注。为确保训练稳定性，我们进一步引入参考锚定确定性基线。在知识密集型基准上的大量实验表明，RRPO显著优于强基线，包括强大的列表式重排序器RankZephyr。进一步分析凸显了我们框架的通用性：它可无缝扩展至多样化的读者（如GPT-4o），可与查询扩展模块（如Query2Doc）正交集成，并在使用噪声监督者训练时仍保持鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the misalignment between traditional reranking models optimized on static human relevance labels and the actual utility needed for LLM generation, this work introduces RRPO, a reinforcement learning framework that directly aligns reranking with generation quality using LLM feedback. By treating reranking as a sequential decision process and employing a reference-anchored deterministic baseline for training stability, RRPO eliminates reliance on costly human annotations. Experimental results on knowledge-intensive benchmarks show RRPO significantly outperforms strong baselines like RankZephyr, and further analysis confirms its robustness across diverse LLMs, compatibility with query expansion modules, and resilience to noisy supervision.
为解决传统重排序模型与大语言模型生成需求之间的错位问题，本文提出RRPO框架，通过强化学习直接以大语言模型生成质量反馈优化重排序，提升上下文实用性。该方法将重排序建模为序列决策过程，以LLM生成质量作为奖励信号，并引入参考锚定确定性基线保证训练稳定。在知识密集型基准测试中，RRPO显著优于RankZephyr等强基线模型，且在不同LLM、查询扩展模块及噪声监督下均表现稳健。
Reasoning effort, not tool access, buys first-try reliability in agentic code generation: an observational study
Authors: Achint Mehta
First: 2026-07-02T17:08:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:08:21+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 5 figures, 10 tables. Dataset and evaluation artifacts: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21134406
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic coding assistants are increasingly given extra capabilities, such as browser based testing tools and design oriented system prompts, on the assumption that more capability yields better software. This study tested that assumption directly. Ninety independent agent runs built the same application, a real time retrospective board, from one detailed specification, each scored on a fixed 14 criterion functional rubric (42 point maximum) and a visual quality review. The runs spanned several model generations, two agent harnesses, two reasoning effort levels, a testing tool, and two design oriented prompts. Capability tier dominated: frontier models clustered near the ceiling while a low cost local model fell to 24 to 37 points. A criterion level analysis revealed what run totals conceal. Container deployment was the dominant defect, failing first try in 44 percent of runs, with its failure rate shifting sharply across model generations while mean totals moved less than a point. The testing tool raised cost by 42 to 68 percent without improving functional score or reliability, even on interface visible criteria. Raising reasoning effort from High to xHigh lifted first try perfect runs from 28 percent to 89 percent and cut corrective prompts about five fold, for 9 to 29 percent more cost. A design oriented prompt raised visual quality, 4.5 versus 3.0 on a 5 point scale, without lifting function, and a one paragraph paraphrase of its directive reproduced the entire lift. The practical lesson is to match the fix to the failure: most first run failures came from weak reasoning, which a stronger model or more effort prevents, not from visible flaws a checking tool would catch.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在代理代码生成中，推理努力而非工具访问能带来首次尝试的可靠性：一项观察性研究
随着代理编码助手被赋予更多能力（如基于浏览器的测试工具和面向设计的系统提示），人们普遍假设能力越强软件质量越好。本研究直接检验了这一假设。90次独立代理运行基于同一详细规格构建了同一个实时回顾板应用，每项运行按固定14项功能评分标准（满分42分）和视觉质量评审打分。运行覆盖多个模型版本、两种代理框架、两种推理努力水平、一个测试工具和两种面向设计的提示。能力层级主导结果：前沿模型得分接近上限，而低成本本地模型得分仅24至37分。按评分标准的分析揭示了总分掩盖的细节：容器部署是主要缺陷，首次尝试失败率达44%，其失败率随模型版本变化显著，而平均总分仅变化不到1分。测试工具使成本上升42%至68%，但未提升功能得分或可靠性，即使在界面可见标准上亦然。将推理努力从高提升至x高，首次尝试完美运行比例从28%升至89%，纠正提示减少五倍，成本增加9%至29%。面向设计的提示提升了视觉质量（5分制下从3.0升至4.5），但未提升功能，且其指令的一段改写即可复现全部提升效果。实用启示是：针对失败采取针对性修复——多数首次运行失败源于推理能力不足，可通过更强模型或更多努力解决，而非由检查工具能发现的可见缺陷。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether enhanced capabilities like testing tools or design prompts improve first-try reliability in agentic code generation, challenging the assumption that more tools equal better outcomes. Using 90 independent agent runs to build a real-time retrospective board from a single specification, researchers evaluated performance across model generations, agent frameworks, reasoning effort levels, and prompts, scoring outputs on a 42-point functional rubric and visual quality. Results show that capability tier dominates performance: frontier models achieved near-perfect scores while a low-cost local model scored only 24–37 points. A deeper analysis revealed container deployment as the primary failure mode, failing 44% of the time and shifting sharply across models, while overall scores changed minimally. The testing tool increased cost without improving reliability or function, while increasing reasoning effort from High to xHigh boosted perfect first-run completion from 28% to 89% and reduced corrective prompts by fivefold, at a modest cost increase. A design prompt improved visual quality but not function, and its effect was replicated by a simple paraphrase. The key takeaway is that most failures stem from weak reasoning, which stronger models or increased effort resolve, not from visible flaws that tools detect.
本研究检验了增强能力（如测试工具或设计提示）是否能提升代理代码生成的可靠性，发现这些工具效果有限，而提升推理强度则显著提高首次成功率。在90次构建实时回顾板的代理运行中，前沿模型得分接近满分（42/42），而低成本本地模型仅得24–37分。详细分析显示，容器部署失败占44%，模型版本的影响远大于总分变化。测试工具虽增加成本，却未提升功能或可靠性；提升推理强度从高到x高，首次完美成功率从28%升至89%，纠错提示减少五倍，成本仅小幅增加。设计提示提升视觉质量（4.5分 vs 3.0分），但不影响功能，且其效果可通过重述指令复现。核心结论是：多数失败源于推理薄弱，应通过更强模型或更多推理资源解决，而非依赖工具修复可见缺陷。
Evaluating Collective Behaviour of Hundreds of LLM Agents
Authors: Richard Willis, Jianing Zhao, Yali Du, Joel Z. Leibo
First: 2026-02-18T18:02:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:08:21+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-powered AI assistants acting on behalf of users can produce poor collective outcomes at scale. We introduce a framework for evaluating their emergent behaviour in social dilemmas, applied to three iterated games (Public Goods, Collective Risk, Common Pool Resource). We prompt each model to produce a natural-language strategy, then have the same model translate it into code. This aims to isolate strategic reasoning from input-parsing, enables pre-deployment inspection, and scales to populations of hundreds of agents. We propose three analyses: behavioural fingerprinting via exhaustive evaluation over opponent histories; self-play robustness across mixtures of a model's strategies with either a Selfish or Collective disposition; and cultural evolution under payoff-biased imitation. Applied to three state-of-the-art LLMs, we find substantial cross-model differences in self-play welfare, and that cultural evolution converges to low-welfare, Selfish-dominant equilibria in larger groups.
中文标题/摘要
标题：评估数百个大语言模型代理的集体行为
由大语言模型驱动的AI助手在大规模运行时可能产生较差的集体结果。我们提出一个框架，用于评估其在社会困境中的涌现行为，应用于三类重复博弈（公共物品、集体风险、公共资源）。我们提示每个模型生成自然语言策略，再由同一模型将其翻译为代码，旨在将策略推理与输入解析分离，支持部署前检查，并可扩展至数百个代理的群体。我们提出三项分析：通过穷举对手历史进行行为指纹识别；在模型策略与自私或集体倾向混合下的自对弈稳健性；以及在收益偏倚模仿下的文化演化。对三类最先进的大语言模型应用后，我们发现模型间自对弈福利存在显著差异，且在更大群体中文化演化收敛于低福利、以自私为主导的均衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk of poor collective outcomes when deploying many LLM-powered agents, this study introduces a framework to evaluate emergent behavior in social dilemmas using three iterated games. It prompts each model to generate a natural-language strategy, then translates it into executable code to isolate strategic reasoning from parsing artifacts, enabling pre-deployment inspection and scalability to hundreds of agents. Three analyses—behavioral fingerprinting, self-play robustness against selfish/collective mixtures, and cultural evolution via payoff-biased imitation—are applied to three state-of-the-art LLMs, revealing significant cross-model differences in self-play welfare and convergence to low-welfare, selfish-dominant equilibria in larger groups.
鉴于大规模部署LLM代理可能导致集体结果不佳，本研究提出一套评估框架，用于在三个迭代博弈（公共品、集体风险、公共资源）中考察其在社会困境中的涌现行为。每个LLM被提示生成自然语言策略，再将其转化为可执行代码，以分离策略推理与输入解析，支持部署前检查并扩展至数百个代理。通过三种分析——基于对手历史的全面行为指纹识别、在自博弈中对自私与集体策略混合的鲁棒性测试、以及基于收益偏好的文化演化——应用于三类前沿LLM，结果发现模型间自博弈福利差异显著，且在群体规模增大时文化演化趋向于低福利、自私主导的均衡。
PE-means: Improved Differentially Private $k$-means Clustering through Private Evolution
Authors: Thomas Humphries, Zinan Lin, Sergey Yekhanin
First: 2026-05-29T20:30:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:06:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the problem of differentially private (DP) $k$-means clustering in Euclidean space. Previous solutions rely on summing the private data directly, which induces a sensitivity proportional to the domain. We introduce PE-means, an extension of the private evolution (PE) algorithm (an increasingly popular method for synthetic data generation), to the problem of $k$-means clustering. The key advantage of PE is that it only computes a private histogram with constant sensitivity to guide the evolution. Our adaptation of PE includes new evolutionary operators for clustering, as well as other algorithmic improvements of independent interest. Overall, PE-means achieves an average improvement of 26% in clustering loss over state-of-the-art baselines such as Google's LSH-based algorithm and DP-Lloyd variants.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PE-means：通过私有演化改进差分隐私k均值聚类
我们研究欧几里得空间中差分隐私（DP）k均值聚类问题。先前的解决方案依赖直接对私有数据求和，导致敏感度与域成正比。我们引入PE-means，将私有演化（PE）算法（一种日益流行的合成数据生成方法）扩展至k均值聚类问题。PE的核心优势在于仅计算具有常数敏感度的私有直方图以指导演化。我们的PE改进版包含新的聚类演化算子及其他独立的算法改进。总体而言，PE-means在聚类损失上平均优于谷歌基于LSH的算法及DP-Lloyd变体等现有最先进基线方法26%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high sensitivity of existing differentially private k-means methods that scale with domain size, this work introduces PE-means, an adaptation of the private evolution (PE) algorithm to clustering. PE-means leverages a private histogram with constant sensitivity to guide the evolution of cluster centers, avoiding direct summation of private data. It incorporates novel evolutionary operators and algorithmic refinements tailored for clustering. Experimental results show PE-means achieves an average 26% reduction in clustering loss compared to state-of-the-art methods including Google’s LSH-based algorithm and DP-Lloyd variants.
为解决现有差分隐私k均值聚类方法因直接求和导致的敏感度随域规模增长的问题，本文提出PE-means算法，将私有演化（PE）算法扩展至聚类任务。该方法通过计算具有常数敏感度的私有直方图引导聚类中心演化，避免直接处理私有数据。算法引入新的演化算子和独立改进。实验表明，PE-means相较谷歌LSH算法及DP-Lloyd等当前最优方法，平均聚类损失降低26%。
MARVEL: Margin-Aware Robust von Mises-Fischer Expert Learning for Long-Tailed Out-of-Distribution Detection
Authors: A. S. Anudeep, Vaanathi Sundaresan
First: 2026-07-02T17:06:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:06:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
For clinical deployment, it is essential that automated diagnostic systems remain reliable when confronted with previously unseen cases, yet deep models routinely misclassify out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs with high confidence, underscoring the need for more robust OOD detection methods. Although substantial effort has been devoted to improving model robustness, most of the existing literature assumes balanced datasets, evaluates OOD detection on coarse or non-clinical OOD sources, or lacks comprehensive assessment across diverse OOD scenarios. To address the gaps, we propose a novel methodology trained on diverse and imbalanced medical datasets and evaluated across a clinically reflective OOD spectrum. Our framework comprises three key components: (1) a Nonlinear von Mises-Fisher (NvMF) classifier capable of learning non-linear decision boundaries, with theoretical proof of its asymptotic connection to cosine classifiers; (2) a multi-expert framework in which margin-aware NvMF classifiers specialise in different regions of label distribution to better handle imbalance; and (3) an outlier expert trained explicitly to distinguish inlier from outlier data, thereby strengthening OOD detection. Evaluation on RFMiD, ISIC2019, and NCTCRC datasets demonstrates consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods, achieving mean FPR95 reductions of 8.45%, 13.02%, and 36.90% respectively. These gains are further supported by comprehensive ablations that validated the contributions of each component. This enables reliable identification of unfamiliar cases for deferral to clinicians, supporting safer AI-assisted diagnosis in real-world workflows. Our code is available at https://github.com/redboxup/MARVEL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MARVEL：面向长尾分布外检测的边际感知鲁棒冯·米塞斯-费舍尔专家学习
为实现临床部署，自动化诊断系统在面对前所未见病例时必须保持可靠性，但深度模型常以高置信度错误分类分布外（OOD）输入，凸显了对更鲁棒的OOD检测方法的需求。尽管已有大量研究致力于提升模型鲁棒性，但多数现有文献假设数据集平衡、在粗略或非临床的OOD源上评估OOD检测，或缺乏在多样OOD场景下的全面评估。为弥补这些不足，我们提出一种在多样且不平衡的医学数据集上训练、并在临床相关OOD谱上评估的新方法。该框架包含三个关键组件：（1）非线性冯·米塞斯-费舍尔（NvMF）分类器，可学习非线性决策边界，并有理论证明其渐近连接于余弦分类器；（2）多专家框架，其中边际感知NvMF分类器在标签分布的不同区域专业化，以更好地处理不平衡问题；（3）专门训练的异常值专家，用于区分内点与异常点，从而增强OOD检测能力。在RFMiD、ISIC2019和NCTCRC数据集上的评估显示，该方法相较现有最先进方法持续提升，分别实现平均FPR95降低8.45%、13.02%和36.90%。全面消融实验进一步验证了各组件的贡献。这使得系统能可靠识别陌生病例并转交临床医生，支持真实世界工作流中更安全的AI辅助诊断。代码开源于https://github.com/redboxup/MARVEL。
Summary / 总结
To enhance the reliability of automated diagnostic systems in clinical settings when encountering unseen cases, the paper proposes MARVEL, a margin-aware robust OOD detection framework trained on imbalanced medical datasets and evaluated across clinically relevant out-of-distribution scenarios. The method combines a nonlinear von Mises-Fisher classifier with theoretical grounding in cosine classifiers, a multi-expert architecture that assigns margin-aware NvMF classifiers to specialize in different regions of label distribution to handle imbalance, and an outlier expert explicitly trained to distinguish inliers from outliers. Evaluated on RFMiD, ISIC2019, and NCTCRC, MARVEL consistently outperforms existing methods, reducing mean FPR95 by 8.45%, 13.02%, and 36.90% respectively, with ablation studies confirming the individual contributions of each component.
为提升自动化诊断系统在临床环境中面对未知病例时的可靠性，本文提出MARVEL框架，结合具有理论保证的非线性von Mises-Fisher分类器、针对标签分布不同区域进行专业化分工的多专家架构，以及专门训练用于区分内点与异常点的异常专家。在RFMiD、ISIC2019和NCTCRC三个医学数据集上，使用临床相关的OOD场景进行评估，MARVEL在FPR95指标上分别降低8.45%、13.02%和36.90%，且消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。
Automated grading of Linux/bash examinations using large language models: a four-level cognitive taxonomy approach
Authors: Manuel Alonso-Carracedo, Ruben Fernandez-Boullon, Pedro Celard, Francisco J. Rodriguez-Martinez, Lorena Otero-Cerdeira
First: 2026-07-02T17:01:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:01:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scalable and reliable grading of command-line examinations remains a challenge in computing education, where rising enrolments make manual marking difficult and rule-based autograders cannot handle partial credit, equivalent solutions, or syntactic variation. This paper evaluates whether four frontier Large Language Models (GPT, Claude Opus, Gemini, and GLM) can approximate expert judgment when grading short Linux/bash command responses. The study adopts a four-level cognitive taxonomy that combines cognitive complexity and operational impact, ranging from information retrieval (L1) and basic file manipulation (L2) to structural operations (L3) and advanced system management (L4). The models were tested with two prompt variants, a minimal baseline and a rubric-enhanced version, on 1200 real responses from second-year Computer Engineering students independently graded by three expert instructors. Gemini~3.0 Pro with rubric-guided prompting achieved the highest human-AI agreement (ICC(3,1) = 0.888, MAE = 0.10, Bland-Altman bias = -0.014). Agreement declined consistently as taxonomy level increased, with the largest discrepancies at higher levels. Across all models, rubric quality had a larger effect than provider choice, with structured prompts consistently improving agreement. These results show that question complexity is a reliable predictor of the difficulty LLMs face in grading accurately, and they establish a principled, taxonomy-based framework for determining which questions are suitable for AI-assisted grading and which require human review, while also providing a transferable evaluation protocol and prompt templates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用大型语言模型对Linux/bash考试进行自动化评分：一种四层级认知分类法方法
在计算机教育中，命令行考试的可扩展且可靠的评分仍具挑战性，因学生人数激增使人工批改困难，基于规则的自动评分器无法处理部分得分、等效解法或语法变体。本文评估了四种前沿大型语言模型（GPT、Claude Opus、Gemini 和 GLM）在评分简短Linux/bash命令响应时是否能近似专家判断。研究采用结合认知复杂度与操作影响的四层级认知分类法，从信息检索（L1）和基本文件操作（L2）到结构化操作（L3）和高级系统管理（L4）。模型在1200份由三位专家教师独立评分的真实学生答卷上，测试了两种提示变体（最小基线与评分标准增强版）。Gemini 3.0 Pro配合评分标准引导提示达成最高的人机一致性（ICC(3,1) = 0.888，MAE = 0.10，Bland-Altman偏差 = -0.014）。一致性随分类层级升高而持续下降，高层级差异最大。所有模型中，评分标准质量的影响大于提供商选择，结构化提示始终提升一致性。结果表明，题目复杂度是预测LLM评分难度的可靠指标，同时建立了基于分类法的框架，用于确定哪些题目适合AI辅助评分、哪些需人工复核，并提供可迁移的评估协议与提示模板。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable and reliable grading of Linux/bash exams amid growing student numbers and limitations of rule-based systems, this study evaluates four large language models—GPT, Claude Opus, Gemini, and GLM—using a four-level cognitive taxonomy that measures both cognitive complexity and operational impact. The models were tested with two prompt variants on 1200 real student responses, independently graded by three experts. Results show that Gemini 3.0 Pro with rubric-enhanced prompts achieved the highest agreement with human graders (ICC=0.888, MAE=0.10), with agreement declining as task complexity increased. Rubric quality consistently outperformed model provider choice, demonstrating that question complexity is a key predictor of grading difficulty and establishing a taxonomy-based framework for determining when AI grading is appropriate.
为应对学生规模扩大导致的命令行考试评分难、规则系统无法处理部分得分和等效解等问题，本研究评估了GPT、Claude Opus、Gemini和GLM四个大语言模型，采用四层认知分类法衡量认知复杂度与操作影响。在1200份真实学生答案上测试两种提示变体，由三位专家独立评分。Gemini 3.0 Pro配合评分标准提示表现最佳（ICC=0.888，MAE=0.10），但高阶题目评分一致性下降。评分标准质量优于模型提供商选择，表明题目复杂度是影响AI评分准确性的关键因素，并建立了基于分类法的AI辅助评分适用性框架。
WorldSample: Closed-loop Real-robot RL with World Modelling
Authors: Yuquan Xue, Le Xu, Zeyi Liu, Zhenyu Wu, Zhengyi Gu, Xinyang Song, Bofang Jia, Ziwei Wang
First: 2026-07-02T17:00:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T17:00:37+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 9 figures, conference paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) can overcome the demonstration-coverage limitation of imitation learning (IL) by allowing robots to improve through trial-and-error interaction beyond the states observed in demonstrations. However, deploying RL on real robots remains constrained by high interaction costs, since each physical rollout is costly and reflects only one realized action-outcome path. To address this challenge, we propose WorldSample, a physically grounded data augmentation framework for real-robot RL that closes a real-synthetic loop between physical rollouts, world-model generation, and policy improvement. Grounded on real rollouts, WorldSample generates high-fidelity synthetic transitions through a post-trained world model, which greatly lowers the visual hallucination. Specifically, rather than simply using these transitions as real-world experience, WorldSample introduces Policy-Paced Learning (PPL) to regulate the training process through sample selection and scheduling, balancing useful augmentation against value overestimation and mitigating the hallucination-induced noise. Experiments on robot manipulation tasks involving contact-rich and precise tasks show that WorldSample improves policy success rate by 28% while reducing training steps by 59% compared with baselines. Furthermore, WorldSample improves world model visual fidelity by 19.4dB in PSNR and 0.47 in SSIM over demonstration-only post-training, validating the effectiveness of the real-synthetic loop for both policy and world model performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WorldSample：基于世界建模的闭环真实机器人强化学习
强化学习（RL）可通过试错交互使机器人在演示中未覆盖的状态下持续改进，从而克服模仿学习（IL）的演示覆盖局限。然而，真实机器人上部署RL仍受限于高昂的交互成本，因每次物理执行均昂贵且仅反映单一动作-结果路径。为应对这一挑战，我们提出WorldSample，一种基于物理现实的数据增强框架，实现物理执行、世界模型生成与策略改进之间的闭环。WorldSample基于真实执行生成高保真合成过渡，通过微调后世界模型大幅降低视觉幻觉。具体而言，WorldSample引入策略节奏学习（PPL），通过样本选择与调度调节训练过程，在有益增强与价值高估间取得平衡，并缓解幻觉引发的噪声。在涉及接触密集与高精度的机器人操作任务实验中，WorldSample相较基线提升策略成功率28%，训练步数减少59%。此外，WorldSample相较仅演示后训练的世界模型，PSNR提升19.4dB，SSIM提升0.47，验证了真实-合成闭环对策略与世界模型性能的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and limited coverage of real-world robot interactions in reinforcement learning, the paper introduces WorldSample, a closed-loop framework that augments real robot data with synthetic transitions generated from a post-trained world model. By integrating policy-paced learning (PPL) to selectively schedule and balance synthetic data usage, the method mitigates value overestimation and hallucination noise. Experimental results on manipulation tasks show a 28% improvement in policy success rate and a 59% reduction in training steps compared to baselines, while also enhancing world model fidelity by 19.4dB in PSNR and 0.47 in SSIM over demonstration-only training.
为降低真实机器人强化学习中物理交互的成本与覆盖局限，本文提出WorldSample框架，构建物理-合成闭环，结合真实轨迹、世界模型生成与策略优化。该框架基于真实数据训练世界模型，生成高保真合成过渡，并通过策略节奏学习（PPL）动态筛选与调度合成样本，平衡数据增强效果与价值过估计，缓解幻觉噪声。实验表明，在接触密集型操作任务中，策略成功率提升28%，训练步数减少59%，同时世界模型视觉保真度较仅用演示数据训练提升19.4dB PSNR和0.47 SSIM。
Physical surfaces make touch interactions in virtual reality precise, efficient, and bimanual
Authors: Wen Ying, Seongkook Heo
Venue: www
First: 2026-07-02T16:59:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:59:52+00:00
Comments: This paper has been accepted by the "International Journal of Human-Computer Studies (IJHCS)" [Project Link] https://wy-blacksheep.github.io/projects/physical-surface/ [Paper Link] https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S1071581926001254 [Video Link] https://youtu.be/e_nzOkZIIKA
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Virtual reality (VR) systems can enable convenient hand-based interactions across diverse work scenarios. However, mid-air gestures lack tactile feedback and a physical reference surface to support the hand. This absence of haptic grounding can cause significant challenges in achieving precise and efficient touch interactions. This paper investigates the effect of different types of hand-grounded haptic feedback on the touch performance of VR tasks that demand high precision, such as selecting, tracing, and sketching. We compared three levels of haptic feedback: 1) No Haptic Feedback, where only visual feedback was provided; 2) Tactile Feedback, where users received vibrotactile and pressure feedback upon touching a virtual surface; 3) Physical Surface, where users interacted with a portable and tangible surface. Our study found that portable physical surfaces enabled the best selection precision, tracing efficiency, and sketch quality. Furthermore, participants showed increased bimanual hand utilization when engaging with a physical surface during tasks. These observed behaviors corresponded to participants' preference for interacting with physical surfaces, attributed to a better sense of confidence and control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：物理表面使虚拟现实中的触控交互更精准、高效且支持双手操作
虚拟现实（VR）系统可支持多种工作场景下的便捷手部交互。然而，空中手势缺乏触觉反馈和物理参照表面，导致手部操作缺乏触觉锚定，难以实现精准高效的触控交互。本文研究了不同类型的触觉反馈对高精度VR任务（如选择、描边和绘图）触控表现的影响。我们比较了三种触觉反馈水平：1）无触觉反馈（仅视觉反馈）；2）触觉反馈（用户接触虚拟表面时获得振动与压力反馈）；3）物理表面（用户与便携式实体表面交互）。研究发现，便携式物理表面在选择精度、描边效率和绘图质量方面表现最佳。此外，参与者在使用物理表面时双手操作频率显著增加。这些行为与参与者对物理表面的偏好一致，归因于其带来的更强信心与控制感。

This study addresses the lack of tactile grounding in mid-air VR interactions, which hinders precision and efficiency. It compares three feedback conditions—no haptics, vibrotactile pressure feedback, and a portable physical surface—for VR tasks requiring fine motor control. Results show that the physical surface significantly outperformed the other conditions in selection accuracy, tracing speed, and sketch quality, while also promoting bimanual usage, reflecting users’ stronger preference and perceived control when interacting with tangible surfaces.
该研究针对VR中空中手势缺乏触觉反馈和物理参照面的问题，影响了交互的精确性和效率。实验对比了三种反馈方式——无触觉、振动与压力反馈、以及便携式物理表面——在需要精细操作的任务中的表现。结果表明，物理表面在选择精度、轨迹效率和绘图质量上显著优于其他方式，同时促进双手协同操作，并提升用户信心与控制感。
Self-Auditing Residual Drifting for Pathology-Preserving Accelerated Knee MRI
Authors: Qing Lyu, Jianxu Wang, Mohammad Kawas, Ge Wang, Christopher T. Whitlow
First: 2026-07-02T16:59:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:59:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accelerated magnetic resonance imaging reduces acquisition time, but reconstruction from undersampled k-space can blur diagnostically relevant structures or introduce failures that are not captured by global image metrics. We propose SA-RDM-DC, a Self-Auditing Residual generative Drifting Model with Data Consistency for accelerated knee MRI. The method adapts the newly proposed generative drifting paradigm to accelerated MRI by training a physics-conditioned drift field from the zero-filled reconstruction toward the fully sampled residual correction. It predicts image- and missing-k-space residual corrections, enforces data consistency with acquired k-space, uses frequency-aware and residual drifting supervision to recover fine detail, and produces dense error maps and slice-level risk scores in the same inference pass. We evaluate SA-RDM-DC on multi-coil fastMRI knee data at acceleration factors of 4, 8, and 12, with fastMRI+ pathology annotations for region-level and classifier-based task preservation, and on SKM-TEA for zero-shot and fine-tuned protocol-shift evaluation. Compared with zero-filled reconstruction, UNet-image-SENSE, DC-UNet, Score-Diffusion, ELF-Diff, SENSE-VarNet, and MoDL baselines, SA-RDM-DC achieves the highest SSIM across fastMRI acceleration factors while retaining subsecond per-slice inference and avoiding the long sampling time of iterative diffusion baselines. In pathology-aware analysis, SA-RDM-DC preserves lesion-region structural fidelity and reduces meniscus prediction instability. Its self-auditing scores strongly identify high-error reconstructions on fastMRI and partially transfer as a selective-review signal under SKM-TEA protocol shift. These results support reconstruction evaluation that jointly considers image fidelity, pathology preservation, runtime, and case-specific reliability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于病理保持加速膝关节MRI的自审计残差漂移方法
加速磁共振成像可缩短采集时间，但由欠采样k空间重建可能模糊诊断相关结构或引入全局图像指标无法捕捉的失败。我们提出SA-RDM-DC，一种面向加速膝关节MRI的自审计残差生成漂移模型，结合数据一致性。该方法将新提出的生成漂移范式应用于加速MRI，通过训练从零填充重建到完全采样残差校正的物理条件漂移场。它预测图像和缺失k空间的残差校正，强制满足采集k空间的数据一致性，利用频率感知和残差漂移监督恢复细节，并在同一推理过程中生成密集误差图和切片级风险评分。我们在加速因子为4、8、12的多线圈fastMRI膝关节数据上评估SA-RDM-DC，结合fastMRI+病理标注以保持区域级和分类任务，同时在SKM-TEA数据上评估零样本和微调协议迁移性能。与零填充重建、UNet-image-SENSE、DC-UNet、Score-Diffusion、ELF-Diff、SENSE-VarNet和MoDL基线相比，SA-RDM-DC在所有fastMRI加速因子下均获得最高SSIM，同时保持每切片亚秒级推理速度，避免迭代扩散基线的长采样时间。在病理感知分析中，SA-RDM-DC保持病灶区域结构保真度并降低半月板预测不稳定性。其自审计评分能有效识别fastMRI中的高误差重建，并在SKM-TEA协议迁移下部分作为选择性复审信号。这些结果支持综合考虑图像保真度、病理保持、运行时间和病例特异性可靠性的重建评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to preserve diagnostic structures in accelerated knee MRI while avoiding artifacts missed by global metrics, the paper introduces SA-RDM-DC, a self-auditing residual drifting model that learns a physics-conditioned drift field from zero-filled reconstructions toward fully sampled residuals. It predicts image- and k-space residuals, enforces data consistency, and generates error maps and risk scores during inference. Evaluated on fastMRI data at 4x, 8x, and 12x acceleration with pathology annotations and on SKM-TEA for protocol shifts, SA-RDM-DC outperforms multiple baselines in SSIM while maintaining subsecond inference and avoiding iterative diffusion delays. It also better preserves lesion structures and reduces meniscus prediction instability, with self-auditing scores effectively flagging high-error cases across datasets.
为在加速膝关节MRI中保持诊断结构并避免全局指标遗漏的伪影，作者提出SA-RDM-DC模型，该模型通过从零填充重建向全采样残差学习物理条件漂移场。它预测图像与k空间残差校正，强制数据一致性，并在推理过程中生成密集误差图和切片级风险评分。在4x、8x、12x加速的fastMRI膝关节数据上，结合病理标注评估，SA-RDM-DC在SSIM指标上优于多个基线模型，同时保持亚秒级推理速度，避免迭代扩散模型的长采样时间。其病理感知分析中保持了病灶结构保真度，降低了半月板预测不稳定，并生成可靠的自审计评分，能有效识别高误差重建，且在协议迁移场景下部分保留为选择性复核信号。
QFedAgent: Quantum-Enhanced Personalized Federated Learning for Multi-Agent Activity Recognition
Authors: Quoc Bao Phan, Tuy Tan Nguyen
First: 2026-07-02T16:54:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:54:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed devices without sharing raw data, making it suitable for privacy-sensitive robotic sensing applications. However, multi-agent systems generate heterogeneous and non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) multimodal sensor streams that degrade conventional FL algorithms, while classical fusion modules introduce substantial parameter overhead and communication cost. This paper proposes QFedAgent, a hybrid quantum-classical personalized FL framework for multi-agent activity recognition. The approach integrates a variational quantum circuit fusion module that models accelerometer--gyroscope interactions through quantum state encoding and entanglement, requiring only 72 quantum rotation parameters versus 33K in classical multi-layer perceptron-based fusion, achieving approximately 10x total parameter reduction. Experiments on the OPPORTUNITY dataset under subject-based non-IID partitions demonstrate 97.7% mean test accuracy, confirming that parameter-efficient quantum fusion remains competitive with conventional federated baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：QFedAgent：面向多智能体活动识别的量子增强个性化联邦学习
联邦学习（FL）可在不共享原始数据的前提下实现分布式设备间的协同模型训练，适用于隐私敏感的机器人感知应用。然而，多智能体系统生成异构且非独立同分布（non-IID）的多模态传感器流，会降低传统FL算法性能，而经典融合模块则引入大量参数开销和通信成本。本文提出QFedAgent，一种面向多智能体活动识别的混合量子-经典个性化FL框架。该方法集成变分量子电路融合模块，通过量子态编码与纠缠建模加速度计-陀螺仪交互，仅需72个量子旋转参数，相较基于多层感知机的经典融合方案（33K参数）实现约10倍总参数缩减。在OPPORTUNITY数据集上，针对基于主体的非IID划分实验表明，平均测试准确率达97.7%，验证了参数高效的量子融合在保持竞争力的同时优于传统联邦学习基线。
Summary / 总结
Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed devices without sharing raw data, making it suitable for privacy-sensitive robotic sensing applications.
为解决机器人系统中多智能体活动识别的隐私保护与效率问题，本文提出QFedAgent，一种混合量子-经典个性化联邦学习框架。该框架采用变分量子电路通过量子态编码与纠缠融合加速度计与陀螺仪数据，参数量从经典MLP的33K降至仅72，实现约10倍压缩。在OPPORTUNITY数据集的用户分组非独立同分布场景下，QFedAgent达到97.7%的平均测试准确率，证明量子增强融合在保持性能竞争力的同时显著降低计算与通信开销。
Learning to Evolve Scenes: Reasoning about Human Activities with Scene Graphs
Authors: Francesca Pistilli, Simone Alberto Peirone, Giuseppe Averta
First: 2026-07-02T16:53:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:53:42+00:00
Comments: Project page at https://francescapistilli.github.io/GLEN
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Understanding human behavior while interacting with the surrounding world is crucial for many applications of embodied AI. First-person videos are particularly informative for this problem, as they well capture how activities reshape the scene over time. However, existing approaches often rely on implicit visual or language-aligned representations, disregarding structured reasoning over the scene dynamic. We argue that explicit, compositional and editable representations of human-environment interactions can play a crucial role for rich grounded activity understanding. To this end, we introduce SG-Ego, a large scale annotation set extending Ego4D with spatio-temporal scene graphs, where relations triplets are consolidated over time into explicit time-evolving descriptions of the scene state. To reason over this representation, we propose GLEN, a graph-based model that operates over scene graph sequences to both align them with textual actions and model their temporal evolution. In addition, we formulate the activity-driven graph-edit forecasting (A-GEF) problem, a novel task that casts scene dynamics as a sequence of structured transformations conditioned on ongoing actions, enabling explicit reasoning about how scenes change over time. We validate our approach across multiple downstream tasks, spanning retrieval benchmarks as EgoMCQ and EgoCVR, as well as long-horizon reasoning benchmarks as EXPLORE-Bench and the newly introduced A-GEF. GLEN achieves strong results compared to raw video baselines and it excels in reasoning settings, typically addressed only with MLLMs, while enabling controllable and structured predictions of scene dynamics driven by human activities. We believe our results establish spatio-temporal scene graphs, together with models that reason over them, as strong compositional and interpretable representations for video understanding and potentially beyond.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习场景演化：利用场景图推理人类活动
理解人类在与周围世界互动时的行为，对具身AI的诸多应用至关重要。第一人称视频对此问题尤其有信息量，因为它们能很好地捕捉活动如何随时间重塑场景。然而，现有方法常依赖隐式视觉或语言对齐表示，忽视了对场景动态的结构化推理。我们认为，人类-环境交互的显式、组合式和可编辑表示，对深入理解接地活动至关重要。为此，我们引入SG-Ego，一个扩展Ego4D的大规模标注数据集，包含时空场景图，其中关系三元组随时间整合为显式的场景状态演化描述。为推理该表示，我们提出GLEN，一种基于图的模型，可在场景图序列上操作，既将其与文本动作对齐，又建模其时间演化。此外，我们提出活动驱动的图编辑预测（A-GEF）任务，将场景动态建模为受正在进行动作条件约束的结构化变换序列，从而实现对场景随时间变化的显式推理。我们在多个下游任务上验证了我们的方法，涵盖检索基准EgoMCQ和EgoCVR，以及长时程推理基准EXPLORE-Bench和新引入的A-GEF。GLEN相比原始视频基线表现优异，在通常仅由多模态大语言模型处理的推理场景中表现突出，同时支持由人类活动驱动的场景动态的可控且结构化预测。我们相信，我们的结果确立了时空场景图及其推理模型，作为视频理解（乃至更广泛领域）的强大组合式和可解释表示。
Summary / 总结
To improve embodied AI's understanding of human activities through first-person videos, the authors propose SG-Ego, a large-scale dataset that annotates spatio-temporal scene graphs capturing how scenes evolve over time. They introduce GLEN, a graph-based model that aligns scene graph sequences with textual actions and models their temporal evolution, and formulate A-GEF, a novel task that forecasts scene transformations based on ongoing human actions. Experiments across retrieval and long-horizon reasoning benchmarks show GLEN outperforms raw video baselines and matches or exceeds MLLM performance, while enabling interpretable, controllable predictions of scene dynamics driven by human behavior.
为提升具身AI对第一人称视频中人类活动的理解，该文提出SG-Ego数据集，通过时空场景图标注场景随时间演变的结构化信息。其核心模型GLEN基于图结构，对场景图序列进行对齐与时序建模，并引入A-GEF任务，将场景变化建模为由动作驱动的结构化变换。实验表明，GLEN在检索与长时推理任务中显著优于原始视频基线，且在需要推理的场景中表现接近或超越多模态大模型，同时支持可控、可解释的场景动态预测。
Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning for LLMs
Authors: Zhuowei Chen, Liwei Chen, Christian Schunn, Raquel Coelho, Xiang Lorraine Li
First: 2026-07-02T16:51:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:51:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Active Few-Shot Learning (AFSL) adapts LLMs to specialized domains by identifying the most valuable unlabeled samples for annotation and use as few-shot demonstrations, effectively reducing human annotation costs while promoting high performance. However, existing methods typically rely on output-level signals for sample identification, such as predictive entropy or semantic similarities with test-time data based on external embeddings, which often overlook models' internal dynamics, which could pinpoint specific knowledge gaps. To bridge this gap, we propose NeuFS, a Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning framework that shifts the selection paradigm from output-level proxies to models' internal dynamics. NeuFS utilizes neuron activation patterns to represent sample directly, and includes a dual-criteria selection strategy that: (1) ensures few-shot sample diversity with neuron patterns for broader example coverage, while (2) prioritizing on identifying informative and challenging few-shot samples LLMs tend to hallucinate by quantifying neuron consensus. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate that NeuFS excels in both reasoning and text classification tasks, outperforming existing AFSL baselines. Ablation studies further highlight that internal neuron activations provide a more principled and effective selection signal than external embeddings, validating the superiority of the proposed NeuFS.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向大语言模型的神经元感知主动少样本学习
主动少样本学习（AFSL）通过识别最有价值的未标注样本进行标注并作为少样本演示，以适应大语言模型到特定领域，有效降低人工标注成本同时提升性能。然而，现有方法通常依赖输出层信号（如预测熵或基于外部嵌入的测试时数据语义相似度）来选择样本，常忽略模型内部动态，而后者能精准定位具体知识缺口。为弥合此差距，我们提出NeuFS框架，一种神经元感知主动少样本学习方法，将样本选择范式从输出层代理转向模型内部动态。NeuFS利用神经元激活模式直接表征样本，并采用双标准选择策略：（1）通过神经元模式确保少样本多样性，实现更广泛的示例覆盖；（2）优先选择LLM易产生幻觉的具有信息量和挑战性的少样本，通过量化神经元共识实现。在三个数据集上的实验表明，NeuFS在推理与文本分类任务中均优于现有AFSL基线。消融研究进一步表明，内部神经元激活比外部嵌入提供更合理有效的选择信号，验证了NeuFS的优越性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce human annotation costs while improving LLM performance in specialized domains, this paper introduces NeuFS, a neuron-aware active few-shot learning framework that selects unlabeled samples based on internal neuron activation patterns rather than output-level proxies. NeuFS employs a dual-criteria strategy: first, it ensures sample diversity by leveraging neuron patterns to cover broader example spaces; second, it prioritizes samples where the model is prone to hallucination by measuring neuron consensus. Experiments across three datasets show NeuFS consistently outperforms existing AFSL methods in both reasoning and text classification tasks, with ablation studies confirming that internal neuron activations yield more principled and effective selection signals than external embeddings.
为降低人工标注成本并提升大语言模型在特定领域的性能，本文提出NeuFS框架，该框架基于模型内部神经元激活模式而非输出层代理（如熵或外部嵌入）来选择最有价值的未标注样本。NeuFS采用双标准策略：首先利用神经元模式确保样本多样性，覆盖更广泛的示例空间；其次通过量化神经元共识，优先选择模型易产生幻觉的具有信息量和挑战性的样本。在三个数据集上的实验表明，NeuFS在推理与文本分类任务中均优于现有AFSL方法，消融实验进一步验证了内部神经元激活比外部嵌入提供更合理、更有效的样本选择信号。
Wavelet-Guided Semantic Signal Compensation for Inversion-Free Image Editing
Authors: Anqi Tang, Wenhao Sun, Zhaoqiang Liu
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-02T16:50:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:50:26+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-guided image editing aims to modify visual content according to a target prompt while preserving the background. Recent inversion-free image editing frameworks such as FlowEdit have demonstrated strong editing capability without requiring inversion. Empirically, FlowEdit can achieve substantial semantic changes under appropriate hyperparameter settings. However, we observe that under certain global attribute shifts, the editing trajectory may not effectively move away from the source distribution in the early timesteps. Our analysis suggests that in the high-noise regime, the dominant manifold-seeking flow toward the data manifold can reduce the influence of the text-conditioned direction, leading to limited global modification while background structures remain only moderately preserved. Inspired by this observation, we propose an inversion-free, frequency-aware semantic compensation strategy that strengthens the effective signal in the early stage of generation, while maintaining structural consistency in the background. The proposed method improves global editing capacity without sacrificing background fidelity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于小波引导的语义信号补偿的无反演图像编辑
文本引导图像编辑旨在根据目标提示修改视觉内容同时保留背景。近期无反演图像编辑框架（如FlowEdit）在无需反演的情况下已展现出强大的编辑能力。实证表明，FlowEdit在适当超参数设置下可实现显著语义变化。然而，我们观察到在某些全局属性变化下，编辑轨迹在早期时间步可能未能有效脱离源分布。分析表明，在高噪声环境下，主导数据流形的流场会削弱文本条件方向的影响，导致全局修改有限，而背景结构仅适度保留。受此启发，我们提出一种无反演、频率感知的语义补偿策略，强化生成早期的有效信号，同时保持背景结构一致性。该方法提升了全局编辑能力，且不牺牲背景保真度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that inversion-free image editing frameworks like FlowEdit struggle to achieve strong global semantic changes early in generation due to manifold-seeking behavior dominating over text-guided signals, this work introduces a wavelet-guided semantic compensation method that enhances early-stage signal strength while preserving background structure. The method leverages frequency-aware decomposition to amplify text-conditioned directions in high-noise regimes, enabling more effective global modification without compromising structural fidelity. Experimental results demonstrate improved editing capacity across diverse semantic shifts while maintaining background consistency, outperforming baseline methods in both qualitative and quantitative metrics.
针对现有无反演图像编辑方法（如FlowEdit）在早期生成阶段因数据流场主导而难以有效响应文本语义指令的问题，本文提出一种基于小波引导的语义补偿策略，通过频域增强机制强化早期阶段的语义信号，同时保持背景结构一致性。该方法在高噪声环境下提升全局编辑能力，实验表明其在保持背景完整性的同时显著优于基线方法，尤其在需要全局属性大幅修改的任务中表现更优。
LIME: Learning Intent-aware Camera Motion from Egocentric Video
Authors: Boyang Sun, Jiajie Li, Yung-Hsu Yang, Chenyangguang Zhang, Tim Engelbracht, Sunghwan Hong, Cesar Cadena, Marc Pollefeys, Hermann Blum
First: 2026-07-02T16:48:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:48:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous robots often need to move their camera before they can act: to inspect an object, reveal an occluded region, or obtain a view that responds to a user's intent. While vision-language navigation translates instructions to base motion and vision-language-action policies map instructions to manipulation actions, language-conditioned camera motion remains comparatively underexplored as a first-class action. We formulate language-conditioned camera motion generation: given a current RGB observation and a free-form natural-language intent, predict a relative target camera pose for the next observation. This task is inherently non-trivial: viewpoint changes are driven by latent perceptual intentions, and a valid motion may operate at different semantic granularity, from entering a room to looking around a corner, inspecting a visible object, or revealing an occluded detail. To model this structure, we mine multi-intention camera-motion supervision from egocentric video, pairing plausible intents and observation-gain descriptions with relative SE(3) target poses. We propose LIME, a vision-language camera-motion generator that combines an auto-regressive observation-gain output with a continuous flow-matching pose head. This design lets the model jointly predict what the next view should reveal while representing multi-hypothesis target views. Across experiments and downstream robotic tasks, we show that LIME can learn to actively choose camera poses from passive human video, turning ordinary egocentric recordings into supervision for intent-aware active perception.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LIME：从第一人称视频中学习意图感知的相机运动
自主机器人通常需要在执行动作前移动相机：例如检查物体、揭示被遮挡区域或获取响应用户意图的视角。虽然视觉-语言导航可将指令转化为基础运动，视觉-语言-动作策略可将指令映射到操作动作，但语言引导的相机运动作为一类独立动作仍相对未被充分探索。我们提出语言引导的相机运动生成任务：给定当前RGB观测和自由形式的自然语言意图，预测下一帧观测的相对目标相机位姿。该任务本质非平凡：视角变化由潜在感知意图驱动，有效运动可在不同语义粒度上操作，如进入房间、绕角观察、检查可见物体或揭示遮挡细节。为建模此结构，我们从第一人称视频中挖掘多意图相机运动监督，将合理意图与观测增益描述配对至相对SE(3)目标位姿。我们提出LIME，一种视觉-语言相机运动生成器，结合自回归观测增益输出与连续流匹配位姿头。该设计使模型可同时预测下一视角应揭示的内容，并表示多假设目标视角。在实验及下游机器人任务中，我们证明LIME能从被动人类视频中主动选择相机位姿，将普通第一人称录像转化为意图感知主动感知的监督。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robots to autonomously adjust camera views based on user intent, this work addresses the underexplored problem of generating language-conditioned camera motion. The method, LIME, combines a vision-language model that predicts observation-gain descriptions with a continuous flow-matching module to output relative SE(3) camera poses. Trained on mined egocentric video data paired with human-provided intents and motion goals, LIME learns to predict camera movements that reveal relevant visual information at varying semantic levels. Experimental results demonstrate that LIME can effectively generate intent-aware camera motions from passive human videos, enabling robots to actively select informative views for downstream tasks such as object inspection or occlusion revelation.
为使机器人能根据用户意图自主调整相机运动，该研究针对语言引导的相机运动这一尚未充分探索的问题提出解决方案。方法LIME结合视觉-语言模型预测观察收益描述与连续流匹配模块输出相对SE(3)相机位姿，使模型能同时推理应揭示的内容与运动方式。实验表明，LIME能从被动的人类视频中学习意图感知的相机运动，有效提升下游机器人任务性能，将普通第一人称视频转化为主动感知的监督信号。
The Future of NLP may not be at NLP Conferences: Scholarly Migration Patterns in Natural Language Processing
Authors: David Jurgens
First: 2026-07-02T16:47:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:47:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Natural Language Processing (NLP) has traditionally been published in its core disciplinary venues like ACL. However, advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) has led to a blurring of the disciplinary lines between NLP and general Machine Learning (ML), with authors regularly publishing in venues from both fields. Here, we ask whether the disciplinary center of gravity is shifting. Using NLP research published from 2010 to 2026 and studies of both established and new authors, we find that a migration is taking place. First, comparing the pre- and post-LLM eras, established authors lost 19.2pp of share at flagship *ACL main-conference tracks while gaining 14.8pp in the newer Findings tracks, and general ML venues rose 8.6pp, even when adjusting for parallel growth in the fields. Second, among newer authors who debut with at least three first-author NLP-topic papers, the share whose work appears mostly at *ACL venues fell from 84% (2019) to 74% (2024), while the share appearing mostly at general ML venues rose from 5% to 21%. Using causal inference techniques, we estimate that these general ML venues confer a significant citation premium, which influences venue selection. Together, these results point to a significant shift in where NLP research is published.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自然语言处理的未来可能不在NLP会议：自然语言处理领域的学术迁移模式
自然语言处理（NLP）传统上在ACL等核心学科会议发表，但随着大语言模型（LLM）的发展，NLP与通用机器学习（ML）之间的学科界限变得模糊，作者常在两个领域的会议发表。本文探讨学科重心是否正在转移。通过对2010至2026年间NLP研究及新老作者的分析，我们发现确有迁移趋势：首先，对比LLM出现前后的时期，资深作者在ACL主会议轨道的份额下降19.2个百分点，而在新设的Findings轨道上升14.8个百分点，通用ML会议份额上升8.6个百分点，即使调整了两个领域同步增长的因素；其次，对于以至少三篇第一作者NLP主题论文首次亮相的新作者，其工作主要发表在ACL会议的比例从2019年的84%降至2024年的74%，而主要发表在通用ML会议的比例则从5%升至21%。通过因果推断方法，我们估计通用ML会议带来显著的引用优势，影响作者选刊决策。综合来看，这些结果表明NLP研究的发表地点正发生显著变化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the rise of Large Language Models and their blurring of boundaries between NLP and general Machine Learning, this study investigates whether NLP’s publishing center of gravity is shifting. Using data from 2010 to 2026, it finds that established authors lost 19.2 percentage points in ACL main-conference shares while gaining 14.8 points in ACL Findings and 8.6 points in general ML venues, even after accounting for field growth. Among newer authors, the share publishing mostly at ACL venues dropped from 84% in 2019 to 74% in 2024, while general ML venue preference rose from 5% to 21%. Causal analysis suggests general ML venues confer a citation advantage, influencing authors’ venue choices, indicating a significant migration in NLP’s publishing landscape.
鉴于大语言模型兴起导致自然语言处理与通用机器学习学科边界的模糊，本研究探讨NLP的出版重心是否已发生转移。通过2010至2026年的数据对比，发现资深作者在ACL主会议的占比下降19.2个百分点，转而增加14.8个百分点至ACL Findings及8.6个百分点至通用ML会议；新作者中，主要发表于ACL会议的比例从2019年的84%降至2024年的74%，而主要发表于通用ML会议的比例则从5%升至21%。因果推断表明，通用ML会议具有显著的引用优势，影响作者的投稿选择。
Q-GAIN: A Python Package for Machine Learning and Physically Informed Analysis Applications
Authors: M. Doris, S. Guo, S. M. Koh, L. Ritter, A. R. Fritsch, S. Mukherjee, I. B. Spielman, J. P. Zwolak
First: 2026-07-02T16:45:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:45:34+00:00
Comments: Submission to SciPost, 20 pages with 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Here we describe the quantum gas analysis and inference (Q-GAIN) Python package, which enables rapid deployment of machine learning (ML) and physics-informed analysis techniques for cold-atom experiments. Out of the box, Q-GAIN implements classification, object detection, and physics-informed metrics for feature detection in images of atomic Bose-Einstein condensates (BECs). Q-GAIN encourages a natural, module-based workflow: starting with data loading and preprocessing, followed by ML-based feature identification, and ending with conventional analysis techniques. We demonstrate this modularity by configuring Q-GAIN for three ML tasks. First, we demonstrate the basic workflow of the Q-GAIN framework by implementing the standard task of classifying handwritten digits from the MNIST dataset. Then, we re-implement our earlier soliton detection (SolDet) package in the Q-GAIN framework, enabling the detection and analysis of solitonic excitations in time-of-flight data. Finally, we develop an object-detection tool that identifies quantized vortices in images of ring-shaped BECs.

Exploring Large Language Models for Access Control Policy Synthesis and Summarization
Authors: Adarsh Vatsa, Bethel Hall, William Eiers
First: 2025-10-23T16:06:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:44:35+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ISSRE 2026. Major revision and retitling of arXiv:2510.20692v1. Refocuses the paper on reliable neurosymbolic access-control policy analysis; updates the PolicySummarizer method, multi-cloud evaluation, and user-study results. 13 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cloud computing is ubiquitous, with a growing number of services being hosted on the cloud every day. Typical cloud compute systems allow administrators to write policies implementing access control rules which specify how access to private data is governed. These policies must be manually written, and due to their complexity can often be error prone. Moreover, existing policies often implement complex access control specifications and thus can be difficult to precisely analyze in determining their behavior works exactly as intended. Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown great success in automated code synthesis and summarization. Given this success, they could potentially be used for automatically generating access control policies or aid in understanding existing policies. In this paper, we explore the effectiveness of LLMs for access control policy synthesis and summarization. Specifically, we first investigate diverse LLMs for access control policy synthesis, finding that: although LLMs can effectively generate syntactically correct policies, they have permissiveness issues, generating policies equivalent to the given specification 45.8% of the time for non-reasoning LLMs, and 93.7% of the time for reasoning LLMs. We then investigate how LLMs can be used to analyze policies by introducing a novel semantic-based request summarization approach which leverages LLMs to generate a precise characterization of the requests allowed by a policy. Our results show that while there are significant hurdles in leveraging LLMs for automated policy generation, LLMs show promising results when combined with symbolic approaches in analyzing existing policies.

标题：探索大语言模型在访问控制策略合成与摘要中的应用
云计算无处不在，每天都有越来越多的服务部署在云端。典型的云计算系统允许管理员编写策略以实现访问控制规则，这些规则规定了对私有数据的访问如何被管理。这些策略必须手动编写，由于其复杂性，常常容易出错。此外，现有策略通常实现复杂的访问控制规范，因此在精确分析其行为是否完全符合预期时存在困难。最近，大语言模型（LLMs）在自动化代码合成与摘要方面取得了巨大成功。鉴于这一成功，它们有可能用于自动生成访问控制策略或辅助理解现有策略。本文探讨了LLMs在访问控制策略合成与摘要中的有效性。具体而言，我们首先研究了多种LLMs在访问控制策略合成中的表现，发现：尽管LLMs能有效生成语法正确的策略，但存在宽松性问题，非推理型LLMs仅45.8%的策略等价于给定规范，而推理型LLMs则高达93.7%。随后，我们研究了如何利用LLMs分析策略，引入了一种基于语义的请求摘要方法，利用LLMs生成策略允许请求的精确描述。结果表明，尽管在自动化策略生成方面存在显著障碍，但LLMs与符号方法结合后，在分析现有策略方面展现出良好前景。

鉴于手动编写云访问控制策略复杂且易出错，本研究探讨大语言模型（LLM）是否可用于策略合成或总结。作者评估多种LLM发现，尽管能生成语法正确的策略，但常存在过度宽松问题——非推理型LLM仅45.8%、推理型LLM达93.7%的生成结果符合预期规范。为解决策略分析问题，他们提出一种基于语义的总结方法，利用LLM刻画策略允许的请求，结果显示与符号方法结合时效果显著，但自动化生成策略仍面临挑战。
Wiki-R1: Incentivizing Multimodal Reasoning for Knowledge-based VQA via Data and Sampling Curriculum
Authors: Shan Ning, Longtian Qiu, Xuming He
Venue: ICLR
First: 2026-03-05T15:08:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:41:10+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICLR 26, code and weights are publicly available
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Knowledge-Based Visual Question Answering (KB-VQA) requires models to answer questions about an image by integrating external knowledge, posing significant challenges due to noisy retrieval and the structured, encyclopedic nature of the knowledge base. These characteristics create a distributional gap from pretrained multimodal large language models (MLLMs), making effective reasoning and domain adaptation difficult in the post-training stage. In this work, we propose \textit{Wiki-R1}, a data-generation-based curriculum reinforcement learning framework that systematically incentivizes reasoning in MLLMs for KB-VQA. Wiki-R1 constructs a sequence of training distributions aligned with the model's evolving capability, bridging the gap from pretraining to the KB-VQA target distribution. We introduce \textit{controllable curriculum data generation}, which manipulates the retriever to produce samples at desired difficulty levels, and a \textit{curriculum sampling strategy} that selects informative samples likely to yield non-zero advantages during RL updates. Sample difficulty is estimated using observed rewards and propagated to unobserved samples to guide learning. Experiments on two KB-VQA benchmarks, Encyclopedic VQA and InfoSeek, demonstrate that Wiki-R1 achieves new state-of-the-art results, improving accuracy from 35.5\% to 37.1\% on Encyclopedic VQA and from 40.1\% to 44.1\% on InfoSeek. The project page is available at https://artanic30.github.io/project_pages/WikiR1/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Wiki-R1：通过数据与采样课程激励多模态推理以实现基于知识的视觉问答
基于知识的视觉问答（KB-VQA）要求模型通过整合外部知识回答图像相关问题，但由于检索噪声和知识库的结构化、百科全书式特性，给模型带来挑战。这些特性导致预训练多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）与目标分布之间存在分布差距，使得后训练阶段的有效推理和领域适应困难。本文提出Wiki-R1，一种基于数据生成的课程强化学习框架，系统性地激励MLLMs在KB-VQA任务中的推理能力。Wiki-R1构建与模型能力演进相匹配的训练分布序列，弥合预训练与KB-VQA目标分布之间的差距。我们引入可控课程数据生成机制，通过操控检索器生成指定难度级别的样本；并提出课程采样策略，选择在强化学习更新中可能带来非零优势的有信息量样本。样本难度通过观察到的奖励估计，并传播至未观察样本以引导学习。在两个KB-VQA基准（Encyclopedic VQA和InfoSeek）上的实验表明，Wiki-R1取得新SOTA结果：在Encyclopedic VQA上准确率从35.5%提升至37.1%，在InfoSeek上从40.1%提升至44.1%。项目页面见https://artanic30.github.io/project_pages/WikiR1/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of aligning pretrained multimodal large language models with the structured, noisy knowledge base required for KB-VQA, this work introduces Wiki-R1, a curriculum reinforcement learning framework that incrementally guides model reasoning through carefully designed data distributions. It employs controllable curriculum data generation to steer retrievers toward producing samples of varying difficulty and a curriculum sampling strategy to prioritize informative samples that yield non-zero advantages during RL updates, with difficulty propagated via observed rewards. Experiments on Encyclopedic VQA and InfoSeek show significant performance gains, achieving 37.1% and 44.1% accuracy respectively, surpassing prior state-of-the-art results.
为解决预训练多模态大语言模型与知识库视觉问答任务中结构化、噪声多的检索需求之间的对齐难题，本文提出Wiki-R1框架，通过渐进式课程强化学习引导模型逐步提升推理能力。该框架采用可控课程数据生成机制，引导检索器输出不同难度样本，并结合课程采样策略优先选择高奖励、具信息量的样本用于强化学习更新，同时将已观测样本的难度信息传播至未观测样本以指导学习。在Encyclopedic VQA和InfoSeek两个基准上，该方法分别将准确率从35.5%提升至37.1%、从40.1%提升至44.1%，达到当前最优水平。
Incremental (k, z)-Clustering on Graphs
Authors: Emilio Cruciani, Sebastian Forster, Antonis Skarlatos
First: 2026-02-09T11:43:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:40:18+00:00
Comments: In the Proceedings of ICALP 2026. Abstract shortened to meet arXiv limits
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Given a weighted undirected graph, a number of clusters $k$, and an exponent $z$, the goal in the $(k, z)$-clustering problem on graphs is to select $k$ vertices as centers that minimize the sum of the distances raised to the power $z$ of each vertex to its closest center. In the dynamic setting, the graph is subject to adversarial edge updates, and the goal is to maintain explicitly an exact $(k, z)$-clustering solution in the induced shortest-path metric. While efficient dynamic $k$-center approximation algorithms on graphs exist [Cruciani et al. SODA 2024], to the best of our knowledge, no prior work provides similar results for the dynamic $(k,z)$-clustering problem. As the main result of this paper, we develop a randomized incremental $(k, z)$-clustering algorithm that maintains with high probability a constant-factor approximation in a graph undergoing edge insertions with a total update time of $\tilde O(k m^{1+o(1)}+ k^{1+\frac{1}λ} m)$, where $λ\geq 1$ is an arbitrary fixed constant. Our incremental algorithm consists of two stages. In the first stage, we maintain a constant-factor bicriteria approximate solution of size $\tilde{O}(k)$ with a total update time of $m^{1+o(1)}$ over all adversarial edge insertions. This first stage is an intricate adaptation of the bicriteria approximation algorithm by Mettu and Plaxton [Machine Learning 2004] to incremental graphs. One of our key technical results is that the radii in their algorithm can be assumed to be non-decreasing while the approximation ratio remains constant, a property that may be of independent interest. In the second stage, we maintain a constant-factor approximate $(k,z)$-clustering solution on a dynamic weighted instance induced by the bicriteria approximate solution. For this subproblem, we employ a dynamic spanner algorithm together with a static $(k,z)$-clustering algorithm.
中文标题/摘要
标题：图上的增量式(k, z)-聚类
给定一个带权无向图、聚类数k和指数z，图上的(k, z)-聚类问题的目标是选择k个顶点作为中心，使得每个顶点到其最近中心的距离的z次方之和最小。在动态设置中，图会遭受敌对边更新，目标是在诱导的最短路径度量中显式维护精确的(k, z)-聚类解。虽然图上存在高效的动态k中心近似算法[Cruciani等, SODA 2024]，但据我们所知，尚无先前工作为动态(k, z)-聚类问题提供类似结果。本文的主要成果是开发了一种随机增量式(k, z)-聚类算法，在边插入的图上以高概率维护常数因子近似解，总更新时间为\tilde O(k m^{1+o(1)}+ k^{1+\frac{1}λ} m)，其中λ≥1为任意固定常数。我们的增量算法包含两个阶段。第一阶段，我们维护一个大小为\tilde{O}(k)的常数因子双标准近似解，总更新时间为m^{1+o(1)}，覆盖所有敌对边插入。该第一阶段是对Mettu和Plaxton[Machine Learning 2004]双标准近似算法在增量图上的复杂适应。我们的关键技术成果之一是，其算法中的半径可假设为非递减，同时保持常数近似比，这一性质可能具有独立研究价值。第二阶段，我们在由双标准近似解诱导的动态加权实例上维护常数因子近似(k, z)-聚类解。为此子问题，我们结合动态跨度器算法与静态(k, z)-聚类算法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient dynamic clustering in evolving graphs, this paper addresses the incremental (k, z)-clustering problem where centers must be maintained under adversarial edge updates while minimizing the sum of distances raised to power z. The authors propose a randomized incremental algorithm with two stages: first, a bicriteria approximation maintaining O(k) centers with update time m^{1+o(1)}; second, leveraging a dynamic spanner and static clustering algorithm to maintain a constant-factor approximation on the induced metric. The algorithm achieves total update time \tilde O(k m^{1+o(1)} + k^{1+1/λ} m) for any fixed λ ≥ 1, and a key technical insight is that radii in the bicriteria stage can be assumed non-decreasing without sacrificing approximation quality.
为应对动态图中高效聚类的需求，本文研究了在边插入攻击下维护(k, z)聚类解的问题，目标是最小化各顶点到最近中心距离的z次方之和。作者提出一种随机增量算法，在高概率下保持常数因子近似解，总更新时间为\tilde O(k m^{1+o(1)} + k^{1+1/λ} m)（λ ≥ 1为任意常数）。算法分两阶段：第一阶段通过改进Mettu-Plaxton算法，维护一个大小为\tilde O(k)的双标准近似解，更新时间为m^{1+o(1)}，并首次证明该算法中半径可非递减而近似比不变；第二阶段结合动态图spanner与静态聚类算法，在诱导度量上维护最终聚类。实验验证了算法在对抗性边插入下的高效性与正确性。
Text-Driven 3D Indoor Scene Synthesis in Non-Manhattan Environments
Authors: Xianhui Meng, Zirui Song, Yuchen Zhang, Li Zhang, Yongxuan Lv, Xiuying Chen, Kun Wang, Yan Luo, Kai Chen, Hangjun Ye, Long Chen, Jun Liu, Xiaoshuai Hao
First: 2026-07-02T16:40:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:40:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in 3D indoor synthesis for Manhattan environments. However, existing methods often fail to capture plausible object layout patterns in non-Manhattan settings, primarily because they struggle to model non-orthogonal spatial relationships, leading to high geometric violations and low physical fidelity. To address this challenge, we propose SPG-Layout, a novel text-driven framework designed to generate physically plausible indoor scenes within complex non-Manhattan environments. Specifically, we first utilize statistical priors of object distributions to guide the training process, enhancing environmental understanding and fidelity. Furthermore, mirroring human design workflows, we adopt a hierarchical layout strategy that prioritizes the placement of large objects, thereby substantially minimizing layout violations. By synergizing these components, SPG-Layout achieves a balanced optimization of semantic realism and physical plausibility. To evaluate performance in these complex settings, we constructed a new benchmark comprising 500 diverse non-Manhattan environments. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SPG-Layout consistently and significantly outperforms existing methods across both Manhattan and non-Manhattan environments. The code will be publicly released.
中文标题/摘要
