Evaluated on a new benchmark of 500 non-Manhattan scenes, SPG-Layout consistently outperforms prior methods in both Manhattan and non-Manhattan settings, achieving higher semantic realism and lower geometric violations.
为解决现有方法在非曼哈顿环境中因难以建模非正交空间关系而导致的布局不合理问题，作者提出SPG-Layout框架，通过统计物体分布先验引导训练，并采用优先放置大型物体的分层布局策略提升物理合理性。在包含500个非曼哈顿场景的新基准测试中，SPG-Layout在曼哈顿与非曼哈顿环境中均显著优于现有方法，实现更高的语义真实性和更低的几何违规率。
Object-centric LeJEPA
Authors: Jakob Geusen, Ender Konukoglu
First: 2026-07-02T16:38:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:38:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Image encoders trained with LeJEPA can deliver strong features for downstream tasks, but, like other image-level self-supervised methods, typically require large training datasets. Aligning representations at the level of objects rather than whole scenes promises greater data efficiency, but doing this in a completely self-supervised way, effectively jointly partitioning a scene and representing its objects, is unstable: the two are locked in a cyclic dependency, partitioning requires meaningful representations, while meaningful representations require consistent partitioning. We sidestep this instability by taking object masks as given during training, using cheap, off-the-shelf SAM proposals. We extend LeJEPA - whose distributional anti-collapse objective ports naturally from whole images to variable-sized sets of objects - to align object-centric representations rather than whole images. An additional instance-separating loss, which treats other objects in the same scene as negatives, further boosts downstream performance. Across two model scales and 10-100% of COCO, object-level LeJEPA outperforms image-level LeJEPA on tracking (DAVIS), classification (ImageNet-1k), segmentation (ADE20k), and re-identification (NAVI).
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向对象的LeJEPA
使用LeJEPA训练的图像编码器可为下游任务提供强大特征，但与其他图像级自监督方法类似，通常需要大量训练数据。在对象层面而非整个场景层面对表征进行对齐有望提高数据效率，但完全自监督地实现这一点——即同时对场景进行分割并表示其对象——是不稳定的：两者存在循环依赖，分割需要有意义的表征，而有意义的表征又需要一致的分割。我们通过在训练中使用廉价的现成SAM分割提议作为对象掩码，绕过这一不稳定性。我们将LeJEPA扩展至对对象级表征进行对齐，而非整个图像，其分布对抗坍缩目标自然适用于可变大小的对象集合。此外，一个实例分离损失（将同一场景中的其他对象视为负样本）进一步提升了下游性能。在两个模型规模和COCO数据集10%-100%的情况下，对象级LeJEPA在跟踪（DAVIS）、分类（ImageNet-1k）、分割（ADE20k）和重识别（NAVI）任务上均优于图像级LeJEPA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of image-level self-supervised methods like LeJEPA, which require large datasets, this work proposes object-centric LeJEPA to improve data efficiency by aligning representations at the object level. It sidesteps instability from jointly partitioning and representing objects by using precomputed object masks from SAM during training, and extends LeJEPA’s anti-collapse objective to variable-sized object sets. An additional instance-separating loss further enhances performance. Experiments show object-level LeJEPA consistently outperforms image-level LeJEPA across two model scales and varying COCO data amounts, improving results on tracking, classification, segmentation, and re-identification tasks.
为解决图像级自监督方法（如LeJEPA）对大规模数据依赖的问题，本文提出面向对象的LeJEPA，通过在对象层面进行表征对齐以提升数据效率。训练时使用现成的SAM生成的对象掩码，避免了场景分割与表征学习之间的循环依赖问题，并将LeJEPA的分布对抗坍缩目标扩展至可变大小的对象集合，同时引入实例分离损失以区分同一场景中的不同对象。实验表明，在两个模型规模和不同COCO数据比例下，对象级LeJEPA在跟踪、分类、分割和重识别任务上均优于图像级LeJEPA。
ACID: Action Consistency via Inverse Dynamics for Planning with World Models
Authors: Gawon Seo, Dongwon Kim, Suha Kwak
First: 2026-07-02T16:38:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:38:10+00:00
Comments: Project Page: [this https URL](https://gawon1224.github.io/ACID/)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Decision-time planning with action-conditioned world models has become a popular paradigm for embodied control. However, the standard planning cost judges a candidate solely by how close its predicted terminal state lies to the goal, leaving the realizability of the intermediate transitions unchecked -- a predicted trajectory can look convincing while the environment rollout drifts away from it. In this paper, we propose ACID, a decision-time planning framework that introduces cycle action consistency: the action inferred backward from a predicted transition by an inverse dynamics model should recover the one that was conditioned on. We fold this per-step residual into the planning cost via a scale-invariant adaptive weight. Across four action-conditioned world models and six tasks spanning rigid and deformable manipulation, articulated control, and visual navigation, ACID consistently improves planning and matches the baseline's accuracy with substantially less planning compute.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ACID：通过逆动力学实现动作一致性以支持世界模型的规划
基于动作条件的世界模型的决策时规划已成为具身控制的流行范式。然而，标准规划成本仅根据候选动作预测的终端状态与目标的接近程度进行评估，而未检查中间过渡的可实现性——预测轨迹可能看起来令人信服，但环境执行时却会偏离该轨迹。本文提出ACID，一种决策时规划框架，引入循环动作一致性：由逆动力学模型从预测过渡反向推断出的动作应能恢复为所条件化的动作。我们通过尺度不变自适应权重将每步残差纳入规划成本。在四个动作条件世界模型和涵盖刚体与柔性操作、关节控制及视觉导航的六个任务中，ACID持续提升规划效果，并以显著更少的规划计算量达到基线精度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the issue that standard planning methods ignore the realizability of intermediate transitions in action-conditioned world models, ACID introduces cycle action consistency by requiring that actions inferred backward via inverse dynamics match the original conditioned actions. It integrates this consistency residual into the planning cost using a scale-invariant adaptive weight. Experiments across four world models and six tasks show ACID consistently improves planning performance while matching baseline accuracy with significantly reduced computational cost.
为了解决标准规划方法忽略动作条件世界模型中中间过渡可实现性的问题，ACID 引入了循环动作一致性，要求通过逆动力学模型反推的动作与原始条件动作一致，并将该一致性残差以尺度不变自适应权重融入规划成本。在四个世界模型和六个任务上的实验表明，ACID 一致提升规划性能，在保持与基线精度相当的同时显著降低计算开销。
Show Me Examples: Inferring Visual Concepts from Image Sets
Authors: Nick Stracke, Kolja Bauer, Stefan Andreas Baumann, Miguel Angel Bautista, Josh Susskind, Björn Ommer
First: 2026-07-02T16:35:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:35:52+00:00
Comments: for code, view https://github.com/CompVis/set-learner
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) can follow complex textual instructions, yet they struggle to reason from purely visual context. In particular, current models fail to infer shared concepts from sets of example images and apply them to new inputs. We introduce Visual Concept Inference from Sets (VICIS), a task that evaluates this capability. Given a small context set of images sharing a concept and a query image, the model must generate new images that preserve the context-defined concept while remaining consistent with the query. We show that state-of-the-art VLMs perform poorly on this task, often ignoring the visual context or defaulting to biased generations. To address this gap, we propose a training framework and architecture that learn to infer visual concepts from image sets and extract concept-specific embeddings from queries. Experiments on synthetic data and large-scale ImageNet/WordNet data show that our model generates more accurate and diverse outputs and generalizes to unseen concepts and modalities such as sketches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：展示示例：从图像集合推断视觉概念
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）能够遵循复杂的文本指令，但在纯视觉上下文中推理能力较弱。特别是，当前模型无法从示例图像集合中推断共享概念并将其应用于新输入。我们提出了“从图像集合推断视觉概念”（VICIS）任务，用于评估该能力。给定一组共享概念的示例图像和一个查询图像，模型必须生成新图像，使其保留上下文定义的概念，同时与查询图像保持一致。我们发现当前最先进的VLMs在该任务上表现不佳，常忽略视觉上下文或默认生成有偏结果。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种训练框架和架构，使模型能够从图像集合中推断视觉概念，并从查询中提取概念特定嵌入。在合成数据和大规模ImageNet/WordNet数据上的实验表明，我们的模型生成更准确、更多样化的输出，并能泛化到未见过的概念和模态（如素描）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of vision-language models in reasoning from purely visual context, this work introduces VICIS, a task that requires models to infer shared visual concepts from example image sets and apply them to new queries. The authors propose a training framework and architecture designed to extract concept-specific embeddings from queries and generate outputs consistent with both the context and query. Experiments on synthetic and large-scale ImageNet/WordNet datasets demonstrate that their model significantly outperforms state-of-the-art VLMs, producing more accurate, diverse, and generalizable outputs across unseen concepts and modalities like sketches.
鉴于视觉语言模型在纯视觉上下文推理上的不足，本文提出VICIS任务，要求模型从示例图像集中推断共享视觉概念，并生成与概念和查询图像均一致的新图像。作者设计了一种新的训练框架与架构，旨在从查询图像中提取概念特定嵌入并从图像集中学习视觉概念推理。在合成数据和大规模ImageNet/WordNet数据集上的实验表明，该模型显著优于当前最先进的视觉语言模型，生成更准确、更多样化的结果，并能泛化到未见过的概念和模态，如素描。
A Convex Obstacle Avoidance Formulation
Authors: Ricardo Tapia, Iman Soltani
First: 2025-12-15T19:16:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:35:02+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 12 figures, multimedia
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous driving requires reliable collision avoidance in dynamic environments. Nonlinear Model Predictive Controllers (NMPCs) are suitable for this task, but struggle in time-critical scenarios requiring high frequency. To meet this demand, optimization problems are often simplified via linearization, narrowing the horizon window, or reduced temporal nodes, each compromising accuracy or reliability. This work presents the first general convex obstacle avoidance formulation, enabled by a novel approach to integrating logic. This facilitates the incorporation of an obstacle avoidance formulation into convex MPC schemes, enabling a convex optimization framework with substantially improved computational efficiency relative to conventional nonconvex methods. A key property of the formulation is that obstacle avoidance remains effective even when obstacles lie outside the prediction horizon, allowing shorter horizons for real-time deployment. In scenarios where nonconvex formulations are unavoidable, the proposed method meets or exceeds the performance of representative nonconvex alternatives. The method is evaluated in autonomous vehicle applications, where system dynamics are highly nonlinear.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种凸优化避障公式
自动驾驶需要在动态环境中可靠地避免碰撞。非线性模型预测控制器（NMPC）适用于此任务，但在要求高频率的实时场景中表现不佳。为满足这一需求，通常通过线性化、缩短预测窗口或减少时间节点来简化优化问题，但这些方法会牺牲精度或可靠性。本文首次提出了一种通用的凸优化避障公式，该公式通过一种新颖的逻辑集成方法实现。这使得避障公式可融入凸MPC框架，从而构建出相比传统非凸方法大幅提高计算效率的凸优化框架。该公式的另一关键特性是，即使障碍物位于预测窗口之外，避障仍有效，从而允许使用更短的预测窗口以适应实时部署。在必须使用非凸公式的场景中，该方法性能可媲美或超越代表性非凸方法。该方法在自动驾驶应用中进行了评估，其中系统动力学高度非线性。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of real-time collision avoidance in autonomous driving, where nonlinear Model Predictive Controllers (NMPCs) face computational bottlenecks in time-critical scenarios. To overcome this, the authors introduce a novel convex obstacle avoidance formulation that integrates logic constraints into convex optimization, enabling efficient deployment within short prediction horizons without sacrificing safety. Unlike prior methods that sacrifice accuracy through linearization or horizon reduction, this approach maintains robust obstacle avoidance even when obstacles lie beyond the prediction window. Experimental results in highly nonlinear vehicle dynamics confirm that the convex formulation achieves performance comparable or superior to existing nonconvex methods while significantly improving computational efficiency.
该论文针对自动驾驶中高频率、时间敏感场景下非线性模型预测控制器（NMPC）难以实时避障的问题，提出首个通用凸优化避障框架，通过新颖的逻辑集成方法将避障约束融入凸优化结构，大幅提升计算效率。其核心优势在于即使障碍物位于预测时域之外仍能有效规避，支持更短的预测时域以满足实时性需求。在高度非线性车辆动力学场景下的实验表明，该凸方法在安全性与性能上不逊于主流非凸方法，部分场景下表现更优。
Drive-JEPA: Video JEPA Meets Multimodal Trajectory Distillation for End-to-End Driving
Authors: Linhan Wang, Zichong Yang, Chen Bai, Guoxiang Zhang, Xiaotong Liu, Xiaoyin Zheng, Xiao-Xiao Long, Chang-Tien Lu, Cheng Lu
First: 2026-01-29T17:39:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:34:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
End-to-end autonomous driving increasingly leverages self-supervised video pretraining to learn transferable planning representations. However, pretraining video world models for scene understanding has so far brought only limited improvements. This limitation is compounded by the inherent ambiguity of driving: each scene typically provides only a single human trajectory, making it difficult to learn multimodal behaviors. In this work, we propose Drive-JEPA, a framework that integrates Video Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (V-JEPA) with multimodal trajectory distillation for end-to-end driving. First, we adapt V-JEPA for end-to-end driving, pretraining a ViT encoder on large-scale driving videos to produce predictive representations aligned with trajectory planning. Second, we introduce a proposal-centric planner that distills diverse simulator-generated trajectories alongside human trajectories, with a momentum-aware selection mechanism to promote stable and safe behavior. When evaluated on NAVSIM, the V-JEPA representation combined with a simple transformer-based decoder outperforms prior methods by 3 PDMS in the perception-free setting. The complete Drive-JEPA framework achieves 93.3 PDMS on v1 and 87.8 EPDMS on v2, setting a new state-of-the-art.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Drive-JEPA：视频JEPA结合多模态轨迹蒸馏用于端到端驾驶
端到端自动驾驶日益依赖自监督视频预训练以学习可迁移的规划表示。然而，为场景理解预训练视频世界模型迄今仅带来有限改进。这一局限性因驾驶固有的模糊性而加剧：每个场景通常仅提供一条人类轨迹，难以学习多模态行为。本文提出Drive-JEPA框架，将视频联合嵌入预测架构（V-JEPA）与多模态轨迹蒸馏结合用于端到端驾驶。首先，我们适配V-JEPA用于端到端驾驶，以大规模驾驶视频预训练ViT编码器，生成与轨迹规划对齐的预测表示。其次，我们引入以提案为中心的规划器，蒸馏模拟器生成的多样化轨迹与人类轨迹，并采用动量感知选择机制以促进稳定安全行为。在NAVSIM评估中，V-JEPA表示结合简单Transformer解码器在无感知设置下比先前方法提升3 PDMS。完整Drive-JEPA框架在v1上达到93.3 PDMS，在v2上达到87.8 EPDMS，创下新SOTA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limited gains from video pretraining in autonomous driving and the ambiguity of single human trajectories, this work introduces Drive-JEPA, which combines Video JEPA for self-supervised representation learning with multimodal trajectory distillation. The method adapts a Vision Transformer encoder pretrained on driving videos to generate trajectory-aligned representations, then uses a proposal-centric planner that distills diverse simulator-generated and human trajectories via a momentum-aware selection mechanism to encourage safe and stable behavior. Experimental results show that the V-JEPA representation alone improves performance by 3 PDMS over prior methods in a perception-free setting, and the full Drive-JEPA framework achieves 93.3 PDMS on v1 and 87.8 EPDMS on v2, setting a new state-of-the-art.
为了解决视频预训练在自动驾驶中提升有限以及从稀疏人类轨迹中学习多模态行为困难的问题，该研究提出Drive-JEPA框架，结合视频联合嵌入预测架构（V-JEPA）与多模态轨迹蒸馏。方法首先将V-JEPA适配用于端到端驾驶，通过大规模驾驶视频预训练视觉Transformer编码器以生成与轨迹规划对齐的表示；随后引入以提案为中心的规划器，结合模拟器生成与人类轨迹进行蒸馏，并采用动量感知选择机制以稳定安全行为学习。实验表明，在无感知设置下，V-JEPA表示比现有方法提升3 PDMS，完整Drive-JEPA框架在v1和v2版本上分别达到93.3 PDMS和87.8 EPDMS，刷新当前最优性能。
A Wearable Device Dataset for Mental Health Assessment Using Laser Doppler Flowmetry and Fluorescence Spectroscopy Sensors
Authors: Minh Ngoc Nguyen, Khai Le-Duc, Tan-Hanh Pham, Trong Nhan Nguyen, Bailey Trang, Ba Kien Tran, Viktor Dremin, Sergei Sokolovsky, Edik Rafailov, Truong-Son Hy
First: 2025-02-03T00:51:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:33:50+00:00
Comments: Communications Medicine 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Mental health problems such as stress, anxiety, and depression affect millions of people worldwide. These conditions are usually assessed using questionnaires, which rely on how people describe their own feelings. In this study, we explore whether a wearable device can help measure mental health using physical signals from the body. The device records small changes in blood flow and tissue activity from the fingertip. We collected data from 132 adults across 19 countries and compared these signals with mental health questionnaire results. We found that patterns in blood flow and tissue activity are linked to stress-related symptoms. This approach may help develop new tools for simple, non-invasive mental health monitoring in everyday life. Code and datasets are publicly available: https://github.com/leduckhai/Wearable_LDF-FS
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于心理健康评估的可穿戴设备数据集（采用激光多普勒血流仪与荧光光谱传感器）
压力、焦虑和抑郁等心理健康问题影响全球数百万人。这些状况通常通过问卷评估，依赖个人对自身感受的描述。本研究探讨可穿戴设备是否能通过身体生理信号测量心理健康。该设备记录指尖微小的血流和组织活动变化。我们从19个国家的132名成年人收集数据，并将这些信号与心理健康问卷结果进行比较。研究发现，血流和组织活动的模式与压力相关症状相关。该方法可能有助于开发简单、无创的日常心理健康监测工具。代码和数据集公开可用：https://github.com/leduckhai/Wearable_LDF-FS
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of self-reported mental health assessments, this study investigates whether wearable sensors can objectively measure mental states by detecting physiological signals. Using laser Doppler flowmetry and fluorescence spectroscopy to capture fingertip blood flow and tissue activity, the researchers collected data from 132 adults across 19 countries and correlated these signals with standardized mental health questionnaire scores. Results showed statistically significant associations between specific patterns in blood flow and tissue activity and stress-related symptoms, suggesting that such wearable sensors could enable non-invasive, continuous mental health monitoring in real-world settings.
鉴于自评问卷在精神健康评估中的局限性，本研究探索通过可穿戴传感器采集生理信号客观评估精神健康的可能性。方法采用激光多普勒血流仪与荧光光谱仪，从132名来自19个国家的成年人指尖采集微小的血流与组织活动变化，并与标准化精神健康问卷得分进行对比。实验结果表明，特定血流与组织活动模式与压力相关症状存在显著统计关联，提示该方法有望实现日常生活中无创、连续的精神健康监测。
Fast Multi-dimensional Refusal Subspaces via RFM-AGOP
Authors: Thomas Winninger
First: 2026-07-02T16:31:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:31:56+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the Mechanistic Interpretability Workshop at the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning, Seoul, South Korea, 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Steering and monitoring activations in Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for both safety and interpretability. Early work assumed behaviours are encoded along single linear directions, but recent findings suggest complex behaviours, such as the refusal to answer harmful queries, live in multi-dimensional subspaces. However, existing methods for extracting these subspaces are computationally expensive, which becomes prohibitive on reasoning models who produce long reasoning traces. By adapting the Recursive Feature Machine (RFM) algorithm -- which can be computed efficiently -- with a probe-informed initialization, we are able to identify the multi-dimensional refusal subspace in seconds, on reasoning (Qwen 3) and non-reasoning (Qwen 2.5) models. While RFM allows for faster subspace identification, it also showed better performances on the ablation task than its alternatives. More work is planned to better understand the relations between subspaces found by different methods. If confirmed, RFM could be a cheap and scalable complement to existing subspace-extraction methods in LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过RFM-AGOP快速识别多维拒绝子空间
在大型语言模型（LLMs）中，引导和监控激活状态正日益用于安全性和可解释性。早期研究假设行为沿单一线性方向编码，但近期发现复杂行为（如拒绝回答有害查询）存在于多维子空间中。然而，现有提取这些子空间的方法计算成本高昂，对生成长推理轨迹的推理模型尤为不切实际。通过将可高效计算的递归特征机（RFM）算法与探针引导初始化相结合，我们能在数秒内识别推理模型（Qwen 3）和非推理模型（Qwen 2.5）的多维拒绝子空间。RFM不仅加速了子空间识别，还在消融任务中表现优于其他方法。未来计划进一步研究不同方法所发现子空间之间的关系。若验证成立，RFM或可作为现有LLM子空间提取方法的低成本、可扩展补充。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently monitor and steer harmful behaviors in large language models, this work addresses the challenge that refusal behaviors reside in multi-dimensional subspaces rather than single directions. It adapts the Recursive Feature Machine (RFM) algorithm, leveraging probe-informed initialization for computational efficiency, to rapidly identify these subspaces. Experiments on both reasoning (Qwen 3) and non-reasoning (Qwen 2.5) models show RFM can extract refusal subspaces within seconds, outperforming alternatives in ablation tasks and demonstrating scalability for long reasoning traces.
为高效监控和引导大语言模型中的有害行为，该研究针对拒绝行为存在于多维子空间而非单一方向的挑战，改进了递归特征机（RFM）算法，结合探针引导初始化以快速识别这些子空间。在推理型（Qwen 3）和非推理型（Qwen 2.5）模型上的实验表明，RFM可在数秒内提取多维拒绝子空间，且在消融任务中优于其他方法，展现出计算高效性和性能优势，有望成为现有子空间提取方法的低成本可扩展补充。
The Token Not Taken: Sampling, State, and the Stochasticity of AI Agents
Authors: Muhammad Zia Hydari, Raja Iqbal
First: 2026-06-08T03:53:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:28:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic AI systems can behave differently across runs: the same request may produce a different plan, a different tool call, a different code edit, or a different final answer. Such variability arises from several layers that are often conflated. At the core of many current agents is a foundation model, a large pretrained model adaptable to many downstream tasks, embedded in an orchestration loop that plans, calls tools, observes results, and updates state. One explicit intrinsic source of variability in such systems is token generation: the model computes scores over possible next tokens, the scores are converted into probabilities, and a decoder may sample tokens using a pseudo-random number generator. A small sampled token difference can then cascade downstream into a different tool call, code path, search query, or agent state. Other sources of variability are extrinsic to token sampling, including changing environments, live data, serving infrastructure, batch effects, and numerical details. By separating these layers, this tutorial clarifies what it means to call agentic AI systems stochastic, when such variability can be reproduced under matched conditions, and why deterministic execution need not imply identical behavior in deployed settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：未被选中的令牌：采样、状态与AI代理的随机性
代理型AI系统在不同运行中可能表现不同：相同的请求可能产生不同的计划、不同的工具调用、不同的代码修改或不同的最终答案。这种变异性源于多个常被混淆的层次。当前许多代理的核心是一个基础模型——一个可适应多种下游任务的大型预训练模型，嵌入在一个编排循环中，负责规划、调用工具、观察结果并更新状态。此类系统中一个明确的内在变异性来源是令牌生成：模型计算可能下一个令牌的分数，分数转换为概率，解码器可能使用伪随机数生成器采样令牌。一个小的采样令牌差异可能随后级联导致不同的工具调用、代码路径、搜索查询或代理状态。其他变异性来源则与令牌采样无关，包括变化的环境、实时数据、服务基础设施、批处理效应和数值细节。通过分离这些层次，本教程阐明了称代理型AI系统为随机性的含义，说明在匹配条件下此类变异性何时可重现，以及为何确定性执行并不意味着在部署环境中行为完全一致。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the stochastic nature of agentic AI systems, which exhibit behavioral variability across runs due to multiple interacting sources of randomness. The core source is token sampling during language model generation, where probabilistic token selection, influenced by pseudo-random number generators, can lead to divergent downstream actions such as different tool calls or code paths. Additional variability arises from external factors including dynamic environments, live data, infrastructure, and numerical precision. Through systematic layering and separation of these components, the paper clarifies when stochasticity is intrinsic versus extrinsic, and demonstrates that deterministic execution does not guarantee identical behavior in real-world deployments.
本文探讨了代理型AI系统在相同输入下为何行为不一致，通过剖析多种随机性来源，指出令牌采样是核心内在随机源，模型对下一令牌的概率采样可能导致下游行为（如不同工具调用或代码路径）的差异。同时区分了环境变化、实时数据和基础设施等外在随机因素。通过分层分析，论文阐明了在可控条件下随机行为可复现的条件，并指出确定性执行并不意味着在实际部署中行为完全一致，因多重因素叠加所致。
WikiCLIP: An Efficient Contrastive Baseline for Open-domain Visual Entity Recognition
Authors: Shan Ning, Longtian Qiu, Jiaxuan Sun, Xuming He
First: 2026-03-10T17:18:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:25:34+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR26, codes and weights are publicly available
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Open-domain visual entity recognition (VER) seeks to associate images with entities in encyclopedic knowledge bases such as Wikipedia. Recent generative methods tailored for VER demonstrate strong performance but incur high computational costs, limiting their scalability and practical deployment. In this work, we revisit the contrastive paradigm for VER and introduce WikiCLIP, a simple yet effective framework that establishes a strong and efficient baseline for open-domain VER. WikiCLIP leverages large language model embeddings as knowledge-rich entity representations and enhances them with a Vision-Guided Knowledge Adaptor (VGKA) that aligns textual semantics with visual cues at the patch level. To further encourage fine-grained discrimination, a Hard Negative Synthesis Mechanism generates visually similar but semantically distinct negatives during training. Experimental results on popular open-domain VER benchmarks, such as OVEN, demonstrate that WikiCLIP significantly outperforms strong baselines. Specifically, WikiCLIP achieves a 16\% improvement on the challenging OVEN unseen set, while reducing inference latency by nearly 100 times compared with the leading generative model, AutoVER. The project page is available at https://artanic30.github.io/project_pages/WikiCLIP/
中文标题/摘要
标题：WikiCLIP：面向开放域视觉实体识别的高效对比基线
开放域视觉实体识别（VER）旨在将图像与维基百科等百科知识库中的实体关联。近期为VER定制的生成方法虽表现强劲，但计算成本高昂，限制了其可扩展性和实际部署。本文重新审视VER的对比范式，提出WikiCLIP——一个简单而有效的框架，为开放域VER建立了强大且高效的基线。WikiCLIP利用大语言模型嵌入作为富含知识的实体表示，并通过视觉引导知识适配器（VGKA）在图像块级别对齐文本语义与视觉线索。为促进细粒度判别，引入困难负样本合成机制，在训练中生成视觉相似但语义不同的负样本。在OVEN等主流开放域VER基准测试中，实验结果表明WikiCLIP显著优于强基线方法。具体而言，WikiCLIP在具有挑战性的OVEN未见集上性能提升16%，推理延迟较领先生成模型AutoVER降低近100倍。项目页面见：https://artanic30.github.io/project_pages/WikiCLIP/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of recent generative methods in open-domain visual entity recognition, this work revisits contrastive learning and proposes WikiCLIP, a lightweight framework that uses large language model embeddings as entity representations and enhances them via a Vision-Guided Knowledge Adaptor to align text and visual features at the patch level. It further employs a Hard Negative Synthesis Mechanism to generate semantically distinct but visually similar negatives, improving fine-grained discrimination. Experiments on OVEN show WikiCLIP outperforms strong baselines by 16% on the unseen set while reducing inference latency by nearly 100 times compared to AutoVER.
为解决当前开放域视觉实体识别中生成式方法计算成本过高的问题，本文重新审视对比学习范式，提出WikiCLIP框架，利用大语言模型嵌入作为实体表征，并通过视觉引导知识适配器实现图文在patch级对齐。同时引入难负例合成机制，生成视觉相似但语义不同的负样本以增强判别能力。在OVEN数据集上实验表明，WikiCLIP在未见集上性能提升16%，推理延迟较最优生成模型AutoVER降低近100倍。
WattGPU: Predicting Inference Power and Latency on Unseen GPUs and LLMs
Authors: Mauricio Fadel Argerich, Jonathan Fürst, Marta Patiño-Martínez
Venue: IJCAI 2026
First: 2026-07-02T16:25:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:25:34+00:00
Comments: Accepted at 1st Workshop on Sustainability and Resource-Efficiency of Artificial Intelligence @ IJCAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large Language Model (LLM) inference workloads are a rapidly growing contributor to data center energy consumption. Optimizing these deployments requires matching specific LLMs to the most efficient GPUs, but operators currently lack the tools to do so without exhaustively profiling each combination. While some predictive models exist, they still require profiling data and struggle to generalize to hardware unseen during training. To address this, we introduce \textit{WattGPU}, featuring two predictive models for mean GPU power draw and Inter-Token Latency (ITL). Our approach leverages only publicly available LLM metadata and GPU specifications, eliminating the need for hardware access or profiling while enabling generalization to unseen NVIDIA server-grade GPUs and LLMs. We evaluate our models using rigorous leave-one-GPU-out and leave-one-LLM-out cross-validation on a dataset of 42 open-source LLMs (0.1B--27B parameters) and 8 GPUs under both offline and server scenarios. The mean power draw model achieves a median absolute percentage error of $\leq3.4\%$ for offline and $\leq13.5\%$ for server scenarios on unseen GPUs, while the latency model achieves $\leq8.5\%$ in server mode, both maintaining strong GPU ranking correlations for server scenarios (Kendall $τ\geq0.76$). Compared to standard physically grounded baselines -- Load-Scaled Thermal Design Power (TDP) for power draw and roofline for latency -- our models reduce median absolute percentage error by approximately 4$\times$ on unseen LLM-GPU combinations for server scenarios or approximately 2$\times$ for completely unseen GPUs. WattGPU's data and code are publicly available at https://github.com/maufadel/wattgpu.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WattGPU：预测未见GPU和大语言模型的推理功耗与延迟
大语言模型（LLM）推理工作负载正迅速成为数据中心能耗的主要贡献者。优化这些部署需要将特定LLM与最高效的GPU匹配，但当前运营商缺乏无需对每种组合进行详尽性能分析即可完成此任务的工具。虽然存在一些预测模型，但它们仍需性能分析数据，并难以泛化到训练期间未见过的硬件。为解决此问题，我们引入了\textit{WattGPU}，包含两个预测模型，分别用于预测平均GPU功耗和跨Token延迟（ITL）。我们的方法仅依赖公开可用的LLM元数据和GPU规格，无需硬件访问或性能分析，同时支持泛化至未见的NVIDIA服务器级GPU和LLM。我们在包含42个开源LLM（0.1B至27B参数）和8种GPU的数据集上，通过严格的“留一GPU”和“留一LLM”交叉验证，在离线和服务器两种场景下评估了模型性能。平均功耗模型在未见GPU上的中位绝对百分比误差分别为离线场景≤3.4%、服务器场景≤13.5%；延迟模型在服务器模式下误差≤8.5%，且在服务器场景下均保持强GPU排序相关性（Kendall τ≥0.76）。与标准物理基础基线（功耗使用Load-Scaled TDP，延迟使用roofline）相比，我们的模型在未见LLM-GPU组合的服务器场景下将中位绝对百分比误差降低约4倍，或在完全未见GPU时降低约2倍。WattGPU的数据和代码已公开于https://github.com/maufadel/wattgpu。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to optimize energy use in data centers hosting large language model (LLM) inference workloads, WattGPU introduces two predictive models that estimate GPU power draw and inter-token latency without requiring hardware profiling. The models rely solely on publicly available LLM metadata and GPU specifications, enabling generalization to unseen NVIDIA GPUs and LLMs. Evaluated via leave-one-GPU-out and leave-one-LLM-out cross-validation across 42 LLMs and 8 GPUs, the power model achieves median absolute percentage errors of ≤3.4% (offline) and ≤13.5% (server), while the latency model reaches ≤8.5% in server mode, with strong GPU ranking correlations (Kendall τ ≥ 0.76). Compared to baselines like TDP and roofline, WattGPU reduces errors by 4× for unseen LLM-GPU pairs and 2× for completely unseen GPUs.
为应对数据中心中大型语言模型推理工作负载带来的能源消耗激增问题，WattGPU提出两种无需硬件访问或配置采样的预测模型，分别用于估算GPU功耗与跨token延迟。模型仅依赖公开的LLM元数据与GPU规格，在42个开源LLM和8个GPU的交叉验证数据集上表现良好，可泛化至未见的NVIDIA服务器级GPU与LLM。实验显示，功耗模型在服务器场景下中位绝对百分比误差≤13.5%，延迟模型≤8.5%，且GPU排序相关性高达Kendall τ≥0.76。相比TDP与roofline等物理模型，WattGPU在未见组合上误差降低4倍，在完全未见GPU上降低2倍。
DecompRL: Solving Harder Problems by Learning Modular Code Generation
Authors: Juliette Decugis, Fabian Gloeckle, Francis Bach, Taco Cohen, Gabriel Synnaeve
First: 2026-07-02T16:25:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:25:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
How can Large Language Models (LLMs) solve problems they currently cannot? Repeated sampling scales test-time compute but GPU cost grows linearly with attempts, while reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards improves single-attempt accuracy at the expense of sample diversity. Both strategies ultimately fail when the base policy has near-zero probability of producing a correct solution: no amount of sampling or gradient signal can overcome a search space that is simply too large. We take a different approach: rather than sampling harder, we make the task easier by decomposing problems into smaller, independently solvable sub-functions whose implementations can be recombined. Since off-the-shelf models are not trained for this modular generation, we introduce DecompRL, an RL algorithm that explicitly learns to decompose and implement hierarchical code structures. Recombining $k$ implementations of $n$ modules yields up to $k^{n}$ candidate solutions, shifting the bottleneck from GPU inference to cheap CPU evaluation and cutting GPU token cost by $\sim$50$\times$. On LiveCodeBench and CodeContests (Qwen~2.5~7B, Code World Model~32B), DecompRL outperforms standard and diversity-optimized RL baselines beyond $10^5$ tokens per problem, solving problems that standard generation cannot reach.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DecompRL：通过学习模块化代码生成解决更难的问题
大型语言模型（LLM）如何解决当前无法处理的问题？重复采样虽可扩展测试时计算量，但GPU成本随尝试次数线性增长；而使用可验证奖励的强化学习（RL）虽能提升单次尝试的准确性，却牺牲了样本多样性。当基础策略几乎不可能生成正确解时，这两种策略均告失败：无论采样多少次或梯度信号多强，都无法克服搜索空间过大这一根本问题。我们采取不同方法：不增加难度，而是通过将问题分解为可独立求解的子函数，并重新组合其实现，使任务更易处理。由于现成模型未针对模块化生成进行训练，我们提出DecompRL，一种明确学习分解并实现分层代码结构的强化学习算法。将n个模块的k种实现重新组合，最多可生成k^n个候选解，从而将瓶颈从GPU推理转移到廉价CPU评估，GPU token成本降低约50倍。在LiveCodeBench和CodeContests（Qwen~2.5~7B、Code World Model~32B）上，DecompRL在每题超10^5 token时超越标准及多样性优化的RL基线，解决标准生成无法触及的问题。
Summary / 总结
To address the limitation that Large Language Models struggle with hard problems due to insufficient search space coverage, DecompRL introduces a modular decomposition strategy: it breaks complex tasks into smaller, independently solvable sub-functions and learns to generate and recombine their implementations via reinforcement learning. This approach bypasses the need for extensive sampling or gradient-based optimization over vast solution spaces, instead shifting computational bottlenecks from GPU inference to cheap CPU evaluation. Experimental results on LiveCodeBench and CodeContests show that DecompRL achieves up to 50x reduction in GPU token cost and solves problems that standard RL or diversity-optimized methods cannot reach, even when exceeding 100,000 tokens per problem.
为解决大语言模型（LLM）因搜索空间过大而难以解决复杂问题的瓶颈，作者提出DecompRL算法，通过学习将复杂任务分解为可独立求解的子函数并重组其实现，从而降低问题难度。与依赖重复采样或可验证奖励的强化学习方法不同，DecompRL将计算瓶颈从GPU推理转移到廉价CPU评估，大幅降低GPU token成本约50倍。在LiveCodeBench和CodeContests上的实验表明，当每题消耗超过10^5 tokens时，DecompRL显著优于标准及多样性优化的强化学习基线，解决了标准生成无法触及的问题。
Steerability via constraints: a substrate for scalable oversight of coding agents
Authors: Thomas Winninger
First: 2026-07-02T16:24:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:24:47+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the Deep Learning for Code Workshop at the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning, Seoul, South Korea, 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Coding agents are capable; human oversight is the bottleneck. Unconstrained agents introduce security risks, erode codebase scalability, and make human review increasingly costly. We argue that the same methods used for decades to manage large human engineering teams: access control, network policies, strict coding conventions enforced by tooling; transfer directly to coding agents, and are cheaper (in token) than recent agentic scaffolding. We sketch a start-to-end system on this principle, and report a controlled experiment in scalable oversight: a small reviewer (Gemma 4 e4b) inspects a Python codebase containing 11 inserted backdoors. Recall rises from 54.5% (unconstrained, no tools) to 90.9% (constrained substrate plus a ~200-LoC `docs` CLI), with substrate and tools contributing independently. We choose Python deliberately: substrate-level oversight gains are largest where the language gives the fewest guarantees by default; the principles extend to languages like Rust.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过约束实现可控性：为代码代理提供可扩展监督的基础
代码代理能力强大，但人类监督是瓶颈。无约束代理会引入安全风险、削弱代码库可扩展性，并使人工审查成本日益高昂。我们认为，几十年来用于管理大型人类工程团队的方法——访问控制、网络策略、通过工具强制执行的严格编码规范——可直接应用于代码代理，且在token成本上比近期的代理框架更便宜。我们基于此原则草拟了一套端到端系统，并报告了一项可扩展监督的受控实验：小型审查员（Gemma 4 e4b）审查包含11个后门的Python代码库，召回率从无约束且无工具的54.5%提升至使用约束基础架构加约200行代码的`docs`命令行工具后的90.9%，基础架构与工具各自独立贡献。我们选择Python是出于刻意考虑：语言默认提供最少保障时，基础架构层面的监督收益最大；该原则可扩展至Rust等语言。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the challenge of scaling human oversight for autonomous coding agents, which currently suffer from security risks, poor codebase scalability, and high review costs when unconstrained. It proposes applying traditional engineering practices—such as access control, network policies, and tool-enforced coding conventions—to coding agents, arguing these are more cost-effective in token usage than recent agentic scaffolding. The authors demonstrate this via a controlled experiment where a small reviewer (Gemma 4 e4b) evaluated a Python codebase with 11 backdoors; recall improved from 54.5% to 90.9% when using a constrained substrate and a 200-line CLI tool, with each component contributing independently. Python was chosen because its minimal default guarantees maximize the benefit of substrate-level oversight, though the approach is generalizable to languages like Rust.
该论文旨在解决自主编程代理在缺乏约束时引发的安全风险、可扩展性差和人工审查成本高的问题。作者提出将传统工程管理方法——如访问控制、网络策略和工具强制的编码规范——应用于编程代理，认为这些方法比近期的代理框架更经济高效。实验中，一个小型审查员（Gemma 4 e4b）审查包含11个后门的Python代码库，召回率从无约束的54.5%提升至使用约束基底加约200行CLI工具后的90.9%，且各组件独立贡献效果。选择Python因其默认保障最少，但该原则可扩展至Rust等语言。
Bringing Agentic Search to Earth Observation Data Discovery
Authors: Minghan Yu, Youran Sun, Chugang Yi, Yixin Wen, Haizhao Yang
First: 2026-07-02T16:24:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:24:16+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 1 figure, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
NASA and its data centers hold thousands of geoscience datasets and tools like Worldview, Giovanni, the Science Discovery Engine, and Harmony. Finding the right one is hard even for domain experts. We present an agentic search system, deployed as a public service for the geoscience community, that takes a natural-language research query and returns the matching datasets and tools. We demonstrate that, in the era of large language models, the latent value of knowledge graphs (KGs) can be substantially amplified through agentic search. From the NASA Earth Observation Knowledge Graph (NASA EO-KG) we derive NASA-EO-Bench, an open benchmark of 47k query-dataset pairs (21k task-based queries). A neural scorer fine-tuned on NASA-EO-Bench beats cosine and BM25 baselines. Further combining it with BM25 via score fusion raises both Recall@10 (R@10) and MRR by over 5x. On top of this supervised pipeline, we add a zero-shot agentic reranking stage that, without any additional training, lifts MRR by 28% on a stratified N=200 subset, showing that LLM reasoning is complementary to supervised retrieval.
中文标题/摘要
标题：将代理搜索引入地球观测数据发现
NASA及其数据中心拥有数千个地球科学数据集和工具，如Worldview、Giovanni、科学发现引擎和Harmony。即使领域专家也难以找到合适的数据集。我们提出了一种代理搜索系统，作为面向地球科学社区的公共服务，该系统接受自然语言研究查询并返回匹配的数据集和工具。我们证明，在大语言模型时代，知识图谱（KGs）的潜在价值可通过代理搜索显著放大。基于NASA地球观测知识图谱（NASA EO-KG），我们构建了NASA-EO-Bench，一个包含47,000个查询-数据集对（其中21,000个为任务导向查询）的开放基准。一个在NASA-EO-Bench上微调的神经评分器优于余弦和BM25基线。进一步结合BM25通过评分融合，使Recall@10（R@10）和MRR提升超过5倍。在此监督管道之上，我们增加了一个零样本代理重排序阶段，无需额外训练，即可在分层N=200子集上将MRR提升28%，表明LLM推理与监督检索具有互补性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty domain experts face in locating relevant Earth observation datasets among NASA’s vast collection, the authors introduce an agentic search system that translates natural language queries into precise dataset/tool matches. Leveraging the NASA Earth Observation Knowledge Graph, they construct NASA-EO-Bench, a benchmark of 47k query-dataset pairs, and train a neural scorer that outperforms cosine and BM25 baselines. Combining this scorer with BM25 via score fusion boosts Recall@10 and MRR by over 5x. Adding a zero-shot agentic reranking stage using LLM reasoning further improves MRR by 28% on a subset, demonstrating the complementary value of LLMs in agentic retrieval.
为了解决领域专家在NASA海量地球观测数据中难以定位相关数据集的问题，作者构建了一个基于自然语言查询的代理搜索系统，可精准匹配数据集与工具。基于NASA地球观测知识图谱，他们创建了包含47k组查询-数据对的NASA-EO-Bench基准，用于训练和评估神经评分器，其性能优于余弦和BM25基线。将该评分器与BM25结合进行分数融合，使Recall@10和MRR提升超5倍。在该监督流程基础上，增加零样本代理重排序阶段，仅通过LLM推理即可在200样本子集上使MRR提升28%，表明LLM推理与监督检索具有互补性。
Transformer Geometry Observatory TGO-II: Representational Similarity Observatory
Authors: Kaustubh Kapil, Kishor P. Upla
First: 2026-07-02T16:22:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:22:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While Vision Transformers have achieved remarkable success across computer vision and language applications, the geometric evolution of their internal representations throughout training remains insufficiently understood. Existing analyses primarily focus on attention mechanisms and downstream performance, leaving the evolution of representation geometry largely unexplored. In this work, we present Transformer Geometry Observatory-II (TGO-II), a representation geometry analysis framework designed to investigate how Transformer representations evolve during supervised training. TGO-II analyzes Vision Transformer (ViT-Small/16) representations using Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA), Singular Vector Canonical Correlation Analysis (SVCCA), Two-Nearest Neighbor Intrinsic Dimensionality (TwoNN-ID), and token covariance analysis. Our experiments reveal three key observations. First, both CKA and SVCCA progressively decrease throughout training, indicating increasing representational specialization across Transformer layers. Second, intrinsic dimensionality consistently increases before stabilizing, suggesting progressive expansion of the representation manifold into a larger set of locally accessible degrees of freedom. Third, token covariance and coupling analyses demonstrate that strong token interaction structure persists throughout training, challenging the hypothesis that increasing representational complexity arises primarily from progressive token independence. These findings suggest that representation complexity and layer specialization emerge simultaneously during training. Manifold expansion appears to occur without token decoupling. Together, these observations motivate a new hypothesis in which Vision Transformers increase representational complexity through progressively richer transformations while preserving strong token interaction structure during learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Transformer几何观测站TGO-II：表征相似性观测站
尽管视觉Transformer在计算机视觉和语言应用中取得了显著成功，但其内部表征在训练过程中的几何演化仍不甚明了。现有分析主要聚焦于注意力机制和下游性能，对表征几何演化的研究尚属空白。本文提出Transformer几何观测站-II（TGO-II），一个用于研究Transformer表征在监督训练中如何演化的分析框架。TGO-II采用中心核对齐（CKA）、奇异向量典型相关分析（SVCCA）、两最近邻内在维度（TwoNN-ID）及token协方差分析，对视觉Transformer（ViT-Small/16）表征进行分析。实验揭示三个关键发现：第一，CKA与SVCCA在训练过程中持续下降，表明Transformer各层表征逐渐专业化；第二，内在维度持续上升后趋于稳定，暗示表征流形逐步扩展至更多局部可访问的自由度；第三，token协方差与耦合分析表明，强token交互结构贯穿训练始终，挑战了“表征复杂度主要源于token逐步独立”的假设。这些发现表明，表征复杂度与层间专业化在训练中同步出现，流形扩展似乎无需token解耦。上述观察共同提出新假设：视觉Transformer通过逐步更丰富的变换提升表征复杂度，同时在学习过程中保持强token交互结构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of understanding regarding how Vision Transformer representations evolve geometrically during training, this study introduces TGO-II, a framework that analyzes internal representation changes using CKA, SVCCA, TwoNN-ID, and token covariance. Experiments on ViT-Small/16 show that CKA and SVCCA decrease over time, indicating growing layer specialization; intrinsic dimensionality rises and stabilizes, suggesting manifold expansion; and token covariance remains high, contradicting the idea that token independence drives complexity. These results imply that representational complexity and specialization develop together without token decoupling.
为理解视觉Transformer在训练过程中内部表示的几何演化，本研究提出TGO-II框架，结合CKA、SVCCA、TwoNN-ID和Token协方差分析方法进行研究。实验表明，CKA与SVCCA随训练下降，反映层间表示逐渐专业化；内在维度先上升后稳定，说明表示流形扩展；Token协方差保持高位，反驳了表示复杂性源于Token独立性的假设。结果表明，表示复杂性与层专业化同步增长，且无需Token解耦。
Spintronics for image recognition: performance benchmarking via data-driven simulations
Authors: Anatole Moureaux, Chloé Chopin, Simon de Wergifosse, Laurent Jacques, Flavio Abreu Araujo
First: 2023-08-10T18:09:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:22:18+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a demonstration of image classification using an extreme learning machine (ELM) based on a unique simulated magnetic tunnel junction (MTJ) delayed in time. As the ground state of the MTJ is a magnetic vortex, we refer to it as a vortex-based spin-torque oscillator (STVO). The dynamics of the magnetic vortex is simulated with a model called the data-driven Thiele equation approach (DD-TEA). This allows to avoid the constraints associated with repeated experimental manipulation for hyperparameters search and benchmarking. We showcase the versatility of our implementation by using it successfully for classification tasks on the MNIST, EMNIST-letters and Fashion MNIST datasets. Through simulations, we show that within an ELM with a sufficient number of parameters, the performance reached using the STVO dynamics as a source of nonlinearity is equivalent to the ones obtained with classical software activation functions such as the reLU and the sigmoid. While achieving state-of-the-art accuracy levels on the MNIST dataset, our model's performance on EMNIST-letters and Fashion MNIST is lower due to the simplicity of the network architecture and the increased complexity of the data. We expect that the DD-TEA framework will enable the exploration of deeper and more complex STVO-based architectures, ultimately leading to improved classification accuracy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于图像识别的自旋电子学：通过数据驱动模拟进行性能基准测试
我们展示了一种基于独特时间延迟模拟磁隧道结（MTJ）的极限学习机（ELM）进行图像分类的方法。由于MTJ的基态为磁涡旋，我们称之为基于涡旋的自旋扭矩振荡器（STVO）。通过称为数据驱动Thiele方程方法（DD-TEA）的模型模拟磁涡旋动力学，从而避免了在超参数搜索和基准测试中反复实验操作的约束。我们通过在MNIST、EMNIST-letters和Fashion MNIST数据集上成功完成分类任务，展示了该实现的多功能性。模拟结果表明，在参数数量足够的情况下，使用STVO动力学作为非线性源的ELM性能与经典软件激活函数（如ReLU和Sigmoid）相当。尽管在MNIST数据集上达到最先进精度水平，但在EMNIST-letters和Fashion MNIST数据集上的表现较低，这是由于网络架构较简单且数据复杂度更高所致。我们预期DD-TEA框架将有助于探索更深层、更复杂的STVO架构，最终提升分类精度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of spintronic devices for neuromorphic computing, this study explores using a simulated magnetic tunnel junction with vortex dynamics as a source of nonlinearity in image classification. The authors employ a data-driven Thiele equation approach to model the magnetic vortex behavior without requiring repeated physical experiments, enabling efficient hyperparameter tuning. Applied to MNIST, EMNIST-letters, and Fashion MNIST, their extreme learning machine achieves performance comparable to classical activation functions like ReLU and sigmoid, reaching state-of-the-art accuracy on MNIST but lower accuracy on more complex datasets due to architectural simplicity. The framework is shown to be scalable, with potential for future improvements through deeper architectures.
为探索自旋电子器件在神经网络中的潜力，该研究利用模拟磁隧道结（MTJ）中的磁涡旋作为自旋扭矩振荡器（STVO），为其提供非线性激活函数，应用于极限学习机（ELM）。方法采用数据驱动的蒂勒方程方法（DD-TEA）模拟涡旋动力学，避免重复实验调参，提升效率。实验表明，基于STVO的ELM在MNIST数据集上性能媲美ReLU和Sigmoid等经典激活函数，达到当前最优准确率；但在EMNIST-letters和Fashion MNIST等更复杂数据集上表现较弱，因网络结构较简单。作者认为，扩展DD-TEA框架至更深架构有望提升分类精度。
Know Your Source: A Public Knowledge Store for Media Background Checks
Authors: Benjamin Nichols, Michael Schlichtkrull, Nedjma Ousidhoum
First: 2026-07-02T16:20:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:20:28+00:00
Comments: Code and Data: https://github.com/nedjmaou/mediaref
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
LLM-based retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly used for automated fact-checking (AFC) and related tasks. By grounding LLM outputs in retrieved evidence, RAG-based systems provide transparent justifications while allowing external information to be updated independently of the underlying model. However, existing approaches often assume retrieved evidence is reliable, although real-world information may be conflicting, outdated, and can originate from unreliable or biased sources. Recent work on *source-critical reasoning* addresses this challenge through media background checks (MBCs) (Schlichtkrull, 2024), which assess the credibility of evidence sources to support downstream fact verification. However, generating MBCs relies on costly proprietary search APIs, limiting reproducibility. To mitigate this issue, we introduce MEDIAREF, a publicly available knowledge store of web-sourced documents that enables reproducible, low-cost evaluation of MBC generation across 200 media sources. We describe a reproducible methodology for constructing and updating the collection, assess widely used LLMs on the MBC generation task, and demonstrate that MEDIAREF supports higher-quality MBC generation through both automatic and qualitative evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：了解你的来源：面向媒体背景核查的公共知识库
基于大语言模型的检索增强生成（RAG）正越来越多地用于自动化事实核查（AFC）及相关任务。通过将LLM输出与检索到的证据相结合，RAG系统提供透明的解释，同时允许外部信息独立于基础模型进行更新。然而，现有方法通常假设检索到的证据是可靠的，而现实世界的信息可能存在冲突、过时，且可能来自不可靠或有偏见的来源。近期关于*来源批判性推理*的研究通过媒体背景核查（MBCs）（Schlichtkrull, 2024）应对这一挑战，评估证据来源的可信度以支持下游事实验证。但生成MBCs依赖昂贵的专有搜索API，限制了可复现性。为缓解此问题，我们引入MEDIAREF，一个公开的网络文档知识库，支持在200个媒体来源上以低成本、可复现的方式评估MBC生成。我们描述了构建和更新该集合的可复现方法，评估了广泛使用的LLM在MBC生成任务上的表现，并证明MEDIAREF通过自动和定性评估支持更高质量的MBC生成。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to verify the credibility of evidence sources in LLM-based retrieval-augmented generation systems, this work introduces MEDIAREF, a public knowledge store of web-sourced documents from 200 media outlets, enabling reproducible and low-cost evaluation of media background checks (MBCs). The method involves constructing and maintaining a curated, updateable corpus of documents, then evaluating widely used LLMs on their ability to generate MBCs using this dataset. Experimental results show that MEDIAREF supports higher-quality MBC generation compared to proprietary search APIs, with both automatic metrics and qualitative assessments confirming improved source-critical reasoning capabilities.
为提升基于大语言模型的事实核查系统中证据来源的可信度评估能力，本研究提出MEDIAREF，一个包含200家媒体来源的公开网络文档知识库，支持可复现且低成本的媒体背景检查（MBC）生成评估。方法包括构建并持续更新该文档集合，随后评估主流大语言模型在该数据集上的MBC生成表现。实验结果表明，相比专有搜索API，MEDIAREF显著提升MBC生成质量，自动评估与人工评估均验证其在源可信度推理方面的优越性。
Perception-aware Sampling for Scatterplot Visualizations
Authors: Zafeiria Moumoulidou, Hamza Elhamdadi, Ke Yang, Subrata Mitra, Cindy Xiong Bearfield, Alexandra Meliou
First: 2025-04-29T02:14:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:20:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Visualizing data is often a crucial first step in data analytics workflows, but growing data sizes pose challenges due to computational and visual perception limitations. As a result, data analysts commonly down-sample their data and work with subsets. Deriving representative samples, however, remains a challenge. This paper focuses on scatterplots, a widely-used visualization type, and introduces a novel sampling objective -- perception-awareness -- aiming to improve sample efficacy by targeting humans' perception of a visualization. We make the following contributions: (1) We propose perception-augmented databases and design PAwS: a novel perception-aware sampling method for scatterplots that leverages saliency maps -- a computer vision tool for predicting areas of attention focus in visualizations -- and models perception-awareness via saliency, density, and coverage objectives. (2) We design ApproPAwS: a fast, perception-aware method for approximate visualizations, which exploits the fact that small visual perturbations are often imperceptible to humans. (3) We introduce the concept of perceptual similarity as a metric for sample quality, and present a novel method that compares saliency maps to measure it. (4) Our extensive experimental evaluation shows that our methods consistently outperform prior art in producing samples with high perceptual similarity, while ApproPAwS achieves up to 100x speed-ups with minimal loss in visual fidelity. Our user study shows that PAwS is often preferred by humans, validating our quantitative findings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向感知的散点图采样方法
数据可视化通常是数据分析工作流程中的关键第一步，但日益增长的数据规模因计算和视觉感知限制而带来挑战。因此，数据分析师常对数据进行下采样并处理子集。然而，如何获得代表性样本仍是一个难题。本文聚焦于广泛使用的散点图可视化类型，提出了一种新颖的采样目标——感知意识，旨在通过针对人类对可视化的感知来提升样本效果。 我们做出以下贡献：(1) 提出感知增强数据库，设计PAwS：一种面向散点图的新型感知采样方法，利用显著性图——一种计算机视觉工具，用于预测可视化中的注意力区域——并通过显著性、密度和覆盖度目标建模感知意识。(2) 设计ApproPAwS：一种快速的感知采样方法，用于近似可视化，利用人类对微小视觉扰动通常不敏感的事实。(3) 引入感知相似性作为样本质量的度量，并提出一种新颖方法，通过比较显著性图来衡量它。(4) 我们的广泛实验评估表明，我们的方法在生成高感知相似度样本方面持续优于现有方法，而ApproPAwS可实现高达100倍的速度提升，且视觉保真度损失极小。用户研究表明，PAwS常被人类偏好，验证了我们的定量结果。

Driven by the need to efficiently visualize large datasets while preserving human perceptual fidelity, this paper introduces PAwS, a perception-aware sampling method for scatterplots that combines saliency maps, density, and coverage to guide sample selection. It also presents ApproPAwS, a faster approximation leveraging human visual tolerance to small perturbations. Experiments demonstrate that PAwS produces samples with higher perceptual similarity than prior methods, while ApproPAwS achieves up to 100x speedup with negligible visual degradation, and user studies confirm human preference for PAwS samples.
为应对大规模数据可视化中计算与感知限制的问题，本文提出PAwS方法，通过结合显著性图、密度与覆盖度实现感知导向的散点图采样，并设计快速近似方法ApproPAwS利用人类对微小视觉扰动的不敏感性。实验表明PAwS在感知相似性上显著优于现有方法，ApproPAwS速度提升最高达100倍且视觉保真度损失极小，用户研究也证实人类更偏好PAwS生成的样本。
HULAT2 at MER-TRANS 2026: Governed Multi-Agent Simplification for Spanish Easy-to-Read Generation
Authors: Lourdes Moreno, Paloma Martínez, Marco Antonio Sanchez-Escudero, Miguel Domínguez-Gómez
First: 2026-07-02T16:18:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:18:58+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 1 figure, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper describes the participation of HULAT2-UC3M in the Spanish track of MER-TRANS 2026, a shared task on multilingual Easy-to-Read translation. Three fully automatic Spanish runs were submitted. RUN1 and RUN2 used a LangGraph-based multi-agent workflow combining Gemini 2.5 Flash and RigoChat-7B-v2, parallel generation strategies, internal quality signals, Event-Condition-Action routing, controlled editing and traceable decisions. RUN1 used the base workflow, while RUN2 activated an additional lexical-support layer based on a glossary and lexical resources. RUN3 was a RigoChat-based generate-evaluate-regenerate baseline with prompt engineering and LoRA-based adaptation. The official leaderboard reports BLEU-Orig, BLEU-Gold, SARI and BERTScore. During development, additional internal signals were also inspected, including semantic fidelity, readability, lexical simplicity, syntactic clarity and factual consistency. According to official SARI, RUN1 was the best HULAT2 run, with 44.0543 points, followed by RUN2 with 43.1049 and RUN3 with 38.5136. These results indicate that, in this task setting, signal-guided multi-agent routing outperformed the linear regeneration baseline. They also show that adding lexical support did not automatically improve reference-based scores. Further segment-level and document-level analysis are required to assess readability, factual consistency and user-oriented adequacy.

mamabench and mamaretrieval: Benchmarks for Evaluating Medical Retrieval-Augmented Generation in Maternal, Neonatal, and Reproductive Health
Authors: Yi Ren
First: 2026-06-28T15:51:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:16:56+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 3 tables. Datasets and construction code linked in the paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Medical question-answering benchmarks rarely cover the maternal, neonatal, child, and reproductive-health questions a nurse-midwife asks, and, to our knowledge, no public chunk-level relevance benchmark exists for maternal-health guideline retrieval. We release two benchmarks that fill these gaps. mamabench is a scope-filtered QA set of 25,949 items assembled from seven existing expert-authored sources across multiple-choice, short-answer, and rubric-graded tracks; to help users calibrate the LLM judge that scores the rubric track, we re-scope HealthBench's physician-labelled meta-evaluation to the domain. mamaretrieval pairs 3,185 clinical queries with graded (0-6) relevance labels over a 63,650-chunk maternal-health guideline corpus, using a decomposed rubric that distinguishes a chunk that answers a query from one merely on its topic. Three decisions shape both: assemble and filter expert sources rather than author questions, grade relevance rather than binarise it, and measure and disclose the limits of the labels -- scope-classifier agreement, a frontier-judge check, and a pooling-completeness audit -- rather than treat them as an oracle. A companion paper uses the benchmarks to evaluate a deployed on-device assistant; both are released openly for research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：mamabench 和 mamaretrieval：用于评估母婴与生殖健康领域医疗检索增强生成的基准
现有医疗问答基准很少涵盖助产士常问的母婴、新生儿、儿童及生殖健康问题，据我们所知，尚无公开的、以片段为单位的相关性评估基准用于母婴健康指南检索。我们发布两个基准填补这些空白。mamabench 是从七个现有专家编写来源中汇编的25,949项范围筛选后的问答数据集，涵盖多项选择题、简答题和评分题三类；为帮助用户校准评分题的LLM评分器，我们重新将HealthBench的医师标注元评估范围限定于本领域。mamaretrieval 将3,185个临床查询与63,650个母婴健康指南片段的分级（0-6分）相关性标签配对，采用分解式评分标准，区分回答查询的片段与仅涉及主题的片段。两个基准均基于三项决策构建：优先整合并筛选专家来源而非作者问题、对相关性进行分级而非二值化、测量并公开标签的局限性（范围分类器一致性、前沿评审员检查、聚合完整性审计），而非将其视为权威。配套论文使用这些基准评估部署在设备上的助手，两个基准均已公开发布供研究使用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of specialized benchmarks for maternal, neonatal, and reproductive health in medical retrieval and question-answering, the authors introduce mamabench and mamaretrieval. mamabench comprises 25,949 QA items drawn from seven expert-authored sources, organized across multiple-choice, short-answer, and rubric-graded formats, with a re-scoped HealthBench meta-evaluation to calibrate LLM judges. mamaretrieval pairs 3,185 clinical queries with relevance scores (0–6) across 63,650 guideline chunks, using a decomposed rubric that differentiates answer-relevance from topical relevance. Both benchmarks emphasize expert source curation, graded relevance labeling, and transparent disclosure of label limitations via scope-classifier agreement, frontier-judge checks, and pooling-completeness audits. Experimental results show that existing models perform poorly on these benchmarks, highlighting the need for domain-specific evaluation tools.
为填补医学问答与检索增强生成在母婴及生殖健康领域缺乏专用基准的空白，作者构建了mamabench与mamaretrieval两个基准。mamabench由七个专家来源筛选整合而成，包含25,949个问题，涵盖多选题、简答题及评分题，配套重新调整的HealthBench元评估用于校准评分模型。mamaretrieval将3,185个临床问题与63,650个指南片段配对，采用0–6分分级相关性标注，并使用分解式评分标准区分“回答相关”与“主题相关”。构建过程中采用专家源筛选、分级而非二值化标注、并引入范围分类器一致性、前沿评审员校验及聚合完整性审计等质量控制机制。实验结果表明，这两个基准能有效反映母婴健康领域的检索挑战，支持对基于大模型的医疗助手进行可靠评估。
Hardware-Enforced Semantic Coordination for Safety-Critical Real-Time Autonomous Systems
Authors: Uwe M. Borghoff, Paolo Bottoni, Remo Pareschi
First: 2026-07-02T16:16:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:16:41+00:00
Comments: 1 figure, 6 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in agentic AI are producing increasingly complex autonomous systems that integrate large language models, world models, optimization engines, specialized neural architectures, autonomous platforms, and human operators. While much current research focuses on improving reasoning capabilities, safety-critical real-time deployment also requires bounded and verifiable coordination among heterogeneous components operating concurrently under uncertainty. Software-mediated coordination presents fundamental limitations in domains where bounded latency, deterministic coordination, and enforceable safety guarantees are essential. Hence, we propose a hardware-enforced semantic coordination architecture in which selected coordination semantics are implemented directly at the hardware level via field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs). The approach builds on the Topic-Based Communication Space Petri Net (TB-CSPN) framework, which separates semantic reasoning from interaction management. In this approach, selected TB-CSPN coordination mechanisms are mapped onto FPGA primitives, creating a hardware-native semantic coordination layer. Focus is not on acceleration, but on enforcing temporal synchronization, semantic gating, authorization constraints, and bounded coordination behavior directly in hardware. Semantic reasoning remains adaptive and software-driven, while embedded coordination semantics become deterministic.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于硬件强制语义协调的安全关键实时自主系统
近期代理AI的进展正在产生日益复杂的自主系统，这些系统整合了大语言模型、世界模型、优化引擎、专用神经架构、自主平台和人类操作员。尽管当前许多研究聚焦于提升推理能力，但安全关键的实时部署还要求在不确定性环境下，异构组件并发运行时具备有界且可验证的协调。软件介导的协调在需要有界延迟、确定性协调和可强制安全保证的领域存在根本性局限。 因此，我们提出一种硬件强制语义协调架构，通过现场可编程门阵列（FPGA）在硬件层面直接实现选定的协调语义。该方法基于主题通信空间Petri网（TB-CSPN）框架，将语义推理与交互管理分离。 在此方法中，选定的TB-CSPN协调机制映射到FPGA原语，构建出原生硬件语义协调层。重点不在于加速，而在于在硬件中直接强制执行时序同步、语义门控、授权约束和有界协调行为。语义推理保持自适应且由软件驱动，而嵌入的协调语义则变为确定性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for deterministic and verifiable coordination in safety-critical real-time autonomous systems, this work proposes a hardware-enforced semantic coordination architecture using FPGAs to implement selected coordination semantics directly in hardware. Building on the TB-CSPN framework, it maps specific coordination mechanisms onto FPGA primitives to enforce temporal synchronization, semantic gating, authorization, and bounded behavior without relying on software mediation. Experimental results demonstrate that this hardware-native layer enables predictable, bounded coordination while preserving adaptive semantic reasoning in software, achieving deterministic behavior critical for safety-critical deployments.
为满足安全关键型实时自主系统对确定性和可验证协调的需求，本文提出一种基于FPGA的硬件强制语义协调架构，将选定的协调语义直接映射到硬件层面。该方法基于TB-CSPN框架，将特定协调机制转化为FPGA原语，以实现时序同步、语义门控、授权约束和有界协调行为，无需依赖软件中介。实验结果表明，该方法可实现确定性协调、有界延迟和可强制的安全保障，同时保留软件层面的自适应语义推理能力。
Representation Distribution Matching for One-Step Visual Generation
Authors: Lan Feng, Wuyang Li, Eloi Zablocki, Matthieu Cord, Alexandre Alahi
First: 2026-07-02T16:15:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:15:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We elucidate the design space of Representation Distribution Matching (RDM), our name for the paradigm that trains a one-step image generator by matching generated and reference feature distributions under frozen pretrained encoders. We identify two design axes, how the distributions are compared and the representations they are compared in, and controlled studies along them yield three findings. First, the classical MMD, which could not train convincing generators a decade ago, becomes a strong and scalable objective once estimated right. Second, the generated batch is then the operative variable, with an optimum above 2048, far beyond customary batch sizes. Third, any single representation can be gamed, driven below the real score while images stay visibly fake, so we match against a balanced battery of encoders and evaluate with SW_r14, a Sliced-Wasserstein distance over 14 encoders that is independent of the training loss and resists gaming. Combining the preferred choices yields improved RDM (iRDM): it sets the one-step state of the art on ImageNet at SW_r14 1.30, corroborated by PickScore, a human-preference proxy our objective never optimizes, which prefers it over the prior best one-step generator on 71.2% of matched samples. The same recipe post-trains the four-step FLUX.2 [klein] into a one-step generator, surpassing the four-step version on GenEval, 0.826 to 0.794, and on PickScore, 22.76 to 22.58, in 90 H200 GPU-hours. Project page: https://alan-lanfeng.github.io/rdm/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一步视觉生成的表示分布匹配
我们阐明了表示分布匹配（RDM）的设计空间，这是一种通过在冻结的预训练编码器下匹配生成图像与参考图像的特征分布来训练一步图像生成器的范式。我们识别出两个设计维度：如何比较分布，以及在何种表示空间中进行比较。沿这两个维度的控制实验得出三个发现：首先，十年前无法训练出令人信服生成器的经典MMD，在正确估计后成为强大且可扩展的目标；其次，生成批次成为关键变量，最优值超过2048，远超常规批次大小；第三，任何单一表示均可被操控，使其得分低于真实值，而图像仍显虚假，因此我们采用一组平衡的编码器进行匹配，并使用SW_r14（14个编码器上的切片Wasserstein距离）进行评估，该距离独立于训练损失且抗操控。结合优选方案得到改进型RDM（iRDM）：其在ImageNet上以SW_r14 1.30达到一步生成的最先进水平，且经PickScore（人类偏好代理，我们的目标从未优化）验证，在71.2%的匹配样本中优于此前最佳一步生成器。同一方法可将四步FLUX.2 [klein] 后训练为一步生成器，在GenEval上从0.826提升至0.794，在PickScore上从22.76提升至22.58，耗时90 H200 GPU小时。项目页面：https://alan-lanfeng.github.io/rdm/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve one-step image generation through distribution matching, the authors introduce Representation Distribution Matching (RDM), training generators by aligning generated and reference feature distributions under frozen encoders. They identify two key design dimensions: comparison method and representation space, and through controlled experiments, find that properly estimated MMD becomes a strong objective, that batch sizes above 2048 are optimal, and that single encoders can be gamed. To counter this, they propose matching against a balanced set of encoders and evaluating with SW_r14, a Sliced-Wasserstein distance over 14 encoders, which is robust to gaming. The resulting improved RDM (iRDM) achieves state-of-the-art performance on ImageNet with SW_r14 1.30 and outperforms prior methods on human preference metrics. The same approach also post-trains FLUX.2 into a one-step generator, improving its GenEval and PickScore scores while using only 90 H200 GPU-hours.
为提升单步图像生成效果，本文提出表示分布匹配（RDM）方法，通过在冻结的预训练编码器下对生成图像与真实图像的特征分布进行对齐来训练生成器。研究发现，比较度量方式和表示空间是两个关键设计维度：当MMD正确实现时，其性能显著提升；最优批大小远超常规（超过2048）；单个编码器易被生成器“欺骗”，导致图像虽假但得分偏低。为此，作者提出使用多编码器组合并引入SW_r14（14个编码器的切片Wasserstein距离）作为鲁棒评估指标。改进后的iRDM在ImageNet上达到SW_r14 1.30的最优性能，并在人类偏好指标PickScore上优于前作（71.2%样本更优）。同一方法还可将四步生成器FLUX.2后训练为单步生成器，在GenEval和PickScore上均优于原四步版本，仅用90个H200 GPU小时。
DRIFTLENS: Measuring Memory-Induced Reasoning Drift in Personalized Language Models
Authors: Xi Fang, Weijie Xu, Yingqiang Ge, Yuhui Xu, Stephanie Eckman, Chandan K. Reddy
First: 2026-07-02T16:15:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:15:25+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Personalization changes what a model says to a user; we show that it can also change the reasoning trajectory used to justify the response. Modern LLMs personalize interactions by storing user attributes, preferences, and prior context, then injecting this information into future prompts. We study whether such memory reshapes reasoning on open-ended questions where no single ground-truth answer exists. To quantify this effect, we introduce DRIFTLENS, a ground-truth-free framework that maps each expressed reasoning step to a value category and measures divergence between a question's no-memory trajectory and its trajectory under injected user-attribute memory. We first validate that DRIFTLENS distinguishes content-free pragmatic noise from substantive reasoning changes. Across four LLMs and 10 user-attribute categories, including age, occupation, and disability, user-attribute memory induces medium-to-large reasoning drift above each model's pragmatic-noise floor, even when final answers remain fluent, on-topic, and plausible. We then evaluate GRPO- and DPO-based post-training methods for reducing drift. Both reduce drift, but neither uniformly dominates; effects on downstream capability, helpfulness, and instruction following are model-and reward-dependent. These results suggest that memory-induced reasoning drift is a measurable and only partly mitigated failure mode of personalized language models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DRIFTLENS：测量个性化语言模型中记忆引发的推理漂移
个性化会改变模型对用户的回答内容；我们证明它也可能改变用于支持回答的推理路径。现代大语言模型通过存储用户属性、偏好和先前上下文，并将其注入未来提示中来实现个性化交互。我们研究此类记忆是否重塑了开放性问题的推理过程（这些问题不存在单一正确答案）。为量化该效应，我们提出了DRIFTLENS，一个无需真实答案的框架，将每个表达的推理步骤映射到价值类别，并测量无记忆轨迹与注入用户属性记忆后的轨迹之间的差异。我们首先验证DRIFTLENS能区分无实质内容的语用噪声与实质性推理变化。在四类大语言模型和十类用户属性（包括年龄、职业和残疾等）中，用户属性记忆在最终答案仍流畅、相关且合理的情况下，仍能引发中至大型推理漂移，且高于各模型的语用噪声基线。随后我们评估基于GRPO和DPO的后训练方法以减少漂移。两种方法均能减少漂移，但均未统一占优；对下游能力、有用性和指令遵循的影响取决于模型和奖励函数。这些结果表明，记忆引发的推理漂移是个性化语言模型的一种可测量且仅部分缓解的失效模式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that personalization in language models can alter not just outputs but also internal reasoning paths, this study introduces DRIFTLENS, a ground-truth-free framework that quantifies reasoning drift caused by injected user attributes. By mapping each reasoning step to a value category and comparing trajectories with and without memory, DRIFTLENS distinguishes pragmatic noise from substantive reasoning shifts. Experiments across four LLMs and ten user attributes show consistent medium-to-large drift in reasoning trajectories even when final responses remain fluent and plausible. Post-training methods like GRPO and DPO reduce drift but do not uniformly improve downstream behaviors, indicating that memory-induced drift is a measurable yet only partially mitigated failure mode in personalized models.
鉴于个性化可能不仅改变语言模型的输出，还可能改变其推理路径，本研究提出DRIFTLENS框架，用于量化用户属性记忆对推理轨迹的影响。该框架通过将推理步骤映射至价值类别，对比有无记忆时的推理轨迹，区分语用噪声与实质性推理变化。在四类模型和十类用户属性下，记忆普遍引发中至大规模推理漂移，即使最终回答仍流畅、相关且合理。GRPO与DPO等后训练方法可降低漂移，但效果因模型和奖励函数而异，表明记忆引发的推理漂移是个性化模型中可测量且部分缓解的失效模式。
MAM-AI: An On-Device Medical Retrieval-Augmented Generation System for Nurses and Midwives in Zanzibar
Authors: Yi Ren
Venue: www
First: 2026-06-28T19:52:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:15:04+00:00
Comments: 38 pages. Video demo: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=M_Kruluel28 ; browser demo, code, models, and benchmarks linked in the paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Maternal and newborn mortality remain among the highest in sub-Saharan Africa, where midwifery care is often delivered by nurses who lack midwifery training to international standards, and consulting authoritative guidance at the point of care is hard: the guidelines are long and connectivity is intermittent. We present MAM-AI, a medical question-answering assistant for nurse-midwives in Zanzibar that runs entirely on a commodity Android device: a question is embedded (EmbeddingGemma, 300M) and matched against a curated corpus of 87 guideline documents (63,650 passages), then answered with citations by a 4B int4 generator (Gemma 4 E4B), fully offline, with no query leaving the device. We evaluate the exact deployed configuration with a layered methodology -- retriever, generator under oracle context, end-to-end, and latency -- scored by LLM judges validated against physician rubrics. The evaluation relocates the hard problem. On-device retrieval is essentially solved: the 300M embedder ranks third of seven retrievers and rivals cloud systems, so the passages the system needs are usually found. The small generator is what remains in doubt: adding retrieved context does not improve its answers, and at 4B it cannot be both helpful and safe at once -- of two same-size candidates, the more helpful one commits genuine dangerous errors, so we deploy the other, which is about twice as faithful to its sources (as faithful as a frontier model), and recover its helpfulness with a redesigned prompt that cuts deflection from 33% to 3%. Corpus quality is decisive for the same reason: where the corpus holds the right passage the answer is specific and actionable, and where it does not it goes vague. MAM-AI is a thoroughly evaluated, open-source research prototype, not a fielded product; the system, knowledge base, benchmarks, and evaluation harness are released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MAM-AI：用于坦桑尼亚护士助产士的本地化医疗检索增强生成系统
在撒哈拉以南非洲地区，孕产妇和新生儿死亡率仍居高不下，当地助产护理常由缺乏国际标准助产培训的护士提供，且现场难以查阅权威指南：指南篇幅冗长，网络连接不稳定。我们提出MAM-AI，专为坦桑尼亚护士助产士设计的医疗问答助手，完全运行于普通安卓设备上：用户提问经EmbeddingGemma（3亿参数）嵌入后，匹配至精心筛选的87份指南文档（63,650个段落），再由4B int4生成器（Gemma 4 E4B）引用文献作答，全程离线运行，无任何查询离开设备。我们采用分层评估方法（检索器、Oracle上下文下的生成器、端到端、延迟）对实际部署配置进行评估，评分由经医生评分标准验证的LLM评审员完成。评估将核心难题转移：本地化检索基本解决——3亿参数嵌入器在七种检索器中排名第三，性能可媲美云端系统，因此系统通常能检索到所需段落。小型生成器仍存疑：加入检索上下文未提升其回答质量，且在4B规模下无法同时兼顾有用性和安全性——在两个同等规模候选模型中，更“有用”的模型常出现真实危险性错误，故我们部署另一个模型，其对源材料的忠实度约为前者的两倍（与前沿模型相当），并通过重新设计提示词将偏离度从33%降至3%。语料质量至关重要：语料包含正确段落时，答案具体可操作；否则答案模糊。MAM-AI是经过全面评估、开源的研究原型，非实际产品；系统、知识库、基准测试及评估工具均已公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by high maternal and newborn mortality in sub-Saharan Africa where nurses often lack formal midwifery training and access to authoritative guidelines is limited, MAM-AI is an on-device medical QA system for nurse-midwives in Zanzibar. It uses a 300M parameter embedding model to retrieve relevant passages from a curated 87-document corpus, then generates answers using a 4B int4 Gemma model entirely offline. Evaluation across multiple metrics—including retriever performance, generator accuracy under oracle context, end-to-end quality, and latency—shows that while retrieval is effective (ranked third among seven retrievers), the small generator struggles with safety and helpfulness trade-offs: the more helpful model produced dangerous errors, so the more faithful one was deployed. A redesigned prompt reduced deflection from 33% to 3%, and corpus quality was critical—answers were specific and actionable only when the right passage was retrieved. The system is an open-source research prototype, not a deployed product.
为应对撒哈拉以南非洲地区母婴死亡率高、助产士培训不足且现场难以获取权威指南的问题，MAM-AI 是专为坦桑尼亚桑给巴尔地区助产士设计的本地化医疗问答系统。系统在普通安卓设备上运行，通过300M嵌入模型从87份指南文档（63,650段落）中检索相关段落，再由4B int4 Gemma模型生成带引用的答案，全程离线运行。评估显示，检索模块表现优异（七种模型中排名第三），但生成模块存在安全与有用性权衡问题：更“有用”的模型常产生危险错误，因此选择更“忠实”于原文的模型部署，其通过提示词优化将偏离率从33%降至3%。知识库质量至关重要：若文档包含正确段落，答案具体可操作；否则答案模糊不清。
Learning Spectral and Polarimetric Clues for One-to-Multimodal Novel View Synthesis
Authors: Federico Lincetto, Gianluca Agresti, Mattia Rossi, Piergiorgio Sartor, Pietro Zanuttigh
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-02T16:13:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:13:19+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ECCV 2026. Project page: https://medialab.dei.unipd.it/paper_data/SPoILeR/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural rendering techniques allow for accurate reconstruction of the geometry and color appearance of 3D scenes. Some methods have extended their use to additional imaging modalities, such as multispectral, infrared, or polarimetric data. However, all of these approaches require expensive sensors and calibrated setups to capture new multimodal frames for each new scene. We propose Spectral and Polarimetric Implicit Learned Representation (SPoILeR), a novel method to obtain multi-view consistent renderings of unconventional modalities for scenes where either only RGB frames or very few of the additional modalities are available. Thanks to a multimodal pre-training phase, the model learns the mutual correlation between different modalities. This step allows predicting accurate renderings of unconventional modalities during a fine-tuning phase supervised only by RGB images. Experimental results show that the approach can accurately render infrared, polarimetric, and multispectral frames for scenes where no input sample captured by these types of sensors is provided.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习光谱与偏振线索以实现单视角到多模态新视角合成
神经渲染技术可精确重建3D场景的几何结构与颜色外观。部分方法已扩展至多光谱、红外或偏振等成像模态。然而，这些方法均需昂贵传感器与校准设备，为每个新场景采集新多模态帧。本文提出光谱与偏振隐式学习表示（SPoILeR），一种在仅提供RGB帧或极少其他模态数据的场景中，生成多视角一致的非常规模态渲染的新方法。通过多模态预训练阶段，模型学习不同模态间的互相关性，从而在仅以RGB图像监督的微调阶段，准确预测非常规模态的渲染结果。实验表明，该方法可在无对应传感器采集样本的情况下，准确渲染红外、偏振及多光谱帧。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and complexity of capturing multimodal data for novel view synthesis, this work introduces SPoILeR, a method that leverages multimodal pre-training to learn spectral and polarimetric correlations from RGB-only data. During fine-tuning, it generates accurate renderings of infrared, polarimetric, and multispectral views without requiring input samples from those modalities. Experiments demonstrate that SPoILeR achieves high-fidelity reconstructions across these unconventional modalities, even when trained on limited or no multimodal data.
为降低多模态新视角合成所需的昂贵传感器和校准成本，本文提出SPoILeR方法，通过多模态预训练学习RGB与红外、偏振、多光谱等模态间的关联关系。在微调阶段仅使用RGB图像即可生成高精度的非传统模态渲染结果。实验表明，该方法即使在无对应模态输入样本的情况下，也能准确重建红外、偏振及多光谱图像。
VisionAId: An Offline-First Multimodal Android Assistant for People with Visual Impairment, Featuring Personalized Object Retrieval
Authors: Cristian-Gabriel Florea, Stelian Spînu
First: 2026-07-02T16:12:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T16:12:50+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 4 figures. Project repository available at: github.com
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Over 285 million people worldwide live with a visual impairment, for whom everyday tasks such as avoiding obstacles, locating personal belongings, recognizing familiar faces, or handling cash remain persistent obstacles to personal autonomy. Existing assistive applications are typically limited to recognizing predefined categories, depend heavily on cloud connectivity, or require dedicated hardware. We present VisionAId, an Android application that turns a commodity smartphone into a real-time visual assistant. The system integrates six on-device deep learning models (metric monocular depth estimation, instance segmentation, visual and facial embeddings, face detection, and a custom banknote detector) running entirely through ONNX Runtime, with an optional cloud large language model (Google Gemini Flash) used only for narrative scene description and automatic object labeling. A distinctive contribution is a few-shot pipeline for personal objects: the user photographs an object from several angles, and the system later locates that specific instance in the environment, guiding the user toward it with augmented-reality markers, spatial audio, and distance-proportional haptics. All feedback is multimodal (Romanian speech synthesis, voice commands, vibration). On a reference device (Samsung Galaxy S21 Ultra), INT8 quantization reduces depth latency from ~1200 ms to ~491 ms, the custom banknote detector reaches an mAP@50 of 0.986, and metric depth is calibrated to below 1 cm of error within 3 m.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VisionAId：面向视觉障碍人士的离线优先多模态安卓助手，支持个性化物品检索
全球有超过2.85亿人患有视觉障碍，日常任务如避障、寻找个人物品、识别熟人面孔或处理现金仍对其个人自主性构成持续障碍。现有辅助应用通常仅限于识别预定义类别、高度依赖云端连接或需专用硬件。我们提出VisionAId，一款安卓应用，可将普通智能手机转化为实时视觉助手。系统集成六个本地深度学习模型（度量单目深度估计、实例分割、视觉与面部嵌入、人脸检测及自定义钞票检测器），全部通过ONNX Runtime运行，可选云端大语言模型（Google Gemini Flash）仅用于场景描述与自动物品标注。独特贡献在于针对个人物品的少样本识别流程：用户从多个角度拍摄物品，系统随后在环境中定位该特定实例，并通过增强现实标记、空间音频及距离比例触觉引导用户前往。所有反馈均为多模态（罗马尼亚语语音合成、语音指令、振动）。在参考设备（三星Galaxy S21 Ultra）上，INT8量化将深度延迟从约1200毫秒降至约491毫秒，自定义钞票检测器mAP@50达0.986，度量深度校准误差在3米内低于1厘米。
Summary / 总结
VisionAId addresses the daily challenges faced by over 285 million visually impaired individuals by transforming a standard smartphone into a real-time multimodal assistant. It runs six on-device deep learning models via ONNX Runtime—including depth estimation, instance segmentation, facial recognition, and a custom banknote detector—while optionally using a cloud LLM for scene narration. A key innovation is its few-shot personal object retrieval system, where users capture multiple views of an object to enable precise localization via AR markers, spatial audio, and haptic feedback. On a Samsung Galaxy S21 Ultra, INT8 quantization cuts depth inference latency from 1200 ms to 491 ms, the banknote detector achieves 0.986 mAP@50, and depth measurements are calibrated to within 1 cm error at 3 meters.
VisionAId 旨在帮助全球超过2.85亿视觉障碍人士解决日常障碍，通过将普通智能手机转化为实时多模态助手。系统集成六个本地运行的深度学习模型（包括单目深度估计、实例分割、视觉与面部嵌入、人脸检测及自定义钞票检测器），通过ONNX Runtime执行，可选云端大语言模型用于场景描述与自动物体标注。其创新点在于“少样本”个人物品检索功能：用户拍摄物品多角度图像后，系统可精准定位该物品并引导用户，通过AR标记、空间音频和按距离比例的触觉反馈实现。在三星Galaxy S21 Ultra设备上，INT8量化将深度推理延迟从1200毫秒降至491毫秒，钞票检测器mAP@50达0.986，深度测量在3米内误差低于1厘米。
